  const trailRuins=[[11,21,1.6],[17,10,1.8],[48.6,16,1.65],[51,36,1.4],[82,40,1.4],[128,68,1.5],[117,117,1.5],[62.5,94.5,1.2],[151.5,4,1.5]];
  function trailVillage(x,y){return (x>=16&&x<=39&&y>=17&&y<=39)||(x>=24&&x<=28&&y>=39&&y<=46)||(x>=38&&x<=44&&y>=22&&y<=26);}
  function trailBuilt(x,y){return trailVillage(x,y)||!!C.landscapeBridge(x,y)||trailRuins.some(([a,b,r])=>Math.hypot(x-a,y-b)<r);}
  function trailId(ax,ay,bx,by){if(![ax,ay,bx,by].every(Number.isInteger)||Math.min(ax,bx,ay,by)<0||Math.max(ax,bx)>=trailCols||Math.max(ay,by)>=trailRows)return null;
    if(ay>by||(ay===by&&ax>bx))[ax,ay,bx,by]=[bx,by,ax,ay];const d=trailDirs.findIndex(([x,y])=>bx-ax===x&&by-ay===y);return d<0?null:(Math.max(ax,bx)<oldTrailCols?((ay*oldTrailCols+ax)*4+d):oldTrailMax+((ay*trailCols+ax)*4+d)).toString(36);}
  function trailDecode(id){if(typeof id!=='string'||!/^[0-9a-z]{1,5}$/.test(id))return null;const n=parseInt(id,36);if(n.toString(36)!==id||n<0||n>=oldTrailMax+trailCols*trailRows*4)return null;const old=n<oldTrailMax,code=old?n:n-oldTrailMax,width=old?oldTrailCols:trailCols,d=trailDirs[code%4],i=Math.floor(code/4),x=i%width,y=Math.floor(i/width),bx=x+d[0],by=y+d[1];if(trailId(x,y,bx,by)!==id)return null;return {id,x:x*TRAIL_GRID,y:y*TRAIL_GRID,bx:bx*TRAIL_GRID,by:by*TRAIL_GRID};}
  function trailWear(w){return w.development?.desirePaths||{};}
  function trailSegment(a,b,passable=()=>true){if(!a||!b||![a.x,a.y,b.x,b.y].every(Number.isFinite)||Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y)>32)return [];const ax=Math.round(a.x/TRAIL_GRID),ay=Math.round(a.y/TRAIL_GRID),bx=Math.round(b.x/TRAIL_GRID),by=Math.round(b.y/TRAIL_GRID),ids=[];let x=ax,y=ay;const n=Math.max(Math.abs(bx-ax),Math.abs(by-ay));if(!n)return [];
    for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){const nx=Math.round(ax+(bx-ax)*i/n),ny=Math.round(ay+(by-ay)*i/n),id=trailId(x,y,nx,ny),p=id&&trailDecode(id);x=nx;y=ny;if(!p)continue;const mx=(p.x+p.bx)/2,my=(p.y+p.by)/2;if(trailBuilt(mx/C.TILE,my/C.TILE))continue;if(![0,.25,.5,.75,1].every(t=>passable(p.x+(p.bx-p.x)*t,p.y+(p.by-p.y)*t)))continue;ids.push(id);}return ids;}
  function trailApply(w,batch){if(!batch||typeof batch!=='object')return;const c=ensurePathCare(w),s=w.development.desirePaths??={};for(const [id,n] of Object.entries(batch)){const p=trailDecode(id);if(!p||!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1||n>20||trailBuilt((p.x+p.bx)/2/C.TILE,(p.y+p.by)/2/C.TILE))fail('Invalid trail traversal.');s[id]=Math.min(20,(s[id]||0)+n);c.last[id]=pathDay(w);}}
  function validateTrail(w){if(!own(w.development,'desirePaths'))return;const s=w.development.desirePaths;if(!s||Object.getPrototypeOf(s)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(s).length>trailCols*trailRows*4)fail('Invalid trail registry.');for(const[id,n]of Object.entries(s)){const p=trailDecode(id);if(!p||!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1||n>20||trailBuilt((p.x+p.bx)/2/C.TILE,(p.y+p.by)/2/C.TILE))fail('Invalid trail state.');}}
  function trailStyle(level){const q=Math.max(1,Math.min(20,level));return {width:4+q,alpha:.25+q*.034,grass:Math.max(0,1-(q-1)/16),compaction:Math.max(0,(q-5)/15),gravel:Math.max(0,(q-9)/11),rut:Math.max(0,(q-13)/7),name:trailNames[q-1]};}

  // World-clock wear, voluntary engineering and route selection share canonical edge IDs.
  const pathDay=w=>Math.floor(now(w)/1440);
  const pathMaterials=[null,{stone:1},{brick:1}];
  const pathLabels=['Natural earth','Gravel bed','Dressed stone'];
  function pathCare(w){return w.development.pathCare||{last:{},roads:{},scanDay:pathDay(w),regrow:true};}
  function ensurePathCare(w){if(!w.development.pathCare){const day=pathDay(w);w.development.pathCare={last:Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(trailWear(w)).map(k=>[k,day])),roads:{},scanDay:day,regrow:true};}return w.development.pathCare;}
  function pathTouch(w,id){ensurePathCare(w).last[id]=pathDay(w);}
  function pathRegrow(w){if(!Object.keys(trailWear(w)).length&&!w.development.pathCare)return;const c=ensurePathCare(w),day=pathDay(w);if(day<=c.scanDay)return;const s=trailWear(w);if(c.regrow)for(const id of Object.keys(s)){if(c.roads[id])continue;const last=c.last[id]??c.scanDay,loss=d=>Math.max(0,Math.floor((d-last-4)/3));const n=loss(day)-loss(c.scanDay);if(n>0){s[id]=Math.max(0,s[id]-n);if(!s[id]){delete s[id];delete c.last[id];}}}c.scanDay=day;}
  function pathSetRegrow(w,enabled){if(typeof enabled!=='boolean')fail('Invalid regrowth choice.');const c=ensurePathCare(w);c.regrow=enabled;for(const id of Object.keys(trailWear(w)))c.last[id]=pathDay(w);c.scanDay=pathDay(w);}
  function pathNear(w,x,y,r=42){if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))return [];const out=[],cx=Math.round(x/16),cy=Math.round(y/16),n=Math.ceil(r/16)+1,wear=trailWear(w),care=pathCare(w);for(let a=cx-n;a<=cx+n;a++)for(let b=cy-n;b<=cy+n;b++)for(const[dx,dy]of trailDirs){const id=trailId(a,b,a+dx,b+dy);if(!id||!wear[id])continue;const p=trailDecode(id),dist=Math.hypot(x-(p.x+p.bx)/2,y-(p.y+p.by)/2);if(dist<=r)out.push({id,dist,level:wear[id],stage:care.roads[id]||0,...p});}return out.sort((a,b)=>a.dist-b.dist||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));}
  function pathEngineer(w,id,expected,x,y,passable){requireReady(w);const p=trailDecode(id),c=ensurePathCare(w),old=c.roads[id]||0;if(!p||cityReserved(w,id)||trailWear(w)[id]!==20||old!==expected||old>=2||typeof passable!=='function'||!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y)||Math.hypot(x-(p.x+p.bx)/2,y-(p.y+p.by)/2)>42||![0,.25,.5,.75,1].every(f=>passable(p.x+(p.bx-p.x)*f,p.y+(p.by-p.y)*f)))fail('Stand near a mature, unobstructed trail segment.');charge(w,pathMaterials[old+1]);c.roads[id]=old+1;pathTouch(w,id);}
  function validatePathCare(w){if(!own(w.development,'pathCare'))return;const c=w.development.pathCare,s=trailWear(w),day=pathDay(w);if(!c||typeof c.regrow!=='boolean'||!integer(c.scanDay)||c.scanDay>day||!c.last||Object.getPrototypeOf(c.last)!==Object.prototype||!c.roads||Object.getPrototypeOf(c.roads)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid trail care registry.');for(const[id,d]of Object.entries(c.last))if(!s[id]||!integer(d)||d>day)fail('Invalid trail usage date.');for(const[id,stage]of Object.entries(c.roads))if(s[id]!==20||![1,2].includes(stage))fail('Invalid engineered road.');}
  // O(1) local query (grid neighbourhood), shared by climate and ground effects.
  function pathSurfaceAt(w,x,y){if(!w.development.desirePaths)return null;const s=trailWear(w),roads=w.development.pathCare?.roads||{},cx=Math.round(x/16),cy=Math.round(y/16);let best=null;for(let a=cx-2;a<=cx+1;a++)for(let b=cy-2;b<=cy+1;b++)for(const[dx,dy]of trailDirs){const id=trailId(a,b,a+dx,b+dy),n=s[id];if(!n)continue;const p=trailDecode(id),vx=p.bx-p.x,vy=p.by-p.y,f=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((x-p.x)*vx+(y-p.y)*vy)/(vx*vx+vy*vy))),dist=Math.hypot(x-p.x-f*vx,y-p.y-f*vy),road=roads[id]||0,r=trailStyle(n).width*.52+road*2.4;if(dist>r)continue;const score=(road*25+n)*(1-dist/(r+1)*.5);if(!best||score>best.score)best={id,level:n,road,dist,score};}return best;}
  function pathRouteStats(w,path){let explored=0,engineered=0;for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){const a=path[i-1],b=path[i],id=trailId(...a,...b);if((trailWear(w)[id]||0)>=8)explored++;if(pathCare(w).roads[id])engineered++;}return {explored,engineered,total:Math.max(0,path.length-1)};}
  // Dijkstra over old collision-audited corridors and discovered cardinal edges. Old
  // corridors remain an invisible fallback for existing quests; new links require wear >=8.
  function planLivingCourier(area,start,end,w){const wear=trailWear(w);if(!Object.values(wear).some(n=>n>=8))return planCourier(area,start,end);const P=root.LQPrecisePhysics;if(!P||!area?.__lqPrecise||!courierValid(start)||!courierValid(end))return null;
    const width=C.W*2,target=courierKey(end),first=courierKey(start),care=pathCare(w),dist=new Map([[first,0]]),parents=new Map([[first,-1]]),heap=[],checked=new Map();
    function push(k,g){const a=[k,g];let i=heap.length;heap.push(a);while(i){const p=(i-1)>>1;if(heap[p][1]<=g)break;heap[i]=heap[p];i=p;}heap[i]=a;}
    function pop(){const a=heap[0],b=heap.pop();if(heap.length){let i=0;while(i*2+1<heap.length){let j=i*2+1;if(j+1<heap.length&&heap[j+1][1]<heap[j][1])j++;if(heap[j][1]>=b[1])break;heap[i]=heap[j];i=j;}heap[i]=b;}return a;}
    function pass(a,b,id,learned){if(checked.has(id))return checked.get(id);const x=courierPoint(a),y=courierPoint(b);let ok=P.segmentClear(area,x,y);for(let j=0;ok&&j<=8;j++){const f=j/8;ok=!P.playerBlocked(area,x.x+(y.x-x.x)*f,x.y+(y.y-x.y)*f)&&(learned||C.landscapeRoadDistance((x.x+(y.x-x.x)*f)/32,(x.y+(y.y-x.y)*f)/32)<1.32);}checked.set(id,ok);return ok;}
    push(first,0);let visits=0;while(heap.length&&visits<50000){const[k,g]=pop();if(g!==dist.get(k))continue;visits++;if(k===target){const out=[];for(let v=k;v!==-1;v=parents.get(v))out.push([v%width,Math.floor(v/width)]);return out.reverse();}const a=[k%width,Math.floor(k/width)];for(const[dx,dy]of [[1,0],[0,1],[-1,0],[0,-1]]){const b=[a[0]+dx,a[1]+dy];if(!courierValid(b))continue;const id=trailId(...a,...b),level=wear[id]||0,learned=level>=8,mx=(a[0]+b[0])/4,my=(a[1]+b[1])/4;if(!learned&&C.landscapeRoadDistance(mx,my)>=1.32)continue;const cost=care.roads[id]===2?.46:care.roads[id]===1?.60:learned?1.08-level*.021:trailBuilt(mx,my)?1:1.45,n=courierKey(b),ng=g+cost;if(ng>=(dist.get(n)??Infinity)||!pass(a,b,id,learned))continue;dist.set(n,ng);parents.set(n,k);push(n,ng);}}
    return planCourier(area,start,end);
  }
  function courierWear(w,j,before){if(j.cursor<=before)return;const batch={},wear=trailWear(w);for(let i=before+1;i<=j.cursor;i++){const id=trailId(...j.path[i-1],...j.path[i]);if(wear[id])batch[id]=1;}if(Object.keys(batch).length)trailApply(w,batch);}

  // Incremental registry on the existing transactional world state. A site cannot
  // generate any output without passing its local restoration/service conditions.
  const frontierById=Object.fromEntries(C.FRONTIER.map(p=>[p.id,p]));
  const frontierNames=['Abandoned','Surveyed','Cleared','Foundation','Support frame','Walls / stonework','Weatherproofing','Equipment','Operational','Specialized'];
  const frontierCosts=[{}, {}, {}, {stone:2},{wood:2},{brick:2},{plank:2},{fitting:1},{wood:1},{'research-scroll':1}];
  const frontierCost=(id,stage)=>{const p=frontierById[id];if(!p||stage<0||stage>9)fail('Unknown work order.');if(stage===5&&p.kind==='spring')return {stone:3};if(stage===6&&p.kind==='wind')return {iron:1};if(stage===7&&p.kind==='seed')return {'mint-seed':2};if(stage===7&&p.kind==='granary')return {cabinet:1};if(stage===9&&p.kind==='rest')return {plank:3};return {...frontierCosts[stage]};};
  const frontierEmpty=()=>({stage:0,seenAt:0,solved:false,paid:{},runs:0,lastDay:0,reading:null});
  const frontierState=(w,id)=>w.development.frontier?.sites?.[id]||frontierEmpty();
  const frontierStore=w=>w.development.frontier??={sites:{},bands:{},reward:false};
  function frontierVisit(w,id){if(!own(frontierById,id))fail('Unknown expedition station.');const f=frontierStore(w);f.sites[id]??={...frontierEmpty(),seenAt:now(w)};return f.sites[id];}
  function frontierBand(w,x,y){if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y)||x<0||y<0||x>=C.W||y>=C.H)return;const z=C.frontierAltitude(x,y),band=C.altitudeBand(z);if(band<0)return;const f=frontierStore(w);f.bands[band]??={at:now(w),z:Math.round(z),x,y};}
  function frontierSolve(w,id,answer){const p=frontierById[id];if(!p||answer!==p.correct)fail('Read the inscription and reconsider.');const s=frontierVisit(w,id);if(s.solved)fail('Already recorded.');s.solved=true;}
  function frontierRestore(w,id,expected){requireReady(w);const s=frontierState(w,id);if(!s.solved||s.stage!==expected||s.stage>=9)fail('Survey the inscription or check the work order.');const cost=frontierCost(id,expected+1);charge(w,cost);for(const [k,n] of Object.entries(cost))add(s.paid,k,n);s.stage++;if(s.stage===8)w.town.prosperity=Math.min(100,w.town.prosperity+1);return s.stage;}
  function frontierRecipe(w,id){const p=frontierById[id],s=frontierState(w,id);if(!p)return null;const bonus=s.stage===9;switch(p.kind){case 'seed':return {input:{herbs:2},output:{'mint-seed':bonus?3:2,'wheat-seed':1}};case 'stone':return {input:{stone:4},output:{brick:bonus?3:2}};case 'granary':return {input:{food:4},output:{copper:bonus?5:3}};case 'spring':return {input:{},output:{water:bonus?3:2}};default:return {input:{},output:{}};}}
  function frontierService(w,id,expected,advance){requireReady(w);const p=frontierById[id],s=frontierState(w,id);if(!p||s.stage<8||s.runs!==expected)fail('Restore the station and check the current service.');const day=w.clock.day,recipe=frontierRecipe(w,id);if(p.kind==='beacon'){const f=frontierStore(w);if(f.reward||!C.FRONTIER.every(q=>frontierState(w,q.id).stage>=8)||Object.keys(f.bands).length!==8||Object.keys(surveyState(w).visited).length!==2||summitState(w).stage<8)fail('Complete all ten landmarks and eight altitude bands first.');give(w,'research-scroll',s.stage===9?2:1);f.reward=true;}
    else if(p.kind==='rest'){if(typeof advance!=='function')fail('Rest requires the game clock.');advance(s.stage===9?60:30);}
    else if(p.kind==='wind'){if(s.lastDay===day)fail('This day’s reading is already recorded.');const future=now(w)+(s.stage===9?180:90);s.reading={at:now(w),nextAt:future,weather:root.LQValleySimulation.weatherAt({day:Math.floor(future/1440)+1,minuteOfDay:future%1440})};}
    else {if(p.kind==='spring'&&s.lastDay===day)fail('The cistern needs time to refill. Return tomorrow.');if(p.kind==='granary'&&s.runs>=3)fail('All three community contracts have been fulfilled.');charge(w,recipe.input);for(const [k,n]of Object.entries(recipe.output))give(w,k,n);if(p.kind==='granary')w.town.prosperity=Math.min(100,w.town.prosperity+2);}
    s.lastDay=w.clock.day;s.runs++;return p.role;}
  function validateFrontier(w){if(!own(w.development,'frontier'))return;const f=w.development.frontier;if(!f||Object.getPrototypeOf(f)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(f).sort().join(',')!=='bands,reward,sites'||!f.sites||Object.getPrototypeOf(f.sites)!==Object.prototype||!f.bands||Object.getPrototypeOf(f.bands)!==Object.prototype||typeof f.reward!=='boolean')fail('Invalid expedition registry.');for(const [id,s]of Object.entries(f.sites)){const p=frontierById[id];if(!p||!s||!integer(s.stage)||s.stage>9||!integer(s.seenAt)||s.seenAt>now(w)||typeof s.solved!=='boolean'||s.stage>0&&!s.solved||!validBag(s.paid)||!integer(s.runs)||!integer(s.lastDay)||s.lastDay>w.clock.day||s.stage<8&&s.runs||p.kind==='granary'&&s.runs>3||p.kind==='beacon'&&s.runs>1||!s.runs&&s.lastDay)fail('Invalid expedition station.');const cost={};for(let i=1;i<=s.stage;i++)for(const [k,n]of Object.entries(frontierCost(id,i)))add(cost,k,n);if(Object.keys(cost).length!==Object.keys(s.paid).length||Object.entries(cost).some(([k,n])=>s.paid[k]!==n))fail('Invalid station material ledger.');if(s.reading!==null){if(p.kind!=='wind'||!integer(s.reading.at)||s.reading.at>now(w)||!integer(s.reading.nextAt)||s.reading.nextAt<=s.reading.at||!['clear','mist','rain','cloudy','wind','storm','snow','heat'].includes(s.reading.weather))fail('Invalid weather record.');}}
    for(const [id,p] of Object.entries(f.bands)){if(!/^[0-7]$/.test(id)||!p||!integer(p.at)||p.at>now(w)||!Number.isFinite(p.x)||!Number.isFinite(p.y)||p.x<0||p.x>=C.W||p.y<0||p.y>=C.H||p.z!==Math.round(C.frontierAltitude(p.x,p.y))||C.altitudeBand(C.frontierAltitude(p.x,p.y))!==Number(id))fail('Invalid altitude observation.');}if(f.reward&&frontierState(w,'summit-beacon').runs!==1)fail('Invalid expedition reward.');}
  // An attended site has a deterministic schedule; attendance never creates free goods.
  function frontierDuty(w,id,minute){const p=frontierById[id];if(!p||frontierState(w,id).stage<8||minute<420||minute>=1080)return null;
    const q=((minute-420)+C.FRONTIER.indexOf(p)*12)%72,ax=p.ax-.65,ay=p.ay,rx=p.route[0],ry=p.route[1];let f,working=false,moving=false;if(q<12){f=q/12;moving=true;}else if(q<46){f=1;working=true;}else if(q<58){f=1-(q-46)/12;moving=true;}else{f=0;}return {x:rx+(ax-rx)*f,y:ry+(ay-ry)*f,dx:(ax-rx)*(q>=46?-1:1),dy:(ay-ry)*(q>=46?-1:1),moving,working};}

  const eastById=Object.fromEntries(C.EAST_SITES.map(p=>[p.id,p]));
  const eastStages=['Abandoned','Surveyed','Cleared','Foundation','Frame','Walls','Roof','Equipment','Operational','Specialized'];
  const eastEmpty=()=>({stage:0,solved:false,paid:{},seen:0,stock:{},job:null,runs:0,visits:0});
  const eastState=(w,id)=>w.development.east?.[id]||eastEmpty();
  function eastVisit(w,id){if(!own(eastById,id))fail('Unknown eastern site.');const all=w.development.east??={};all[id]??={...eastEmpty(),seen:now(w)};all[id].visits=Math.min(100000,all[id].visits+1);return all[id];}
  function eastSolve(w,id,answer){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||answer!==p.correct)fail('Read the description again.');const s=eastState(w,id);if(s.solved)fail('Already recorded.');eastVisit(w,id).solved=true;}
  function eastCost(id,stage){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||!integer(stage)||stage>9)fail('Unknown stage.');const table=[{},{},{},{stone:3},{wood:3},{brick:2},{plank:3},{fitting:1},{wood:1},{'research-scroll':1}];let c={...table[stage]};if(p.monument&&stage>=3){const t=[{},{},{},{stone:7,brick:2},{wood:6,plank:2},{brick:6,stone:3},{plank:5,fitting:2},{fitting:3,cabinet:1},{wood:3,'woven-cloth':2},{'research-scroll':2,'field-book':1}];return {...t[stage]};}if(stage===5&&['kiln','forge','lapidary','bakery'].includes(p.kind))c.brick=3;if(stage===7&&['nursery','glass','seedbank','dye'].includes(p.kind))c={'mint-seed':1,plank:1};if(p.civic&&stage===9)c={...c,[['repair-kit','travel-pack','woven-cloth','field-book','repair-kit','travel-pack','repair-kit','herbal-bundle','travel-pack','woven-cloth','field-book','survey-chart'][C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p)-18]]:1};return c;}
  function monumentReady(w,id){const p=eastById[id];return !p?.monument||!p.project||publicState(w,p.project).stage===3;}
  function eastRestore(w,id,expected){requireReady(w);if(constructionState(w,id))fail("Already under construction.");const s=eastState(w,id);if(!s.solved||s.stage!==expected||s.stage>=9||s.job)fail('Survey before rebuilding or finish the order.');if(expected>=2&&!monumentReady(w,id))fail('Finish the three partner public works phases before the foundation.');const c=eastCost(id,expected+1);charge(w,c);for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(c))add(s.paid,k,n);s.stage++;if(s.stage===8)w.town.prosperity=Math.min(100,w.town.prosperity+1);}
  const eastLimit=48;
  const eastKeys=id=>{const p=eastById[id];return p?[...new Set([...Object.keys(p.input),...Object.keys(p.output)])]:[];};
  function eastTransfer(w,id,key,n,put){const s=eastState(w,id);if(s.stage<8||!eastKeys(id).includes(key)||!integer(n)||n<1||n>eastLimit)fail('Invalid storage transfer.');if(put){if(sum(s.stock)+n>eastLimit)fail('Local store is full.');charge(w,{[key]:n});add(s.stock,key,n);}else{if(count(s.stock,key)<n)fail('Not enough local stock.');give(w,key,n);add(s.stock,key,-n);if(!s.stock[key])delete s.stock[key];}}
  function eastOrder(w,id,expected,at=now(w)){requireReady(w);if(branchJob(w,id))fail("Finish and collect the specialist commission first.");const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||s.stage<8||s.job||s.runs!==expected||!enough(s.stock,p.input))fail('Restore, stock the workshop and check the current order.');for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(p.input)){add(s.stock,k,-n);if(!s.stock[k])delete s.stock[k];}s.job={progress:0,started:at,expected,phase:'working'};charterPrepareOrder(w,id,s,at);const mastery=masteryFactor(w,id);if(mastery<100)s.job.masteryFactor=mastery;const study=studyRate(w,id,at);if(study<100)s.job.studyFactor=study;const publicRate=publicFactor(w,id,at);if(publicRate<100)s.job.publicFactor=publicRate;if(s.maintenance){s.job.tuned=true;delete s.maintenance;}}
  function eastCancel(w,id,expected){const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||!s.job||s.job.expected!==expected||s.job.progress!==0)fail('Work has already begun.');if(sum(s.stock)+sum(p.input)>eastLimit)fail('Clear local storage first.');for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(p.input))add(s.stock,k,n);if(s.job.tuned)s.maintenance=true;if(s.job.masteryTrial){const rec=w.development.mastery[id];rec.phase=0;rec.baseline=s.runs;rec.at=now(w);}s.job=null;}
  const eastDuration=(p,s)=>Math.ceil(p.minutes*(s.specialization==='production'?.85:1)*(s.stage===9?.75:1)*(s.job?.tuned ? .85 : 1)*((s.job?.charterFactor??100)/100)*((s.job?.publicFactor??100)/100)*((s.job?.studyFactor??100)/100)*((s.job?.masteryFactor??100)/100));
  // Finite opt-in standing orders. Runs consume the same stock as manual jobs.
  function civicPolicy(w,id){return w.development.civic?.orders?.[id]||0;}
  function civicSetPolicy(w,id,n){requireReady(w);if(!eastById[id]||eastState(w,id).stage<8||!integer(n)||n>8)fail('Restore the hall and choose up to eight additional orders.');const r=w.development.civic??={orders:{},contracts:{},next:1};r.orders[id]=n;}
  function civicReserve(w,id,at){if(branchJob(w,id))return false;const s=eastState(w,id),p=eastById[id],n=civicPolicy(w,id);if(!n||s.job||s.stage<8||!enough(s.stock,p.input)||sum(s.stock)-sum(p.input)+sum(p.output)>eastLimit)return false;eastOrder(w,id,s.runs,at);s.job.started=at;w.development.civic.orders[id]--;return true;}
  function civicContractState(w){return w.development.civic||{orders:{},contracts:{},next:1};}
  function civicSetContract(w,from,to,key,n){requireReady(w);const a=eastState(w,from),b=eastState(w,to);if(!eastById[from]||!eastById[to]||from===to||a.stage<8||b.stage<8||!own(eastById[from].output,key)||!own(eastById[to].input,key)||!integer(n)||n<1||n>5)fail('Choose a compatible producing and consuming workshop.');const r=w.development.civic??={orders:{},contracts:{},next:1};if(Object.keys(r.contracts).length>=24)fail('Maximum 24 standing supply agreements.');if(Object.values(r.contracts).some(c=>c.from===from&&c.to===to&&c.key===key))fail('Agreement already exists.');const id=r.next++;r.contracts[id]={from,to,key,n,left:charterHas(w,'logistics')?8:4,retry:0};return id;}
  function civicRemoveContract(w,id,from){const r=w.development.civic,c=r?.contracts?.[id];if(!c||c.from!==from)fail('No such agreement at this workshop.');delete r.contracts[id];}
  function civicDispatch(w,area){const r=w.development.civic;if(!r||districtCargo(w).job||!area?.__lqPrecise)return false;const t=now(w);let changed=false;for(const [id,c] of Object.entries(r.contracts)){if(!c.left||c.retry>t)continue;let a=eastState(w,c.from),b=eastState(w,c.to),required=eastById[c.to].input[c.key],n=Math.min(c.n,count(a.stock,c.key),Math.max(0,required*2-count(b.stock,c.key)),eastLimit-sum(b.stock));if(n<1)continue;c.retry=t+60;changed=true;try{districtSend(w,area,c.from,c.to,c.key,n);}catch(e){continue;}c.left--;return true;}return changed;}
  function civicCanDispatch(w){const r=w.development.civic;if(!r||districtCargo(w).job)return false;const t=now(w);return Object.values(r.contracts).some(c=>c.left>0&&c.retry<=t&&count(eastState(w,c.from).stock,c.key)>0&&count(eastState(w,c.to).stock,c.key)<eastById[c.to].input[c.key]*2&&sum(eastState(w,c.to).stock)<eastLimit);}
  function validateCivic(w){const r=w.development.civic;if(!r)return;if(!r.orders||Object.getPrototypeOf(r.orders)!==Object.prototype||!r.contracts||Object.getPrototypeOf(r.contracts)!==Object.prototype||!integer(r.next)||r.next<1||Object.keys(r.contracts).length>24)fail('Invalid civic registry.');for(const [id,n]of Object.entries(r.orders))if(!eastById[id]||eastState(w,id).stage<8||!integer(n)||n>8)fail('Invalid standing order.');for(const [id,c]of Object.entries(r.contracts))if(!/^\d+$/.test(id)||+id<1||+id>=r.next||!c||!eastById[c.from]||!eastById[c.to]||c.from===c.to||eastState(w,c.from).stage<8||eastState(w,c.to).stage<8||!own(eastById[c.from].output,c.key)||!own(eastById[c.to].input,c.key)||!integer(c.n)||c.n<1||c.n>5||!integer(c.left)||c.left>(charterHas(w,'logistics')?8:4)||!integer(c.retry)||c.retry>now(w)+60)fail('Invalid standing supply agreement.');}

  const civicFlags=['#a9b7a0','#cda5b5','#d7b77c'];
  const civicLessons=[['Which sentence describes a completed survey?',['We surveyed the valley yesterday.','We survey yesterday.','We surveying the valley.'],0],['Choose the polite request.',['Bring me those charts now.','Could you bring me those charts, please?','You brought those charts?'],1],['Choose the conditional sentence.',['If we restore the pump, water will flow.','If we restored, water flow yesterday.','We will if pump restoring.'],0],['Choose the comparison.',['The mesa is more high than the ridge.','The mesa is highest than the ridge.','The mesa is higher than the ridge.'],2]];
  const civicFacility=(w,id)=>w.development.civic?.facilities?.[id]||{};
  function civicFacilityWrite(w,id){const r=w.development.civic??={orders:{},contracts:{},next:1};r.facilities??={};return r.facilities[id]??={};}
  const civicPumpOpen=(w,id)=>id!=='fen-aqueduct'||civicFacility(w,id).gate!==false;
  function civicMaintenance(w,id){requireReady(w);const s=eastState(w,id);if(s.stage<8||s.maintenance||s.job)fail('Finish the current order before servicing the machine.');charge(w,{'repair-kit':1});s.maintenance=true;}
  function civicService(w,id,action,a){requireReady(w);const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||!p.civic||s.stage<8)fail('Restore and visit this civic facility.');const f=civicFacilityWrite(w,id),day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440);
    if(action==='gate'&&id==='fen-aqueduct'){f.gate=f.gate===false;return;}
    if(action==='banner'&&['heath-weaver','prairie-clothier'].includes(id)){if(!integer(a)||a>=civicFlags.length||f.banner===a)fail('Choose a different woven banner.');charge(w,{'woven-cloth':1});f.banner=a;return;}
    if(action==='meal'&&id==='prairie-kitchen'){if(f.mealDay===day||count(s.stock,'food')<3)fail('Need three portions and a new day.');const guests=C.EAST_SITES.filter(q=>q.region===p.region&&q.id!==id&&eastState(w,q.id).stage>=8);if(guests.length<2)fail('Restore at least two other Prairie workshops.');add(s.stock,'food',-3);if(!s.stock.food)delete s.stock.food;f.mealDay=day;f.meals=(f.meals||0)+1;f.guests=guests.map(q=>q.id);for(const q of guests){const v=eastState(w,q.id),m=v.memory??={talks:0,lastDay:-1,trust:0,help:0};m.trust=Math.min(100,m.trust+2);}return;}
    if(action==='survey'&&id==='heath-maproom'){const n=civicTrailSurvey(w);if(!n.total||f.survey&&f.survey.total===n.total&&f.survey.mature===n.mature)fail('Explore more eastern trails before recording another survey.');f.survey={...n,day};return;}
    if(action==='lesson'&&id==='moon-academy'){const n=f.lessons||0;if(n>=civicLessons.length||a!==civicLessons[n][2])fail('Review the sentence and try again.');if(n===civicLessons.length-1){charge(w,{'field-book':1});give(w,'research-scroll',1);}f.lessons=n+1;return;}
    if(action==='archive'&&id==='moon-bindery'){if(f.archiveDay===day||count(s.stock,'field-book')<1)fail('Bring a bound field book; one accession per day.');add(s.stock,'field-book',-1);if(!s.stock['field-book'])delete s.stock['field-book'];f.archiveDay=day;f.archived=(f.archived||0)+1;const m=s.memory??={talks:0,lastDay:-1,trust:0,help:0};m.trust=Math.min(100,m.trust+(charterHas(w,'heritage')?3:2));return;}
    fail('This civic action belongs to another station.');
  }
  function civicTrailSurvey(w){const r={total:0,mature:0,regions:{}};for(const [id,n]of Object.entries(trailWear(w))){const p=trailDecode(id);if(!p||p.x/32<175||n<1)continue;const region=C.eastBiome(p.x/32,p.y/32);r.total++;if(n>=8)r.mature++;r.regions[region]=(r.regions[region]||0)+1;}return r;}
  function validateCivicServices(w){const all=w.development.civic?.facilities;if(!all)return;if(Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid facility log.');const day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440);for(const[id,f]of Object.entries(all)){if(!eastById[id]?.civic||eastState(w,id).stage<8||!f||Object.getPrototypeOf(f)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid civic facility.');for(const key of Object.keys(f)){if(!({gate:1,banner:1,mealDay:1,meals:1,guests:1,survey:1,lessons:1,archiveDay:1,archived:1})[key])fail('Unknown civic state.');}if(own(f,'gate')&&(id!=='fen-aqueduct'||typeof f.gate!=='boolean'))fail('Invalid pump state.');if(own(f,'banner')&&(!['heath-weaver','prairie-clothier'].includes(id)||!integer(f.banner)||f.banner>=civicFlags.length))fail('Invalid banner.');if(own(f,'mealDay')&&(id!=='prairie-kitchen'||!integer(f.mealDay)||f.mealDay>day||!integer(f.meals)||f.meals<1||!Array.isArray(f.guests)||f.guests.length<2||new Set(f.guests).size!==f.guests.length||f.guests.some(q=>q===id||eastById[q]?.region!=='goldgrass'||eastState(w,q).stage<8)))fail('Invalid meal log.');if(own(f,'survey')){const z=f.survey;if(id!=='heath-maproom'||!z||!integer(z.total)||!integer(z.mature)||z.mature>z.total||!integer(z.day)||z.day>day||!z.regions||Object.entries(z.regions).some(([k,n])=>!C.EAST_BY_REGION[k]||!integer(n))||sum(z.regions)!==z.total)fail('Invalid cartography log.');}if(own(f,'lessons')&&(id!=='moon-academy'||!integer(f.lessons)||f.lessons>civicLessons.length))fail('Invalid lesson progress.');if(own(f,'archiveDay')&&(id!=='moon-bindery'||!integer(f.archiveDay)||f.archiveDay>day||!integer(f.archived)||f.archived<1))fail('Invalid archive record.');}}

  function eastAdvance(w,from,to){for(const [id,s]of Object.entries(w.development.east||{})){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||!s.job&&!civicPolicy(w,id))continue;for(let t=from;t<to;t++){const m=t%1440;if(!s.job){if(m<420||m>=1080||!civicPumpOpen(w,id)||!civicReserve(w,id,t))continue;}const j=s.job;if(t<j.started)continue;if(j.phase==='working'&&civicPumpOpen(w,id)&&utilityStatus(w,id,t).ok&&m>=420&&m<1080&&(m-420)%60>=10&&(m-420)%60<46){j.progress=Math.min(eastDuration(p,s),j.progress+1);if(j.progress>=eastDuration(p,s))j.phase='waiting';}if(j.phase==='waiting'&&sum(s.stock)+sum(p.output)<=eastLimit){for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(p.output))add(s.stock,k,n);s.runs++;if(j.masteryTrial){const rec=w.development.mastery[id];rec.phase=3;rec.at=t+1;}s.job=null;charterCompleteOrder(w,id,t+1);w.town.prosperity=Math.min(100,w.town.prosperity+(s.specialization==='community'?2:1));if(!civicPolicy(w,id))break;}}}}
  function eastDuty(w,id,minute){const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||s.stage<8||minute<420||minute>=1080)return null;const q=(minute-420)%60;let t=0,moving=false,working=false;if(q<10){t=q/10;moving=true;}else if(q<46){t=1;working=!!s.job&&s.job.phase==='working'&&civicPumpOpen(w,id)&&utilityStatus(w,id).ok;}else if(q<56){t=1-(q-46)/10;moving=true;}return {x:p.home[0]+(p.work[0]-p.home[0])*t,y:p.home[1]+(p.work[1]-p.home[1])*t,moving,working,dx:q<46?1:-1};}
  function validateEast(w){const all=w.development.east;if(!all)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid eastern registry.');for(const[id,s]of Object.entries(all)){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||!s||!integer(s.stage)||s.stage>9||typeof s.solved!=='boolean'||s.stage>0&&!s.solved||!integer(s.seen)||s.seen>now(w)||!integer(s.visits)||s.visits>100000||!integer(s.runs)||!validBag(s.paid)||!validBag(s.stock)||sum(s.stock)>eastLimit||Object.keys(s.stock).some(k=>!eastKeys(id).includes(k))||s.stage<8&&(s.runs||s.job||sum(s.stock)))fail('Invalid eastern workshop.');if(s.stage>=3&&!monumentReady(w,id))fail('Monument foundation lacks completed partner works.');const ledger={};for(let i=1;i<=s.stage;i++)for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(eastCost(id,i)))add(ledger,k,n);if(Object.keys(ledger).length!==Object.keys(s.paid).length||Object.entries(ledger).some(([k,n])=>s.paid[k]!==n))fail('Invalid eastern ledger.');if(own(s,'specialization')&&(s.stage!==9||!own(specializationDefs,s.specialization)))fail('Invalid specialization.');if(own(s,'maintenance')&&(typeof s.maintenance!=='boolean'||s.stage<8||s.job))fail('Invalid maintenance state.');if(s.job){const j=s.job;if(own(j,'charterFactor')&&(!charterState(w)?.houses[id]||![...Array.from({length:11},(_,n)=>100+n*3),...Array.from({length:11},(_,n)=>90+n*3)].includes(j.charterFactor)))fail('Invalid charter work rate.');if(own(j,'studyFactor')&&(![68,76,84,92].includes(j.studyFactor)||j.studyFactor<studyRate(w,id,j.started)))fail('Invalid advanced research work rate.');if(own(j,'publicFactor')&&(j.publicFactor!==90||publicFactor(w,id,j.started)!==90))fail('Invalid public works rate.');if(own(j,'masteryFactor')&&(![88,92,96].includes(j.masteryFactor)||j.masteryFactor<masteryFactor(w,id)))fail('Invalid mastery rate.');if(own(j,'masteryTrial')&&(j.masteryTrial!==true||w.development.mastery?.[id]?.phase!==2||w.development.mastery[id].baseline!==j.expected))fail('Invalid supervised order.');if(own(j,'tuned')&&typeof j.tuned!=='boolean')fail('Invalid maintained order.');if(!integer(j.progress)||j.progress>eastDuration(p,s)||j.expected!==s.runs||!integer(j.started)||j.started>now(w)||!['working','waiting'].includes(j.phase)||j.phase==='waiting'&&j.progress!==eastDuration(p,s))fail('Invalid eastern order.');}}}

  // V100: district planning backed by the existing surveyed road graph.
  const CITY_LIMIT=24,CITY_BATCH=8;
  const cityProjects=w=>w.development.cityProjects||{};
  const cityId=(a,b)=>[a,b].sort().join('~');
  function cityPair(a,b){const p=eastById[a],q=eastById[b];return !!p&&!!q&&a!==b&&p.region===q.region&&Math.hypot(p.ax-q.ax,p.ay-q.ay)<=48;}
  function cityEdges(path){if(!Array.isArray(path)||path.length<3||path.length>241)return null;const out=[],seen=new Set();for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){const a=path[i-1],b=path[i];if(!Array.isArray(a)||!Array.isArray(b)||a.length!==2||b.length!==2||!courierValid(a)||!courierValid(b))return null;const id=trailId(...a,...b),p=id&&trailDecode(id);if(!p||seen.has(id)||trailBuilt((p.x+p.bx)/2/C.TILE,(p.y+p.by)/2/C.TILE))return null;seen.add(id);out.push(id);}return out;}
  function cityOpen(w,a,b){return cityPair(a,b)&&eastState(w,a).stage>=8&&eastState(w,b).stage>=8&&!!charterState(w);}
  function cityCandidates(w,a){return C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>cityOpen(w,a,p.id)&&!cityProjects(w)[cityId(a,p.id)]).sort((p,q)=>Math.hypot(p.ax-eastById[a].ax,p.ay-eastById[a].ay)-Math.hypot(q.ax-eastById[a].ax,q.ay-eastById[a].ay));}
  function cityEdgeClear(area,id){const P=root.LQPrecisePhysics,p=trailDecode(id);if(!P||!area?.__lqPrecise||!p)return false;const a={x:p.x,y:p.y},b={x:p.bx,y:p.by};return P.segmentClear(area,a,b)&&[0,.125,.25,.375,.5,.625,.75,.875,1].every(f=>!P.playerBlocked(area,a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f,a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f));}
  function citySurvey(w,area,a,b){requireReady(w);const id=cityId(a,b);if(!cityOpen(w,a,b)||cityProjects(w)[id]||Object.keys(cityProjects(w)).length>=CITY_LIMIT)fail('Choose two operating halls in one district; maximum 24 surveys.');const path=districtRoute(area,a,b,w,true),edges=cityEdges(path);if(!edges||!edges.every(k=>(trailWear(w)[k]||0)>=8&&cityEdgeClear(area,k)))fail('Walk the entire continuous ground corridor to trail level 8 first.');if(edges.every(k=>pathCare(w).roads[k]===2))fail('This corridor is already paved.');charge(w,{'survey-chart':1});(w.development.cityProjects??={})[id]={from:a,to:b,path,surveyed:now(w),pending:null,batches:0};return id;}
  function cityReserved(w,k){return Object.values(cityProjects(w)).some(p=>p.pending?.edges.includes(k));}
  function cityProgress(w,id){const p=cityProjects(w)[id],edges=p&&cityEdges(p.path)||[],roads=pathCare(w).roads;return {total:edges.length,gravel:edges.filter(k=>roads[k]>=1).length,paved:edges.filter(k=>roads[k]===2).length,learned:edges.filter(k=>(trailWear(w)[k]||0)>=8).length};}
  function cityNext(w,id){const p=cityProjects(w)[id];if(!p||p.pending)return null;const edges=cityEdges(p.path);if(!edges)return null;const roads=pathCare(w).roads,stage=edges.some(k=>!roads[k])?1:edges.some(k=>roads[k]===1)?2:0;if(!stage)return null;const first=edges.findIndex(k=>(roads[k]||0)<stage),batch=[];for(let i=first;i<edges.length&&batch.length<CITY_BATCH;i++){const k=edges[i];if((roads[k]||0)>=stage)break;batch.push(k);}return {stage,edges:batch,cost:{[stage===1?'stone':'brick']:batch.length},minutes:20+12*batch.length,ready:stage===1||charterState(w)?.era>=3};}
  function cityBuild(w,area,id,expected){requireReady(w);const p=cityProjects(w)[id],n=cityNext(w,id);if(!p||!cityOpen(w,p.from,p.to)||!n||!n.ready||n.stage!==expected||!n.edges.every(k=>!cityReserved(w,k)&&(trailWear(w)[k]||0)>=8&&cityEdgeClear(area,k)))fail('Survey, maintain the learned route, resolve overlapping works and meet the era requirement.');charge(w,n.cost);p.pending={stage:n.stage,edges:n.edges,started:now(w),ends:now(w)+n.minutes};for(const k of n.edges)pathTouch(w,k);}
  function cityAdvance(w,at){for(const p of Object.values(cityProjects(w))){const j=p.pending;if(!j||j.ends>at)continue;const care=ensurePathCare(w),wear=w.development.desirePaths??={};for(const k of j.edges){wear[k]=20;care.roads[k]=Math.max(care.roads[k]||0,j.stage);care.last[k]=pathDay(w);}p.batches++;p.pending=null;}}
  function cityDiscard(w,id){if(civicPockets(w)[id])fail("A civic court is attached to this road.");const p=cityProjects(w)[id];if(!p||p.pending)fail('Wait until the paid work batch is complete.');delete w.development.cityProjects[id];}
  function citySummary(w,region){const sites=C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.region===region),active=sites.filter(p=>eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8),homes=C.HOMES.filter(h=>eastById[h.site]?.region===region),projects=Object.entries(cityProjects(w)).filter(([,p])=>eastById[p.from]?.region===region);return {sites:sites.length,active:active.length,homes:homes.length,housed:homes.filter(h=>homeState(w,h.id).stage===3).length,staff:active.filter(p=>charterState(w)?.houses[p.id]).length,shortages:active.filter(p=>!eastState(w,p.id).job&&Object.entries(p.input).some(([k,n])=>count(eastState(w,p.id).stock,k)<n)).length,projects:projects.length,pending:projects.filter(([,p])=>p.pending).length,complete:projects.filter(([id])=>{const v=cityProgress(w,id);return v.total>0&&v.paved===v.total;}).length};}
  function validateCity(w){const all=w.development.cityProjects;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>CITY_LIMIT)fail('Invalid district works registry.');const reserved=new Set();for(const [id,p] of Object.entries(all)){const edges=p&&cityEdges(p.path),a=p&&districtAnchor(p.from),b=p&&districtAnchor(p.to);if(!p||!cityPair(p.from,p.to)||id!==cityId(p.from,p.to)||!edges||!a||!b||p.path[0].join(',')!==a.join(',')||p.path.at(-1).join(',')!==b.join(',')||!integer(p.surveyed)||p.surveyed>now(w)||!integer(p.batches)||p.batches>edges.length*2||!(p.pending===null||p.pending&&typeof p.pending==='object'))fail('Invalid district works project.');const j=p.pending;if(j){if(![1,2].includes(j.stage)||!Array.isArray(j.edges)||!j.edges.length||j.edges.length>CITY_BATCH||new Set(j.edges).size!==j.edges.length||!j.edges.every(k=>edges.includes(k)&&!reserved.has(k))||!integer(j.started)||j.started<p.surveyed||j.started>now(w)||j.ends!==j.started+20+12*j.edges.length||j.ends<=now(w))fail('Invalid district construction batch.');for(let i=1;i<j.edges.length;i++)if(edges.indexOf(j.edges[i])!==edges.indexOf(j.edges[i-1])+1)fail('Noncontinuous district batch.');for(const k of j.edges)reserved.add(k);}}}

  // V101: six independent, walkable commons on collision-checked ground.
  const commons=Object.freeze([
    ['amberwood','birch-sawmill',184,19.05,'Birch Lantern Green','wood','Woodcraft exchange',0,960,'Which question politely asks for a craft demonstration?',['Could you show me how the wood is shaped?','Give me wood now.','I did not bring anything.']],
    ['heath','heath-dyeworks',233,18.05,'Heather Mosaic Court','mosaic','Colour and textile exhibition',1,900,'Which question invites an artist to explain a design?',['Where is my money?','What inspired your design?','Stop explaining.']],
    ['coppervale','copper-kiln',184,59.05,'Copperstone Forum','stone','Masons’ demonstration',2,960,'Which sentence describes a completed repair?',['We repair it tomorrow.','We are repairing it now.','We repaired the arch yesterday.']],

    ['moonbloom','moon-library',233,101.05,'Moonleaf Reading Garden','library','Open-air reading circle',2,900,'Which sentence invites a discussion?',['Nobody can speak.','This book is mine.','What did you think of the story?']]
  ].map(([id,site,x,y,name,style,event,correct,hour,question,answers],i)=>Object.freeze({id,site,x,y,name,style,event,correct,hour,question,answers,weekday:i})));
  const commonById=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(commons.map(p=>[p.id,p])));
  const commonCosts=Object.freeze([{stone:4,plank:2},{brick:3,water:2,wood:2},{fitting:1,plank:2,'woven-cloth':1}]);
  const commonMinutes=[90,150,120];
  const commonState=(w,id)=>w.development.commons?.[id]||{stage:0,pending:null,tended:-1,lastEvent:-1,events:0};
  function commonReady(w,id){const p=commonById[id];return !!p&&!!charterState(w)&&eastState(w,p.site).stage>=8;}
  function commonBuild(w,id){requireReady(w);const r=commonState(w,id);if(!commonReady(w,id)||r.stage>=3||r.pending||charterState(w).era<r.stage)fail('Restore the partner hall and meet the era requirement before commissioning the next phase.');charge(w,commonCosts[r.stage]);const s=(w.development.commons??={})[id]??=r;s.pending={started:now(w),ends:now(w)+commonMinutes[r.stage]};}
  function commonAdvance(w,at){for(const r of Object.values(w.development.commons||{}))if(r.pending&&r.pending.ends<=at){r.stage++;r.pending=null;}}
  function commonDay(w){return Math.floor(now(w)/1440);}
  function commonTend(w,id){requireReady(w);const r=commonState(w,id);if(!commonReady(w,id)||r.stage<2||r.tended===commonDay(w))fail('Build the garden and tend it at most once per day.');charge(w,{water:1});r.tended=commonDay(w);}
  function commonEventOpen(w,id){const p=commonById[id],r=commonState(w,id),m=w.clock.minuteOfDay;return !!p&&commonReady(w,id)&&r.stage===3&&r.tended===commonDay(w)&&commonDay(w)%7===p.weekday&&m>=p.hour&&m<p.hour+120&&r.lastEvent!==commonDay(w)&&!['storm','snow','heat'].includes(w.weather.kind);}
  function commonAttend(w,id,answer){requireReady(w);const p=commonById[id],r=commonState(w,id);if(!commonEventOpen(w,id)||answer!==p.correct)fail('Attend at the scheduled time, tend the garden, and choose the appropriate English response.');charge(w,{food:1,water:1});r.lastEvent=commonDay(w);r.events++;w.town.morale=Math.min(100,w.town.morale+2);charterLegacy(charterState(w),now(w),'commons',p.event);}
  function validateCommons(w){const a=w.development.commons;if(a===undefined)return;if(!a||Object.getPrototypeOf(a)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(a).length>6)fail('Invalid commons registry.');for(const [id,r]of Object.entries(a)){const p=commonById[id],day=commonDay(w);if(!p||!commonReady(w,id)||!r||!integer(r.stage)||r.stage>3||r.tended!==-1&&(!integer(r.tended)||r.tended>day)||r.stage<2&&r.tended!==-1||!Number.isSafeInteger(r.lastEvent)||r.lastEvent< -1||r.lastEvent>day||!integer(r.events)||r.events>Math.floor(day/7)+1||r.events===0!==(r.lastEvent===-1)||r.lastEvent>=0&&(r.stage!==3||r.lastEvent%7!==p.weekday||r.lastEvent>r.tended)||r.stage===0&&!r.pending||r.stage===3&&r.pending)fail('Invalid common state.');if(r.pending!==null){const j=r.pending;if(!j||!integer(j.started)||j.started>now(w)||j.ends!==j.started+commonMinutes[r.stage]||j.ends<=now(w))fail('Invalid commons construction.');}}}

  // V102: scheduled events use the same apprentice actors, never duplicate them.
  const gatheringRoutes=Object.freeze([{"id":"ap-01","common":"amberwood","path":[[5710,647.6],[5852,645.6]]},{"id":"ap-32","common":"amberwood","path":[[6098,807.6],[5888,720],[5888,657.6]]},{"id":"ap-19","common":"amberwood","path":[[5806,295.6],[5924,645.6]]},{"id":"ap-04","common":"heath","path":[[7442,615.6],[7420,613.6]]},{"id":"ap-21","common":"heath","path":[[7662,487.6],[7456,625.6]]},{"id":"ap-36","common":"heath","path":[[7282,391.6],[7488,512],[7492,613.6]]},{"id":"ap-07","common":"coppervale","path":[[5710,1927.6],[5852,1925.6]]},{"id":"ap-38","common":"coppervale","path":[[6130,2055.6],[5888,1952],[5888,1937.6]]},{"id":"ap-08","common":"coppervale","path":[[6386,2311.6],[5920,2176],[5924,1925.6]]},{"id":"ap-10","common":"glasswater","path":[[7410,1895.6],[7388,1893.6]]},{"id":"ap-25","common":"glasswater","path":[[7502,2311.6],[7424,2080],[7424,1905.6]]},{"id":"ap-11","common":"glasswater","path":[[7918,2279.6],[7440,2032],[7460,1893.6]]},{"id":"ap-13","common":"goldgrass","path":[[5710,3271.6],[5852,3269.6]]},{"id":"ap-27","common":"goldgrass","path":[[6094,3335.6],[5888,3281.6]]},{"id":"ap-14","common":"goldgrass","path":[[6450,3719.6],[5920,3504],[5924,3269.6]]},{"id":"ap-16","common":"moonbloom","path":[[7442,3271.6],[7420,3269.6]]},{"id":"ap-29","common":"moonbloom","path":[[7086,3719.6],[7296,3632],[7296,3472],[7456,3281.6]]},{"id":"ap-17","common":"moonbloom","path":[[7886,3719.6],[7488,3296],[7492,3269.6]]}].map(r=>Object.freeze({...r,path:Object.freeze(r.path.map(p=>Object.freeze(p)))})));
  const gatheringByApprentice=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(gatheringRoutes.map(r=>[r.id,r])));
  const gatheringQuestions=Object.freeze({
    amberwood:[['Ask how the grain affects the work.','How does the wood grain affect your work?','Give me the finished table.'],['Ask to borrow a tool politely.','Could I borrow that measuring tool?','You must lend me everything.'],['Suggest reusing spare wood.','We could use the offcuts for small objects.','Throw every piece away.']],
    heath:[['Ask about the colour choice.','Why did you choose these colours?','Your colours are wrong.'],['Invite someone to explain a pattern.','Could you tell me how you made this pattern?','Stop talking about fabric.'],['Offer constructive feedback.','The border might look clearer with a lighter thread.','This is worthless.']],

    glasswater:[['Suggest conserving water.','We could collect rainwater for the plants.','Leave the tap running all day.'],['Ask about the reeds.','How do reeds help the wetland?','Cut all the reeds down.'],['Compare two irrigation ideas.','This method uses less water than the other one.','All water is exactly the same.']],

  function gatheringHours(w,id,m=w.clock.minuteOfDay){const p=commonById[id],r=commonState(w,id);if(!p||!commonReady(w,id)||r.stage!==3||r.tended!==commonDay(w)||commonDay(w)%7!==p.weekday||['storm','snow','heat'].includes(w.weather.kind))return null;const h=p.hour;if(m<h-20||m>=h+80)return null;return m<h?{phase:'Walking to gathering',t:(m-h+20)/20,moving:true}:m<h+55?{phase:'At the gathering',t:1,moving:false}:{phase:'Returning to workshop',t:1-(m-h-55)/25,moving:true};}
  function gatheringPoint(path,t){let lens=path.slice(1).map((b,i)=>Math.hypot(b[0]-path[i][0],b[1]-path[i][1])),dist=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t))*lens.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let i=0;i<lens.length;i++){if(dist<=lens[i]||i===lens.length-1){let a=path[i],b=path[i+1],u=lens[i]?Math.min(1,dist/lens[i]):0;return{x:a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,y:a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u};}dist-=lens[i];}return{x:path[0][0],y:path[0][1]};}
  function gatheringDuty(w,id,m=w.clock.minuteOfDay){const r=gatheringByApprentice[id];if(crewRegistry(w)[id]||teamMember(w,id)||!r||!apprenticeActive(w,id))return null;const d=gatheringHours(w,r.common,m);return d?{...d,...gatheringPoint(r.path,d.t),common:r.common}:null;}
  function gatheringQuestion(id){const r=gatheringByApprentice[id];if(!r)return null;const i=gatheringRoutes.filter(a=>a.common===r.common).findIndex(a=>a.id===id),q=gatheringQuestions[r.common][i],correct=(i+commons.findIndex(p=>p.id===r.common))%2;return {prompt:q[0],answers:correct?[q[2],q[1]]:[q[1],q[2]],correct};}
  function gatheringRecord(w,id){return w.development.gatheringTalks?.[id]||{lastDay:-1,count:0};}
  function gatheringTalk(w,id,answer){requireReady(w);const d=gatheringDuty(w,id),q=gatheringQuestion(id),day=commonDay(w),r=gatheringRecord(w,id);if(d?.phase!=='At the gathering'||!q||answer!==q.correct||r.lastDay===day)fail('Meet the apprentice during the gathering and choose an appropriate response.');(w.development.gatheringTalks??={})[id]={lastDay:day,count:r.count+1};}
  function validateGathering(w){const all=w.development.gatheringTalks;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>gatheringRoutes.length)fail('Invalid gathering journal.');for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(all)){const route=gatheringByApprentice[id];if(!route||!apprenticeActive(w,id)||commonState(w,route.common).stage!==3||!r||!integer(r.lastDay)||r.lastDay>commonDay(w)||r.lastDay%7!==commonById[route.common].weekday||!integer(r.count)||r.count<1||r.count>Math.floor(r.lastDay/7)+1)fail('Invalid gathering conversation.');}}

  // V102.1: additive saved reservations, one per site, no stage advance until due.
  const constructionMinutes=(id,target)=>{const base=[0,20,45,120,150,180,150,90,60][target];return Number.isFinite(base)?Math.ceil(base*(eastById[id]?.monument?1.6:1)):0;};
  const constructionState=(w,id)=>w.development.construction102?.[id]||null;
  const constructionProgress=(w,id)=>{const r=constructionState(w,id);if(!r)return 0;const from=r.team?Math.max(...teamAt(w,id).members.map(m=>m.arrival)):r.started;return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(now(w)-from)/(r.ends-from)));};
  function constructionStart(w,id,expected){requireReady(w);const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||!s.solved||s.stage!==expected||expected>=8||s.job||constructionState(w,id))fail('Check the survey and existing construction.');if(expected>=2&&!monumentReady(w,id))fail('Complete partner works first.');const cost=eastCost(id,expected+1),started=now(w);charge(w,cost);(w.development.construction102??={})[id]={target:expected+1,started,ends:started+constructionMinutes(id,expected+1),cost};}
  function constructionCancel(w,id){requireReady(w);const r=constructionState(w,id);if(!r||r.crew||r.team||now(w)!==r.started)fail('Cancellation is available before time advances and before assigning a crew.');const clone=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w));for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(r.cost))give(clone,k,n);for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(r.cost))give(w,k,n);delete w.development.construction102[id];}
  function constructionAdvance(w,t){for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(w.development.construction102||{})){if(r.ends>t)continue;const s=eastState(w,id);if(s.stage!==r.target-1)fail('Construction stage mismatch.');for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(r.cost))add(s.paid,k,n);s.stage=r.target;if(s.stage===8)w.town.prosperity=Math.min(100,w.town.prosperity+1);delete w.development.construction102[id];}}
  function validateConstruction(w){const all=w.development.construction102;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>62)fail('Invalid construction registry.');for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(all)){const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p||!r||!s.solved||!integer(r.target)||r.target!==s.stage+1||r.target>8||s.job||!integer(r.started)||r.started>now(w)||!integer(r.ends)||r.ends!==(r.team?teamAt(w,id)?.finish:r.started+constructionMinutes(id,r.target)-(r.crew?crewBonus(id,r.target,crewTravel(crewRegistry(w)[r.crew]?.path||[])):0))||r.team&&(r.team!==true||r.crew||teamAt(w,id)?.target!==r.target||teamAt(w,id)?.started!==r.started)||r.crew&&crewRegistry(w)[r.crew]?.finish!==r.ends||r.ends<=now(w)||r.target>=3&&!monumentReady(w,id)||!validBag(r.cost))fail('Invalid construction reservation.');const c=eastCost(id,r.target);if(Object.keys(c).length!==Object.keys(r.cost).length||Object.entries(c).some(([k,n])=>r.cost[k]!==n))fail('Invalid reserved materials.');}}

  // V102.2: one reserved apprentice per site, real disclosed materials remain in construction102.
  const crewRegistry=w=>w.development.crew1022||{};
  const crewAtSite=(w,id)=>Object.entries(crewRegistry(w)).find(([,r])=>r.site===id&&now(w)<r.finish)||null;
  const crewTravel=path=>Math.max(5,Math.ceil(path.slice(1).reduce((n,b,i)=>n+Math.hypot(b[0]-path[i][0],b[1]-path[i][1]),0)/2));
  const crewBonus=(id,target,travel)=>Math.max(0,Math.floor((constructionMinutes(id,target)-travel)/5));
  const crewCandidates=(w,id)=>apprentices.filter(a=>a.site!==id&&eastById[a.site].region===eastById[id]?.region&&apprenticeActive(w,a.id)&&!crewRegistry(w)[a.id]&&!teamMember(w,a.id)&&!gatheringDuty(w,a.id));
  function crewAssign(w,area,id,worker){requireReady(w);const r=constructionState(w,id),a=apprenticesById[worker];if(!r||r.started!==now(w)||r.target<3||r.target>7||r.crew||r.team||!crewCandidates(w,id).includes(a))fail('Assign one available local apprentice before the foundation, frame, wall, roof or equipment phase starts.');const path=districtRoute(area,a.site,id,w);if(!path||path.length<2||path.length>241)fail('Explore a connected trail between the two sites first.');const travel=crewTravel(path),bonus=crewBonus(id,r.target,travel);if(bonus<1)fail('The commute is too long for this phase.');charge(w,{food:1});r.crew=worker;r.ends-=bonus;(w.development.crew1022??={})[worker]={site:id,target:r.target,started:r.started,arrival:r.started+travel,finish:r.ends,returned:r.ends+travel,path};}
  function crewDuty(w,worker,m=w.clock.minuteOfDay){const duty=teamDuty(w,worker,m);if(duty)return duty;const r=crewRegistry(w)[worker];if(!r)return null;const t=now(w)-w.clock.minuteOfDay+m;if(t<r.started||t>=r.returned)return null;const d=t<r.arrival?{phase:'To construction site',t:(t-r.started)/(r.arrival-r.started),moving:true}:t<r.finish?{phase:'Construction work',t:1,moving:false}:{phase:'Returning from construction',t:1-(t-r.finish)/(r.returned-r.finish),moving:true};return {...d,...gatheringPoint(r.path.map(p=>[p[0]*16,p[1]*16]),d.t),crew:true,site:r.site,target:r.target};}
  function crewTick(w,t){for(const[k,r]of Object.entries(crewRegistry(w)))if(t>=r.returned)delete w.development.crew1022[k];}
  function validateCrew(w){const all=w.development.crew1022;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>apprentices.length)fail('Invalid workforce registry.');const activeSites=new Set();for(const[worker,r]of Object.entries(all)){const a=apprenticesById[worker],p=eastById[r?.site];if(!a||!p||a.site===r.site||eastById[a.site].region!==p.region||!apprenticeActive(w,worker)||!integer(r.target)||r.target<3||r.target>7||!Array.isArray(r.path)||r.path.length<2||r.path.length>241||!r.path.every(courierValid)||!courierSame(r.path[0],districtAnchor(a.site))||!courierSame(r.path.at(-1),districtAnchor(p.id))||!r.path.slice(1).every((b,i)=>!!trailId(...r.path[i],...b))||!integer(r.started)||r.started>now(w))fail('Invalid worker route.');const travel=crewTravel(r.path),bonus=crewBonus(p.id,r.target,travel);if(!bonus||r.arrival!==r.started+travel||r.finish!==r.started+constructionMinutes(p.id,r.target)-bonus||r.returned!==r.finish+travel||r.returned<=now(w))fail('Invalid crew schedule.');if(now(w)<r.finish){const build=constructionState(w,p.id);if(activeSites.has(p.id)||!build||build.crew!==worker||build.started!==r.started||build.ends!==r.finish||build.target!==r.target)fail('Invalid crew reservation.');activeSites.add(p.id);}else if(eastState(w,p.id).stage<r.target)fail('Worker returned before phase completed.');}}
  const specializationDefs=Object.freeze({production:{name:'Production atelier',cost:{fitting:2,plank:2},detail:'15% shorter future orders.',mark:'P'},conservation:{name:'Conservation guild',cost:{'repair-kit':1,'field-book':1},detail:'Staffed halls gain 2 wear per order instead of 5.',mark:'C'},community:{name:'Community hall',cost:{'woven-cloth':2,'paper-sheet':1},detail:'One additional prosperity point per completed order, capped at 100.',mark:'H'}});
  function specializationSet(w,id,branch){requireReady(w);if(branchJob(w,id))fail("Collect the specialist commission before changing branch.");const s=eastState(w,id),def=specializationDefs[branch];if(!eastById[id]||!own(specializationDefs,branch)||!def||s.stage!==9||s.job||s.specialization===branch||branch==='conservation'&&!charterState(w)?.houses[id])fail('Specialize a completed idle hall; conservation needs staffed lodging.');charge(w,def.cost);s.specialization=branch;}

  // One physical expansion pilot: the library's upper reading hall gains four walkable rows.
  const annexId='mon-library',annexCost=Object.freeze({plank:4,brick:3,fitting:2,'paper-sheet':2}),annexMinutes=240;
  const annexLessons=Object.freeze([
    {title:'The reader’s guide',text:'The returning visitors found the old catalogue after the library had reopened.     {title:'The restoration notebook',text:'If the community protects its documents, future readers will be able to compare accounts of the same event.   ]);
  const annexState=w=>w?.development?.libraryAnnex1023||null;
  const annexComplete=w=>annexState(w)?.status==='open';
  function annexStart(w){requireReady(w);if(!galleryOpen(w,annexId)||eastState(w,annexId).job||annexState(w))fail('Restore the library and finish its current order first.');charge(w,annexCost);let started=now(w);w.development.libraryAnnex1023={status:'building',started,ends:started+annexMinutes,read:[]};}
  function annexAdvance(w,t){const r=annexState(w);if(r?.status==='building'&&r.ends<=t)r.status='open';}
  function annexAnswer(w,i,answer){const r=annexState(w),lesson=annexLessons[i];if(!annexComplete(w)||!lesson||!integer(i)||!integer(answer)||answer!==lesson.correct)fail('Read the text and try the correct answer.');if(r.read.includes(i))return false;r.read.push(i);return true;}
  function validateAnnex(w){const r=annexState(w);if(!own(w.development,'libraryAnnex1023'))return;if(!r||!galleryOpen(w,annexId)||!integer(r.started)||r.started<0||r.started>now(w)||r.ends!==r.started+annexMinutes||!['building','open'].includes(r.status)||r.status!==(r.ends<=now(w)?'open':'building')||!Array.isArray(r.read)||r.read.length>2||new Set(r.read).size!==r.read.length||r.read.some(i=>!integer(i)||!annexLessons[i])||r.status==='building'&&r.read.length)fail('Invalid library extension.');}

  // V102.4: optional mechanical power connections; finite, shared minute slots.
  const utilityHub='wind-loft',utilityLinks=w=>w.development.utilities1024?.links||{},utilityAuto=w=>w.development.utilities1024?.auto||{},utilityRoot=w=>w.development.utilities1024??={links:{},auto:{},enabled:true};

  const utilityMinutes=len=>60+2*len;
  function utilityCandidates(w){const h=eastById[utilityHub];return C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.id!==utilityHub&&p.region===h.region&&eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8&&Math.hypot(p.ax-h.ax,p.ay-h.ay)<=48&&!utilityLinks(w)[p.id]);}
  function utilitySurvey(w,area,id){requireReady(w);if(!charterState(w)||charterState(w).era<2||eastState(w,utilityHub).stage<8||!utilityCandidates(w).some(p=>p.id===id)||Object.keys(utilityLinks(w)).length>=4)fail('Restore Wind Loft and a nearby workshop; era III and up to four links.');const path=districtRoute(area,utilityHub,id,w,true);if(!path||!cityEdges(path)||!cityEdges(path).every(k=>cityEdgeClear(area,k)))fail('Explore a continuous unobstructed ground route first.');charge(w,{'survey-chart':1});utilityRoot(w).links[id]={path,surveyed:now(w),phase:'surveyed',enabled:true};}

  const utilityCapacity=(w,kind)=>eastState(w,utilityHub).stage<8||w.development.utilities1024?.enabled===false?0:({wind:2,clear:1,cloudy:1,rain:1,mist:1,heat:1,storm:0,snow:0}[kind]??0);
  const utilityWeather=t=>root.LQValleySimulation.weatherAt({day:1+Math.floor(t/1440),minuteOfDay:t%1440});
  function utilityStatus(w,id,t=now(w),weather=utilityWeather){const r=utilityLinks(w)[id];if(!r||r.phase==='surveyed'||t<(r.readyAt??Infinity))return {ok:true,label:r?'Connection under planning or construction':'Manual drive'};if(!r.enabled)return {ok:false,label:'Local transmission disengaged'};const cap=utilityCapacity(w,weather(t));if(!cap)return {ok:false,label:w.development.utilities1024?.enabled===false?'Wind Loft drive stopped':'No export power in current weather'};const ids=Object.entries(utilityLinks(w)).filter(([,v])=>v.enabled&&v.readyAt!==undefined&&v.readyAt<=t).map(([id])=>id).sort(),i=ids.indexOf(id),n=ids.length;const ok=i>=0&&((i-t%n+n)%n)<Math.min(cap,n);return {ok,label:ok?'Mechanical drive available':'Waiting for allocated drive slot'};}
  function utilityAdvance(w,t){for(const r of Object.values(utilityLinks(w)))if(r.phase==='building'&&r.readyAt<=t)r.phase='open';}
  function utilityAutoToggle(w,id){const l=infraLines(w)[id];if(!l||l.mode!=='water'||l.phase!=='open')fail('Commission a water pipeline first.');const a=utilityRoot(w).auto;if(a[id])delete a[id];else a[id]=true;}
  function utilityWaterPlan(w,id){const l=infraLines(w)[id];if(!l||l.mode!=='water'||l.phase!=='open')return {n:0,label:'Pipeline not commissioned'};const source=eastState(w,infraHub('water')),dest=eastState(w,l.to),need=eastById[l.to].input.water*2;const required=Math.max(0,need-count(dest.stock,'water'));if(!l.gate)return {n:0,label:'Valve closed'};if(l.job)return {n:0,label:'Water in transit: '+l.job.remaining};if(!required)return {n:0,label:'Target reserve met'};if(!count(source.stock,'water'))return {n:0,label:'Aqueduct reservoir empty'};const n=Math.min(10,required,count(source.stock,'water'),eastLimit-sum(dest.stock));return {n,label:n?'Ready to supply '+n+' water':'Destination store full'};}
  function utilityWaterTick(w){for(const id of Object.keys(utilityAuto(w)).sort()){const plan=utilityWaterPlan(w,id);if(plan.n)infraDispatch(w,id,'water',plan.n);}}
  function validateUtilities(w){const u=w.development.utilities1024;if(u===undefined)return;if(!u||Object.getPrototypeOf(u)!==Object.prototype||!u.links||Object.getPrototypeOf(u.links)!==Object.prototype||!u.auto||Object.getPrototypeOf(u.auto)!==Object.prototype||typeof u.enabled!=='boolean'||Object.keys(u.links).length>4)fail('Invalid utility registry.');for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(u.links)){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||id===utilityHub||p.region!==eastById[utilityHub].region||eastState(w,id).stage<8||eastState(w,utilityHub).stage<8||!r||!cityEdges(r.path)||!courierSame(r.path[0],districtAnchor(utilityHub))||!courierSame(r.path.at(-1),districtAnchor(id))||!integer(r.surveyed)||r.surveyed>now(w)||typeof r.enabled!=='boolean'||!['surveyed','building','open'].includes(r.phase)||r.phase==='surveyed'&&r.readyAt!==undefined||r.phase!=='surveyed'&&(!integer(r.readyAt)||r.readyAt<r.surveyed+utilityMinutes(r.path.length)||(r.phase==='open')!==(r.readyAt<=now(w))))fail('Invalid mechanical connection.');}for(const [id,v]of Object.entries(u.auto))if(v!==true||infraLines(w)[id]?.mode!=='water'||infraLines(w)[id]?.phase!=='open')fail('Invalid automatic water agreement.');}

  // Two further buildable upper-room wings. Existing library save schema is untouched.

  // V102.5: funded roadside pocket plazas, tied to surveyed, completed roads.
  const civicPockets=w=>w.development.civicPockets1025||{};
  const pocketCost=Object.freeze({stone:3,brick:2,plank:2}),pocketMinutes=90;
  const pocketThemes=Object.freeze(['birch','heath','copper','fen','prairie','moon']);
  function pocketLocation(p,index,side){const a=p.path[index],b=p.path[index+1],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];return {x:(a[0]+b[0])*8-dy*side*32,y:(a[1]+b[1])*8+dx*side*32,ax:(a[0]+b[0])*8,ay:(a[1]+b[1])*8};}
  function pocketSafe(w,area,id,index,side){const p=cityProjects(w)[id],P=root.LQPrecisePhysics;if(!p||!P||!area?.__lqPrecise||!integer(index)||index<1||index>=p.path.length-2||![1,-1].includes(side))return false;const q=pocketLocation(p,index,side);if(!cityEdgeClear(area,cityEdges(p.path)[index]))return false;
    // A flush paved, walkable 24x24 lay-by. No collidable furniture; keep every sampled point clear.
    for(const dx of [-12,-6,0,6,12])for(const dy of [-12,-6,0,6,12])if(P.playerBlocked(area,q.x+dx,q.y+dy))return false;
    if(!P.segmentClear(area,{x:q.ax,y:q.ay},{x:q.x,y:q.y}))return false;
    for(const f of [0,.25,.5,.75,1])if(P.playerBlocked(area,q.ax+(q.x-q.ax)*f,q.ay+(q.y-q.ay)*f))return false;
    for(const v of Object.values(cityProjects(w)))for(const a of v.path)if(Math.hypot(a[0]*16-q.x,a[1]*16-q.y)<20)return false;
    for(const v of Object.values(civicPockets(w)))if(Math.hypot(v.x-q.x,v.y-q.y)<54)return false;
    for(const v of commons)if(Math.hypot(v.x*C.TILE-q.x,v.y*C.TILE-q.y)<80)return false;
    for(const v of C.HOMES)if(Math.hypot(v.ax*C.TILE-q.x,v.ay*C.TILE-q.y)<70)return false;
    return true;
  }
  function pocketCandidate(w,area,id){const p=cityProjects(w)[id],g=cityProgress(w,id);if(!p||!g.total||g.paved!==g.total||p.pending||civicPockets(w)[id])return null;const order=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,p.path.length-3)},(_,i)=>i+1).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a-p.path.length/2)-Math.abs(b-p.path.length/2));for(const index of order)for(const side of [1,-1])if(pocketSafe(w,area,id,index,side))return {index,side,...pocketLocation(p,index,side)};return null;}
  function pocketBuild(w,area,id){requireReady(w);const q=pocketCandidate(w,area,id);if(!q)fail('Complete the stone road and find a clear roadside lay-by.');charge(w,pocketCost);(w.development.civicPockets1025??={})[id]={...q,started:now(w),ends:now(w)+pocketMinutes,status:'building',water:0,visits:0,lastDay:-1};}
  function pocketAdvance(w,t){for(const r of Object.values(civicPockets(w)))if(r.status==='building'&&r.ends<=t)r.status='open';}
  function pocketWater(w,id,put){const r=civicPockets(w)[id];if(!r||r.status!=='open')fail('Open the public lay-by first.');if(put){if(r.water>=8)fail('Public water reserve is full.');charge(w,{water:1});r.water++;}else{if(!r.water)fail('The reserve is empty.');give(w,'water',1);r.water--;}}
  function pocketVisit(w,id){const r=civicPockets(w)[id],day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440);if(!r||r.status!=='open')fail('Open the lay-by first.');if(r.lastDay===day)return false;r.lastDay=day;r.visits++;return true;}
  function validatePockets(w){const all=w.development.civicPockets1025;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>24)fail('Invalid pocket registry.');for(const [id,r]of Object.entries(all)){const p=cityProjects(w)[id],g=cityProgress(w,id);if(!p||!r||!g.total||g.paved!==g.total||!integer(r.index)||r.index<1||r.index>=p.path.length-2||![1,-1].includes(r.side))fail('Invalid pocket road.');const q=pocketLocation(p,r.index,r.side);if(Object.keys(q).some(k=>q[k]!==r[k])||!integer(r.started)||r.started>now(w)||r.ends!==r.started+pocketMinutes||r.status!==(r.ends<=now(w)?'open':'building')||!integer(r.water)||r.water>8||!integer(r.visits)||!Number.isInteger(r.lastDay)||r.lastDay< -1||r.lastDay>Math.floor(now(w)/1440)||r.visits===0&&r.lastDay!==-1||r.visits>0&&r.lastDay<0||r.status==='building'&&(r.water||r.visits))fail('Invalid pocket state.');}}
  // Distinct branch commissions: separate manual projects using known inventory items, not duplicate ordinary orders.
  const branchProjects=Object.freeze({

      production:{name:'Bookbinding desk',cost:{'paper-sheet':3,'woven-cloth':1},out:'field-book',n:1,minutes:100,question:'Why should the pages be numbered before binding?',answers:['To keep them in order.','To make the ink vanish.','To hide the title.'],correct:0},
      conservation:{name:'Manuscript preservation',cost:{'field-book':1,'paper-sheet':2},out:'research-scroll',n:1,minutes:150,question:'Why should a fragile original be copied?',answers:['To erase its record.','To reduce handling of the original.','To make the archive smaller.'],correct:1},

      conservation:{name:'Native plant seed bank',cost:{'mint-seed':2,'paper-sheet':1,water:1},out:'field-book',n:1,minutes:135,question:'Why label a seed batch?',answers:['To forget its source.','To record its origin.','To change its species.'],correct:1},

  });
  const branchJob=(w,id)=>w.development.branchJobs1025?.[id]||null;
