  // V92: real work orders and site-specific training. Optional save extension.
  const masteryBySite=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(masteryProfessions.flatMap(p=>p.sites.map(id=>[id,p]))));
  const masteryEmpty=()=>({completed:0,phase:0,baseline:0,lastOrder:0,at:0});
  const masteryState=(w,id)=>w.development.mastery?.[id]||masteryEmpty();
  const masteryRank=n=>n>=6?3:n>=3?2:n>=1?1:0;
  const masteryFactor=(w,id)=>100-4*masteryRank(masteryState(w,id).completed);
  function masteryQuestion(w,id){const p=masteryBySite[id],s=masteryState(w,id);if(!p)return null;const q=p.questions[Math.min(2,masteryRank(s.completed))],flip=(s.completed+p.sites.indexOf(id))%2;return {prompt:q[0],answers:flip?[q[2],q[1]]:[q[1],q[2]],correct:flip};}
  function masteryMissing(w,id){const p=eastById[id],s=eastState(w,id);if(!p)return {};return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p.input).map(([k,n])=>[k,Math.max(0,n-count(s.stock,k))]).filter(([,n])=>n>0));}
  function masteryAction(w,id,cmd,arg){requireReady(w);const p=eastById[id],job=eastState(w,id),prev=masteryState(w,id);if(!masteryBySite[id]||job.stage<8)fail('Restore this workshop first.');const rec={...prev};
    if(cmd==='begin'){
      if(rec.phase!==0||job.job||civicPolicy(w,id)||arg!==masteryQuestion(w,id).correct)fail('Review the briefing and finish any standing orders first.');
      if(rec.completed>=6)fail('Mastery certificate already earned.');rec.phase=1;rec.baseline=job.runs;rec.at=now(w);
    }else if(cmd==='stage'){
      if(rec.phase!==1||job.job||job.runs!==rec.baseline||civicPolicy(w,id))fail('Start a fresh supervised batch.');const missing=masteryMissing(w,id);if(!sum(missing))fail('All ingredients are already staged.');if(sum(job.stock)+sum(missing)>eastLimit||!enough(w.resources,missing))fail('Bring all missing ingredients and clear storage space.');for(const[k,n]of Object.entries(missing))eastTransfer(w,id,k,n,true);
    }else if(cmd==='commission'){
      if(rec.phase!==1||job.job||job.runs!==rec.baseline||civicPolicy(w,id)||!enough(job.stock,p.input))fail('Stage all ingredients and stop standing orders first.');eastOrder(w,id,rec.baseline);job.job.masteryTrial=true;rec.phase=2;rec.at=now(w);
    }else if(cmd==='review'){
      if(rec.phase!==3||job.runs<=rec.baseline||job.runs<=rec.lastOrder)fail('Wait for the supervised order to complete.');rec.completed++;rec.lastOrder=job.runs;rec.phase=0;rec.baseline=job.runs;rec.at=now(w);
      const m=job.memory??={talks:0,lastDay:-1,trust:0,help:0};m.trust=Math.min(100,m.trust+2);m.help=Math.max(m.help,job.runs);
    }else if(cmd==='reset'){
      if(!rec.phase)fail('No training plan is open.');if(job.job?.masteryTrial)delete job.job.masteryTrial;rec.phase=0;rec.baseline=job.runs;rec.at=now(w);
    }else fail('Unknown mastery action.');
    (w.development.mastery??={})[id]=rec;
  }
  function validateMastery(w){const all=w.development.mastery;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid mastery registry.');for(const[id,v]of Object.entries(all)){const s=eastState(w,id);if(!masteryBySite[id]||s.stage<8||!v||Object.getPrototypeOf(v)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(v).some(k=>!['completed','phase','baseline','lastOrder','at'].includes(k))||![v.completed,v.phase,v.baseline,v.lastOrder,v.at].every(integer)||v.completed>6||v.phase>3||v.baseline>s.runs||v.lastOrder>v.baseline||v.completed>v.lastOrder||v.at>now(w)||v.completed===6&&v.phase!==0||v.phase===2&&(!s.job?.masteryTrial||s.job.expected!==v.baseline)||v.phase===3&&s.runs<=v.baseline)fail('Invalid mastery record.');}}

  // V93: optional persisted physical object states, no duplicated inventory.
  const livingObjectIds=['stock','aux','ledger'];
  const livingOpen=(w,id,obj)=>w.development.physical?.[id]?.[obj]===true;
  function livingToggle(w,id,obj){if(!eastById[id]||!roomOpen(w,id)||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||!livingObjectIds.includes(obj))fail('Stand at an installed room object.');const rec=(w.development.physical??={})[id]??={};rec[obj]=!livingOpen(w,id,obj);return rec[obj];}
  function validatePhysical(w){const d=w.development.physical;if(d===undefined)return;if(!d||Object.getPrototypeOf(d)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid physical registry.');for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(d)){if(!eastById[id]||!roomOpen(w,id)||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||!r||Object.getPrototypeOf(r)!==Object.prototype||Object.entries(r).some(([k,v])=>!livingObjectIds.includes(k)||typeof v!=='boolean'))fail('Invalid physical object.');}}
  // Daily itinerary uses the appointed lodging and actual workshop. Production remains governed by the original ledger.
  function livingShift(w,id,m){const p=eastById[id];if(!p||eastState(w,id).stage<8)return null;m=((m%1440)+1440)%1440;const housed=!!charterState(w)?.houses[id],offset=(C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p)%5)*5;
    if(m<390+offset||m>=1260)return {activity:housed?'Sleeping':'Off duty',station:'rest',from:'rest',t:1,move:false,pose:housed?'sleep':'sit'};
    const seq=[[390+offset,420+offset,'Breakfast','rest','rest','sit'],[420+offset,440+offset,'Opening workshop','rest','stock','walk'],[440+offset,465+offset,'Sorting supplies','stock','stock','carry'],[465+offset,480+offset,'Setting up','stock','work','walk'],[480+offset,660+offset,'Morning work','work','work','work'],[660+offset,680+offset,'Lunch break','work','rest','walk'],[680+offset,745+offset,'Lunch and reading','rest','rest','sit'],[745+offset,765+offset,'Returning to work','rest','work','walk'],[765+offset,990+offset,'Afternoon work','work','work','work'],[990+offset,1010+offset,'Stock check','work','stock','walk'],[1010+offset,1050+offset,'Packing goods','stock','stock','carry'],[1050+offset,1070+offset,'Bookkeeping','stock','ledger','walk'],[1070+offset,1110,'Reading records','ledger','ledger','read'],[1110,1135,'Returning to lodging','ledger','rest','walk'],[1135,1260,'Evening rest','rest','rest','sit']];
    const row=seq.find(q=>m>=q[0]&&m<q[1]);if(!row)return {activity:'Resting',station:'rest',from:'rest',t:1,move:false,pose:'sit'};const[a,b,activity,from,station,pose]=row;return {activity,station,from,t:from===station?1:(m-a)/(b-a),move:from!==station,pose};}
  // Quarter-tile cardinal A*: only use roomWalkable, never cross furniture. Cache is held by room instance in the renderer.
  function livingRoomPath(id,from,to){const l=roomLayouts[id];if(!l)return null;const get=k=>l.objects.find(o=>o.id===k||k==='work'&&o.action==='work');const a=get(from),b=get(to);if(!a||!b)return null;const step=4,W=l.w*step,H=l.h*step,point=(x,y)=>({x:x/step,y:y/step}),walk=(x,y)=>roomWalkable(l,x/step,y/step,.23),near=o=>{let best=null,dist=Infinity;for(let y=Math.max(0,Math.floor(o.ay*step)-4);y<=Math.min(H,Math.ceil(o.ay*step)+4);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.floor(o.ax*step)-4);x<=Math.min(W,Math.ceil(o.ax*step)+4);x++){const d=Math.hypot(x/step-o.ax,y/step-o.ay);if(d<dist&&walk(x,y)){best=[x,y];dist=d;}}return best;};const start=near(a),end=near(b);if(!start||!end)return null;const key=(x,y)=>y*(W+1)+x,s=key(...start),e=key(...end),queue=[start],parents=new Map([[s,-1]]);for(let i=0;i<queue.length;i++){const[x,y]=queue[i],k=key(x,y);if(k===e){const p=[];for(let j=e;j!==-1;j=parents.get(j))p.push(point(j%(W+1),Math.floor(j/(W+1))));return p.reverse();}for(const[dx,dy]of [[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const X=x+dx,Y=y+dy,K=key(X,Y);if(X>=0&&X<=W&&Y>=0&&Y<=H&&!parents.has(K)&&walk(X,Y)&&roomWalkable(l,(x+X)/(2*step),(y+Y)/(2*step),.23)){parents.set(K,k);queue.push([X,Y]);}}}return null;}
  function livingPoint(path,t){if(!path?.length)return null;const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t))*(path.length-1),i=Math.floor(x),a=path[i],b=path[Math.min(i+1,path.length-1)],f=x-i;return{x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f};}
  // The existing charter transactions remain authoritative. This exposes real neighbour contacts, never invented purchases.
  function livingContacts(w,id){const c=charterState(w);if(!c)return [];return c.ledger.filter(e=>e.kind==='sale'&&(e.id===id||e.buyer===id)).slice(-8).reverse().map(e=>({other:e.id===id?e.buyer:e.id,key:e.key,n:e.n,at:e.at,bought:e.buyer===id}));}
  const livingCartCost=Object.freeze({plank:3,fitting:2,'woven-cloth':1});
  function livingCart(w){return !!w.development.freightCart;}
  function livingBuildCart(w,id){requireReady(w);if(id!=='mon-terminal'||eastState(w,id).stage<8||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||districtCargo(w).job||livingCart(w))fail('Visit the operating railway terminal with no active delivery.');charge(w,livingCartCost);w.development.freightCart={at:now(w)};}
  function validateLivingCart(w){const c=w.development.freightCart;if(c===undefined)return;if(!c||Object.getPrototypeOf(c)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(c).length!==1||!integer(c.at)||c.at>now(w)||eastState(w,'mon-terminal').stage<8||!w.development.rooms?.['mon-terminal'])fail('Invalid freight cart.');}
  // Only the actual courier manifest can move cargo. Extra step is allowed on ground and good weather; no item minting.
  function livingCartBonus(w,j,t){return livingCart(w)&&t%2===0&&!['storm','snow','heat','rain'].includes(w.weather?.kind)&&j.cursor>=j.pickup&&j.cursor<j.path.length-1&&(!j.levels||j.levels[j.cursor]==='ground'&&j.levels[j.cursor+1]==='ground');}

  // V94: small-gauge freight lines and pumped water links over surveyed ground corridors.
  const infraHub=mode=>mode==='rail'?'mon-terminal':mode==='water'?'mon-aqueduct':null;
  const infraLines=w=>w.development.infrastructure||{};
  const infraId=(mode,to)=>mode+'_'+to;
  function infraCandidates(w,mode){const hub=eastById[infraHub(mode)];if(!hub)return [];return C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p!==hub&&eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8&&Math.hypot(p.ax-hub.ax,p.ay-hub.ay)<=48&&(mode==='water'?!!p.input.water:eastKeys(p.id).some(k=>eastKeys(hub.id).includes(k)))).sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.ax-hub.ax,a.ay-hub.ay)-Math.hypot(b.ax-hub.ax,b.ay-hub.ay));}
  const infraCost=(mode,len)=>mode==='rail'?{plank:Math.ceil(len/12)+2,fitting:Math.ceil(len/16)+2,brick:Math.ceil(len/24)+1}:{fitting:Math.ceil(len/12)+1,brick:Math.ceil(len/16)+1,plank:2};
  const infraDuration=len=>20+len*2;
  function infraSurvey(w,area,mode,to){requireReady(w);const hub=infraHub(mode),id=infraId(mode,to);if(!hub||!roomOpen(w,hub)||!w.development.rooms?.[hub]||!infraCandidates(w,mode).some(p=>p.id===to)||infraLines(w)[id]||Object.values(infraLines(w)).filter(l=>l.mode===mode).length>=4)fail('Choose an operating nearby facility; up to four links per utility.');const path=districtRoute(area,hub,to,w);if(!path||path.length<3||path.length>240)fail('No suitable surveyed ground corridor (3–240 half-tile points). Walk a continuous level-8 trail first.');const used=new Set();for(const l of Object.values(infraLines(w)))if(l.mode===mode)for(let i=1;i<l.path.length;i++)used.add(trailId(...l.path[i-1],...l.path[i]));if(mode==='rail'&&path.slice(1).some((p,i)=>used.has(trailId(...path[i],...p))))fail('This route shares an existing track. Choose a separate corridor.');charge(w,{'survey-chart':1});(w.development.infrastructure??={})[id]={mode,to,path,phase:'surveyed',surveyed:now(w),position:0,gate:true,delivered:0,journeys:0};return id;}
  function infraBuild(w,id){const l=infraLines(w)[id];if(!l||l.phase!=='surveyed')fail('Survey this link first.');charge(w,infraCost(l.mode,l.path.length));l.phase='building';l.readyAt=now(w)+infraDuration(l.path.length);}
  function infraGate(w,id){const l=infraLines(w)[id];if(!l||l.mode!=='water'||l.phase!=='open')fail('Open the pipeline first.');l.gate=!l.gate;}
  function infraDispatch(w,id,key,n){const l=infraLines(w)[id];if(!l||l.phase!=='open'||l.job)fail('Link is not idle and operational.');const hub=infraHub(l.mode),from=l.position===0?hub:l.to,to=l.position===0?l.to:hub;if(l.mode==='water'&&(key!=='water'||l.position!==0||!l.gate))fail('Open the valve and choose water.');if(!integer(n)||n<0||n>(l.mode==='rail'?12:10)||(!n&&l.mode!=='rail')||(!n&&key!==''))fail('Invalid load.');if(n&&(!eastKeys(from).includes(key)||!eastKeys(to).includes(key)||count(eastState(w,from).stock,key)<n||sum(eastState(w,to).stock)+n>eastLimit))fail('Insufficient compatible cargo or destination storage.');if(n){const s=eastState(w,from);add(s.stock,key,-n);if(!s.stock[key])delete s.stock[key];}l.job={key,n,remaining:n,progress:0,started:now(w)};}
  function infraCancel(w,id){const l=infraLines(w)[id],j=l?.job;if(!j||j.progress!==0||j.remaining!==j.n)fail('Shipment already departed.');const s=eastState(w,l.position===0?infraHub(l.mode):l.to);if(sum(s.stock)+j.n>eastLimit)fail('Source storage is full.');if(j.n)add(s.stock,j.key,j.n);delete l.job;}
  function infraTick(w,from,to,weather){for(const l of Object.values(infraLines(w))){if(l.phase==='building'&&l.readyAt<=to)l.phase='open';if(l.phase!=='open'||!l.job)continue;const j=l.job,d=eastState(w,l.position===0?l.to:infraHub(l.mode)),distance=l.path.length-1;for(let t=Math.max(from,j.started);t<to;t++){const m=t%1440,kind=weather({day:Math.floor(t/1440)+1,minuteOfDay:m});if(l.mode==='rail'){if(j.progress<distance){if(m<360||m>=1260||kind==='storm'||kind==='snow')continue;j.progress=Math.min(distance,j.progress+3);}if(j.progress===distance&&sum(d.stock)+j.n<=eastLimit){if(j.n)add(d.stock,j.key,j.n);l.delivered+=j.n;l.journeys++;l.position=1-l.position;delete l.job;break;}}else{if(!l.gate||kind==='snow')continue;if(j.progress<distance)j.progress=Math.min(distance,j.progress+2);else if(t%6===0&&sum(d.stock)<eastLimit){add(d.stock,'water',1);j.remaining--;l.delivered++;if(!j.remaining){l.journeys++;delete l.job;break;}}}}}}
  function validateInfra(w){const all=w.development.infrastructure;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype||Object.keys(all).length>8)fail('Invalid infrastructure registry.');const railEdges=new Set(),modeCounts={rail:0,water:0};for(const [id,l] of Object.entries(all)){const hub=infraHub(l?.mode);if(!hub||id!==infraId(l.mode,l.to)||!eastById[l.to]||l.to===hub||!roomOpen(w,hub)||!roomOpen(w,l.to)||!['surveyed','building','open'].includes(l.phase)||!integer(l.surveyed)||l.surveyed>now(w)||!Array.isArray(l.path)||l.path.length<3||l.path.length>240||!l.path.every(courierValid)||!courierSame(l.path[0],districtAnchor(hub))||!courierSame(l.path.at(-1),districtAnchor(l.to))||new Set(l.path.map(p=>p.join(','))).size!==l.path.length||l.path.slice(1).some((p,i)=>!trailId(...l.path[i],...p))||![0,1].includes(l.position)||l.mode==='water'&&l.position!==0||typeof l.gate!=='boolean'||!integer(l.delivered)||!integer(l.journeys))fail('Invalid infrastructure line.');if(++modeCounts[l.mode]>4)fail('Too many utility links.');if(l.mode==='rail')for(let i=1;i<l.path.length;i++){const edge=trailId(...l.path[i-1],...l.path[i]);if(railEdges.has(edge))fail('Overlapping rail segments.');railEdges.add(edge);}if(l.phase==='surveyed'?(l.readyAt!==undefined||l.job||l.position||l.delivered||l.journeys):(!integer(l.readyAt)||l.readyAt<l.surveyed+infraDuration(l.path.length)||l.phase==='open'&&l.readyAt>now(w)||l.phase==='building'&&(l.readyAt<=now(w)||l.job)))fail('Invalid construction timing.');const j=l.job;if(j){const from=l.position?l.to:hub,to=l.position?hub:l.to;if(!integer(j.n)||j.n<0||j.n>(l.mode==='rail'?12:10)||!integer(j.remaining)||j.remaining>j.n||l.mode==='rail'&&j.remaining!==j.n||l.mode==='water'&&(j.key!=='water'||!j.remaining)||(!j.n&&j.key!=='')||j.n&&(!eastKeys(from).includes(j.key)||!eastKeys(to).includes(j.key))||!integer(j.progress)||j.progress>l.path.length-1||!integer(j.started)||j.started<l.readyAt||j.started>now(w))fail('Invalid infrastructure cargo.');}}}

  // V95 independent residential plots share the charter household ledger, never duplicate wages or possessions.
  const homeById=Object.fromEntries(C.HOMES.map(h=>[h.id,h])),homeBySite=Object.fromEntries(C.HOMES.map(h=>[h.site,h]));
  const homeStages=['Reserved plot','Stone foundation','Weatherproof shell','Furnished home'];
  const homeCosts=[{stone:4,plank:2},{plank:5,brick:3,fitting:1},{cabinet:1,'woven-cloth':2,plank:2}];
  const homeMinutes=[90,180,120];
  const homeState=(w,id)=>w.development.homeland?.[id]||{stage:0,readyAt:0,visits:0,lastVisit:-1};
  function homeStart(w,id){requireReady(w);const h=homeById[id],v=homeState(w,id);if(!h||!charterState(w)?.houses[h.site]||eastState(w,h.site).stage<8||v.stage>=3||v.readyAt)fail('Assign the resident staff lodging first, then finish the current phase.');charge(w,homeCosts[v.stage]);const r=(w.development.homeland??={})[id]??=v;r.readyAt=now(w)+homeMinutes[r.stage];}
  function homeTick(w,t){for(const r of Object.values(w.development.homeland||{}))if(r.readyAt&&r.readyAt<=t){r.stage++;r.readyAt=0;}}
  function homeGreet(w,id,choice){const h=homeById[id],r=homeState(w,id);if(!h||r.stage!==3||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||!homeSocial(w,id,w.clock.minuteOfDay))fail('Visit at breakfast or in the evening.');const day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440);if(r.lastVisit===day)fail('You already visited today.');if(choice!==C.HOMES.indexOf(h)%3)fail('Choose the courteous response.');r.visits++;r.lastVisit=day;const s=eastState(w,h.site),m=s.memory??={talks:0,lastDay:-1,trust:0,help:0};m.trust=Math.min(100,m.trust+1);}
  function homeAt(w,id,m){return homeState(w,id).stage===3&&(m<360||m>=1140)&&!neighbourhood(w,id,m);}
  function homeSocial(w,id,m){return homeAt(w,id,m)&&(m>=345&&m<360||m>=1140&&m<1240);}
  function homeSchedule(w,id,m){if(!homeAt(w,id,m))return null;const rows=[[0,330,'bed','bed','sleep'],[330,345,'bed','table','walk'],[345,360,'table','table','sit'],[1140,1160,'table','table','sit'],[1160,1170,'table','books','walk'],[1170,1240,'books','books','read'],[1240,1260,'books','bed','walk'],[1260,1441,'bed','bed','sleep']];const [a,b,from,to,pose]=rows.find(r=>m>=r[0]&&m<r[1]);return {from,to,pose,t:from===to?1:(m-a)/(b-a)};}
  function homeDuty(w,site,m){const h=homeBySite[site];if(!h||homeState(w,h.id).stage!==3)return undefined;const social=neighbourhood(w,h.id,m);if(social)return social;const path=C.HOME_ROUTES[h.id];if(!path?.length)return undefined;if(m<360||m>=1140)return null;if(m<380)return {x:h.ax,y:h.ay,moving:false,working:false,dx:0,activity:'Garden',errand:true};if(m<420||m>=1080){let q=m<420?(m-380)/40:1-(m-1080)/60;const i=Math.min(path.length-2,Math.floor(q*(path.length-1))),a=path[i],b=path[i+1],f=q*(path.length-1)-i;return {x:a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f,y:a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f,moving:true,working:false,dx:(b[0]-a[0])*(m<420?1:-1),dy:(b[1]-a[1])*(m<420?1:-1),activity:m<420?'Going to work':'Going home',errand:true};}return undefined;}
  function validateHomes(w){const all=w.development.homeland;if(all===undefined)return;if(!all||Object.getPrototypeOf(all)!==Object.prototype)fail('Invalid residential registry.');for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(all)){const h=homeById[id];if(!h||!charterState(w)?.houses[h.site]||!r||!integer(r.stage)||r.stage>3||!integer(r.readyAt)||r.readyAt&& (r.stage===3||r.readyAt<=now(w)||r.readyAt>now(w)+homeMinutes[r.stage])||!integer(r.visits)||!Number.isInteger(r.lastVisit)||r.lastVisit< -1||r.lastVisit>Math.floor(now(w)/1440)||r.visits>r.lastVisit+1||r.stage<3&&(r.visits||r.lastVisit!==-1))fail('Invalid residential state.');}}

  // V96 household object state and six paired neighbourhood visits.
  const residencePairs=C.HOMES.filter((_,i)=>i%2===0).map((h,i)=>[h.id,C.HOMES[i*2+1].id]);
  const partnerOf=id=>{const i=C.HOMES.findIndex(h=>h.id===id);return i<0?null:C.HOMES[i^1];};
  const domesticState=(w,id)=>w.development.domestic?.[id]||{};
  function domesticToggle(w,id,obj){if(!homeById[id]||homeState(w,id).stage!==3||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||!['pantry','books','curtains'].includes(obj))fail('Visit the installed household object.');const r=(w.development.domestic??={})[id]??={};r[obj]=!r[obj];return r[obj];}
  function domesticServe(w,id,key){const h=homeById[id],r=w.development.rooms?.[id],staff=h&&charterState(w)?.houses[h.site],day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440);if(!staff||!r||!homeAt(w,id,w.clock.minuteOfDay)||!['food','water'].includes(key))fail('Resident must be at home.');const field=key==='food'?'lastMeal':'lastWater';if(staff[field]===day||key==='food'&&districtMemory(w,h.site).mealDay===day)fail('Today’s need is already met.');if(!r.stock?.[key])fail('The cupboard is empty.');add(r.stock,key,-1);if(!r.stock[key])delete r.stock[key];staff[field]=day;}
  function neighbourhood(w,id,m,day=Math.floor(now(w)/1440),weather=w.weather.kind){const h=homeById[id],p=partnerOf(id);if(!h||!p||homeState(w,id).stage!==3||homeState(w,p.id).stage!==3||['storm','snow','heat'].includes(weather)||m<1155||m>=1230||!C.NEIGHBOUR_ROUTES[id]?.length)return null;const i=C.HOMES.indexOf(h),pair=Math.floor(i/2);if((day+pair)%3!==0)return null;const guest=(Math.floor((day+pair)/3)+pair)%2===i%2;if(!guest){if(m<1175||m>=1205)return null;return {x:h.ax,y:h.ay,moving:false,working:false,dx:0,dy:1,errand:true,activity:'Meeting neighbour'};}const path=C.NEIGHBOUR_ROUTES[id];if(m>=1175&&m<1205)return {x:p.ax,y:p.ay+.18,moving:false,working:false,dx:0,dy:-1,errand:true,activity:'Visiting '+p.name};const f=m<1175?(m-1155)/20:1-(m-1205)/25,q=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,f))*(path.length-1),j=Math.min(path.length-2,Math.floor(q)),a=path[j],b=path[j+1],u=q-j,dir=m<1175?1:-1;return {x:a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,y:a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u,moving:true,working:false,dx:(b[0]-a[0])*dir,dy:(b[1]-a[1])*dir,errand:true,activity:'Neighbourhood walk'};}
  function neighbourTick(w,from,to,weather){if(to<=from)return;for(let day=Math.floor(from/1440);day<=Math.floor(to/1440);day++){const t=day*1440+1200;if(!(from<t&&t<=to))continue;for(const [id,p] of residencePairs){if(!neighbourhood(w,id,1200,day,typeof weather==='function'?weather({day:day+1,minuteOfDay:1200}):typeof weather==='string'?weather:w.weather.kind)||!neighbourhood(w,p,1200,day,typeof weather==='function'?weather({day:day+1,minuteOfDay:1200}):typeof weather==='string'?weather:w.weather.kind))continue;const r=(w.development.neighbours??={})[id]??={meetings:0,lastDay:-1};if(r.lastDay>=day)continue;r.meetings++;r.lastDay=day;}}}
  const neighbourRecord=(w,id)=>{const h=homeById[id],i=C.HOMES.indexOf(h);return i<0?{meetings:0,lastDay:-1}:w.development.neighbours?.[C.HOMES[i-i%2].id]||{meetings:0,lastDay:-1};};
  function validateDomestic(w){for(const key of ["domestic","neighbours"]){const v=w.development[key];if(v!==undefined&&(!v||Object.getPrototypeOf(v)!==Object.prototype))fail("Invalid household registry.");}for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(w.development.domestic||{})){if(!homeById[id]||homeState(w,id).stage!==3||!w.development.rooms?.[id]||!r||Object.getPrototypeOf(r)!==Object.prototype||Object.entries(r).some(([k,v])=>!['pantry','books','curtains'].includes(k)||typeof v!=='boolean'))fail('Invalid household object state.');}for(const[id,r]of Object.entries(w.development.neighbours||{})){const p=residencePairs.find(a=>a[0]===id);if(!p||p.some(k=>homeState(w,k).stage!==3)||!r||!integer(r.meetings)||!Number.isInteger(r.lastDay)||r.lastDay<0||r.lastDay>Math.floor(now(w)/1440)||r.meetings>Math.floor(r.lastDay/3)+1||r.meetings<1)fail('Invalid neighbourhood record.');}}

  root.LQValleyDevelopment=Object.freeze({teamRegistry,teamAt,teamMember,teamAssign,teamDuty,teamStash,blueprintFloors,civicPockets,pocketCost,pocketCandidate,pocketBuild,pocketWater,pocketVisit,branchProjects,branchDef,branchJob,branchStart,branchCollect,utilityHub,utilityLinks,utilityAuto,utilityCost,utilityMinutes,utilityCandidates,utilitySurvey,utilityBuild,utilityToggle,utilitySwitch,utilityCapacity,utilityStatus,utilityAutoToggle,utilityWaterPlan,wingDefs,wingState,wingStart,wingAnswer,wingLayout,annexState,annexComplete,annexCost,annexMinutes,annexLessons,annexStart,annexAnswer,annexLayout,crewRegistry,crewCandidates,crewAssign,crewDuty,crewAtSite,specializationDefs,specializationSet,constructionMinutes,constructionState,constructionProgress,constructionStart,constructionCancel,gatheringRoutes,gatheringHours,gatheringPoint,gatheringDuty,gatheringQuestion,gatheringRecord,gatheringTalk,commons,commonById,commonCosts,commonMinutes,commonState,commonReady,commonBuild,commonTend,commonEventOpen,commonAttend,cityProjects,cityId,cityCandidates,cityEdges,citySurvey,cityProgress,cityNext,cityBuild,cityDiscard,citySummary,cityReserved,archiveCatalog,archiveOpen,archiveState,archiveChoices,archiveRead,archiveSolve,archiveCount,archiveHatch,archiveLayout,galleryCatalog,galleryOpen,galleryLayout,galleryProgress,galleryAnswer,domesticState,domesticToggle,domesticServe,neighbourhood,neighbourRecord,homeById,homeBySite,homeStages,homeCosts,homeMinutes,homeState,homeStart,homeGreet,homeAt,homeSocial,homeSchedule,homeDuty,infraLines,infraId,infraCandidates,infraCost,infraDuration,infraSurvey,infraBuild,infraGate,infraDispatch,infraCancel,infraTick,livingOpen,livingToggle,livingShift,livingRoomPath,livingPoint,livingContacts,livingCartCost,livingCart,livingBuildCart,livingCartBonus,masteryProfessions,masteryBySite,masteryRank,masteryState,masteryFactor,masteryQuestion,masteryMissing,masteryAction,apprentices,apprenticesById,apprenticeActive,apprenticeship,apprenticeDuty,apprenticeLesson,regionalRequests,regionalById,regionalState,regionalCargo,regionalAccept,regionalDeliver,regionalReturn,regionalAnswer,monumentReady,festivals,festivalYear,festivalDay,festivalAt,festivalActive,festivalDone,festivalJoin,studyBranches,studyLevel,studyAdvance,studyRate,chainRoutes,chainStatus,chainSend,publicWorks,publicState,publicAt,publicReady,publicStart,publicFactor,charterEras,charterTech,charterState,charterHas,charterFound,charterFund,charterHomeCost,charterHouse,charterWear,charterKit,charterService,charterProvision,charterResearch,charterCanSell,charterLicense,charterDemand,charterQuote,charterCollect,charterMetrics,charterRequirements,charterPromote,crossingCosts,crossingStages,crossingState,crossingVisit,crossingBuild,crossingTrip,crossingNetwork,districtLayerRoute,districtLayerPathValid,civicFacility,civicFlags,civicLessons,civicPumpOpen,civicService,civicTrailSurvey,civicMaintenance,civicPolicy,civicSetPolicy,civicContractState,civicSetContract,civicRemoveContract,civicCanDispatch,civicDispatch,districtProfile,districtMemory,districtTalk,districtHelp,districtProvision,districtAnchor,districtRoute,districtCargo,districtSend,districtCancel,eastById,eastStages,eastState,eastCost,eastVisit,eastSolve,eastRestore,eastKeys,eastTransfer,eastOrder,eastCancel,eastAdvance,eastDuty,eastDuration,frontierById,frontierNames,frontierCost,frontierState,frontierVisit,frontierBand,frontierSolve,frontierRestore,frontierRecipe,frontierService,frontierDuty,pathCare,pathRegrow,pathSetRegrow,pathNear,pathEngineer,pathMaterials,pathLabels,pathRouteStats,pathSurfaceAt,planLivingCourier,trailVillage,trailRuins,trailBuilt,trailId,trailDecode,trailWear,trailSegment,trailApply,trailStyle,trailNames,tunnelPortals,tunnelLayout,tunnelStages,tunnelState,tunnelOpen,tunnelRects,tunnelWalkable,tunnelRestore,tunnelRead,tunnelCross,tunnelSurvey,reserves,reserveById,reserveState,reservePrepare,reserveSow,reserveWater,reserveSurvey,fieldSpecies,fieldRecord,reserveReward,guildDefs,guildW })(globalThis);

</script>
<script id="lq841-valley-simulation">

(function (root, create) {
  'use strict';
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = create(require('./valley-content.js'), require('./valley-equipment.js'));
  else root.LQValleySimulation = create(root.LQValleyContent, root.LQValleyEquipment);
})(globalThis, function (content, equipment) {
  'use strict';
  const { ID, HOUSES, NODES, COST, QUESTS, REGION, QUARRY } = content;
  const SCHEMA = 7;
  const WORLD = 5;
  const LODGING = Object.freeze({ wood: 3, food: 2 });
  const WAREHOUSE = 'ruined-warehouse';
  const WEATHER = ['mist', 'clear', 'cloudy', 'rain','wind','storm','snow','heat'];
  const SEASONS=['spring','summer','autumn','winter'];
  const seasonAt=clock=>SEASONS[Math.floor((clock.day-1)/12)%4];
  const ASSET_STATES = ['damaged', 'repairing', 'completed', 'operational', 'standing', 'upgraded'];
  const count = value => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
  const plain = value => value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
  const total = world => (world.clock.day - 1) * 1440 + world.clock.minuteOfDay;
  const warehouse = world => world.buildings[WAREHOUSE];
  const ready = world => ['completed', 'operational', 'upgraded'].includes(warehouse(world)?.status);
  const quest = (world, id) => world.quests[id];
  const progress = world => world.quests['a-place-to-begin'].gathered;
  const reached = world => Object.entries(COST).every(([id, amount]) => progress(world)[id] >= amount);
  const hasCost = world => Object.entries(COST).every(([id, amount]) => (world.resources[id] || 0) >= amount);
  const add = (bag, id, amount) => { bag[id] = (bag[id] || 0) + amount; };
  const byId = new Map(NODES.map(node => [node.id, node]));
  const quarryStages=[...QUARRY.stages.map(stage=>stage.id),'exhausted','preserved','restored'];
  const packUpgrades=[
    {wood:8,'copper-ore':2,clay:2}, {stone:8,'ancient-stone':2},
    {wood:18,silverstone:2}, {iron:2,mooncrystal:1},
  ];

  function migrate(world, fresh) {
    if (world.schema === 1) migrateFoundation(world, fresh);
    if(world.schema<4) {
      world.visitor ??= null;
      arrive(world);
    }
    if(world.schema<6)equipment.migrate(world);
    globalThis.LQValleyDevelopment.migrate(world);
    world.ground ??= {next:1,drops:{}};
    world.excavations ??= {};
    world.schema = SCHEMA;
    world.worldVersion = WORLD;
    return world;
  }

  function migrateFoundation(world, fresh) {
    for (const home of HOUSES) {
      const building = world.buildings[home.id] ??= {
        type: home.id === WAREHOUSE ? 'warehouse' : home.id,
        status: home.id === WAREHOUSE ? 'damaged' : 'standing',
        level: 1, position: null,
      };
      // Phase 1 had no authored terrain; explicit positions in older data are retained.
      building.position ??= { region: 'forgotten-hamlet', x: home.x, y: home.y };
    }
    for (const [id] of QUESTS) world.quests[id] ??= {};
    const gathered = quest(world, 'a-place-to-begin').gathered ??= {};
    for (const id of Object.keys(COST)) gathered[id] ??= 0;
    if (fresh) delete world.items['mysterious-letter'];
    world.nodes ??= {};
    world.storage ??= {};
    world.regions ??= {};
    world.opening ??= { seen: false };
    world.campfire ??= { lit: false };
    world.weather ??= { kind: weatherAt(world.clock), observedAt: total(world) };
  }

  function validate(world) {
    if (world.worldVersion !== WORLD || !plain(world.nodes) || !plain(world.storage) || !plain(world.regions) ||
        !plain(world.opening) || typeof world.opening.seen !== 'boolean' || !plain(world.campfire) ||
        typeof world.campfire.lit !== 'boolean' || !plain(world.weather) ||
        !WEATHER.includes(world.weather.kind) || !count(world.weather.observedAt) ||
        (world.weather.previous !== undefined && !WEATHER.includes(world.weather.previous)) ||
        !QUESTS.every(([id]) => plain(world.quests[id])) || !plain(progress(world)) ||
        !Object.keys(COST).every(id => count(progress(world)[id]))) {
      throw new Error('Invalid Valley journey data.');
    }
    const visitor = world.visitor;
    if (visitor !== null && (!plain(visitor) || !count(visitor.arrivedOn) || visitor.arrivedOn < 2)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid Valley visitor.');
    }
    if (world.residents.mara && (!visitor || world.residents.mara.home !== WAREHOUSE ||
        world.residents.mara.job !== 'joiner')) throw new Error('Invalid Valley lodging.');
    const buildings = new Set();
    for (const building of Object.values(world.buildings)) {
      const pos = building.position;
      if (!pos) continue;
      const key = [pos.region, pos.x, pos.y].join('/');
      if (buildings.has(key)) throw new Error('Valley building positions overlap.');
      buildings.add(key);
    }
    if (!HOUSES.every(home => world.buildings[home.id]) || !ASSET_STATES.includes(warehouse(world).status) ||
        (warehouse(world).status === 'repairing' &&
          (!count(warehouse(world).repairStartedAt) || !count(warehouse(world).repairEndsAt) ||
            warehouse(world).repairEndsAt <= warehouse(world).repairStartedAt))) {
      throw new Error('Invalid Valley building state.');
    }
    for (const quantity of Object.values(world.storage)) {
      if (!count(quantity)) throw new Error('Invalid warehouse quantity.');
    }
    for (const [id, node] of Object.entries(world.nodes)) {
      if (!byId.has(id) || !plain(node) || !count(node.remaining) || node.remaining > byId.get(id).stock ||
          (node.respawnAt !== null && !count(node.respawnAt)) ||
          (byId.get(id).kind==='tree'&&node.remaining===0&&(!count(node.harvestedAt)||
            !count(node.respawnAt)||node.respawnAt<=node.harvestedAt))) throw new Error('Invalid resource node.');
    }
    if(!plain(world.excavations) || Object.entries(world.excavations).some(([id,data])=>
      id!==QUARRY.id || !plain(data) || !quarryStages.includes(data.stage) ||
      !count(data.surveyedOn) || data.surveyedOn<1 || data.surveyedOn>world.clock.day)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid Valley excavation.');
    }
    equipment.validate(world);
    validateGround(world);
    globalThis.LQValleyDevelopment.validate(world);
  }

  function weatherAt(clock) {
    const { day, minuteOfDay: minute } = clock;
    if (minute < 570 || minute >= 1290) return 'mist';
    if (day === 1) {
      if (minute >= 850 && minute < 985) return 'rain';
      return minute >= 770 && minute < 1020 ? 'cloudy' : 'clear';
    }
    const period = Math.floor(minute / 170);
    const patterns={spring:['clear','cloudy','rain','wind','storm','clear','rain'],summer:['heat','clear','storm','wind','cloudy','rain','heat'],autumn:['wind','clear','cloudy','rain','mist','wind','clear'],winter:['snow','cloudy','wind','clear','snow','mist','storm']};
    const pattern=patterns[seasonAt(clock)];return pattern[(day*11+period*7+Math.floor(day/3)+period*period)%pattern.length];
  }

  function nodeStock(world, node) {
    const saved = world.nodes[node.id];
    if(node.kind==='tree')return saved?.remaining??node.stock;
    if (!saved || (saved.respawnAt !== null && total(world) >= saved.respawnAt)) return node.stock;
    return saved.remaining;
  }

  function treeStage(world,node) {
    if(node.protected||nodeStock(world,node)>0)return 'mature';
    const saved=world.nodes[node.id],age=total(world)-saved.harvestedAt;
    if(age<75)return 'stump';
    return age<node.regen*.67?'sapling':'young';
  }

  function blocksResource(world,id) {
    if(content.TREES_BY_ID[id]?.protected)return true;
    const def=byId.get(id);
    if(def)return nodeStock(world,def)>0;
    return false;
  }

  function occupiedGrowth(world,node) {
    const edge=content.bounds(content.FEATURES[node.id]);
    const overlaps=(x,y,w,h)=>edge.x<x+w&&edge.x+edge.w>x&&edge.y<y+h&&edge.y+edge.h>y;
    if(HOUSES.some(b=>overlaps(b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h+1)))return true;
    if(Object.values(world.buildings).some(b=>b.position&&overlaps(b.position.x,b.position.y,1,1)))return true;
    return Object.values(world.ground.drops).some(d=>overlaps(d.x/content.TILE-.2,d.y/content.TILE-.2,.4,.4));
  }

  function regrow(world,canRegrow) {
    for(const [id,state] of Object.entries(world.nodes)){
      const node=byId.get(id);
      if(node.kind==='tree'&&!state.remaining&&total(world)>=state.respawnAt&&
          !occupiedGrowth(world,node)&&(!canRegrow||canRegrow(node)))delete world.nodes[id];
    }
  }

  const goodPoint=point=>plain(point)&&count(point.x)&&count(point.y)&&
    point.x>4&&point.y>4&&point.x<content.W*content.TILE-4&&point.y<content.H*content.TILE-4;

  function validateGround(world) {
    const pile=world.ground;
    if(!plain(pile)||!count(pile.next)||pile.next<1||!plain(pile.drops))throw new Error('Invalid ground items.');
    for(const [id,entry] of Object.entries(pile.drops)){
      const n=Number(id.slice(5));
      if(!/^drop-[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(id)||!count(n)||n>=pile.next||!goodPoint(entry)||
          !Object.hasOwn(content.INVENTORY,entry.item)||!count(entry.quantity)||!entry.quantity||
          !count(entry.createdAt)||!Number.isInteger(entry.variant)||entry.variant<0||entry.variant>3||
          typeof entry.credited!=='boolean'||!(byId.has(entry.source)||[QUARRY.id,'backpack'].includes(entry.source))){
        throw new Error('Invalid ground item.');
      }
    }
  }

  function placeDrop(world,item,quantity,point,source,credited) {
    if(!goodPoint(point))throw new Error('Find clear ground for the materials.');
    const {drops}=world.ground;
    const close=Object.values(drops).find(d=>d.item===item&&d.credited===credited&&
      Math.hypot(d.x-point.x,d.y-point.y)<14&&Number.isSafeInteger(d.quantity+quantity));
    if(close){close.quantity+=quantity;return;}
    const sequence=world.ground.next++;
    drops['drop-'+sequence]={item,quantity,x:point.x,y:point.y,source,
      createdAt:total(world),variant:sequence%4,credited};
  }

  function pickup(world,id) {
    const entry=Object.hasOwn(world.ground.drops,id)?world.ground.drops[id]:null;
    if(!entry)throw new Error('That item has already been picked up.');
    const amount=Math.min(entry.quantity,equipment.space(world,entry.item));
    if(!amount)throw new Error('Backpack full. The materials are still here.');
    const key=entry.item;
    if(content.INVENTORY[key][4]==='unique') {
      world.items[key] ??= 1;
      if(key==='ancient-fragment')world.clues['seven-marks'] ??= {
        day:world.clock.day,text:'Seven cuts on a stone older than the hamlet.'};
      if(key==='quarry-tablet')world.clues['quarry-tally'] ??= {day:world.clock.day,
        text:'A tally beneath the iron seam repeats the seventh mark of the Wayfarers’ Arch.'};
    } else {
      add(world.resources,key,amount);
      if(entry.credited&&Object.hasOwn(COST,key))add(progress(world),key,amount);
    }
    entry.quantity-=amount;
    if(!entry.quantity)delete world.ground.drops[id];
    settle(world);
    return {amount,key,left:entry.quantity};
  }

  function dropSupplies(world,key,quantity,point) {
    if(!Object.hasOwn(equipment.limits,key)||!count(quantity)||quantity<1||(world.resources[key]||0)<quantity) {
      throw new Error('That stack cannot be put down.');
    }
    placeDrop(world,key,quantity,point,'backpack',false);
    world.resources[key]-=quantity;
    equipment.reconcile(world);
  }

  function checkGather(world, nodeId) {
    const node = byId.get(nodeId);
    if (!node) throw new Error('Unknown resource.');
    if (node.kind === 'fragment' && !quest(world, 'where-am-i').done) throw new Error('Read the letter and meet Arven first.');
    const remaining = nodeStock(world, node);
    if (!remaining) throw new Error(node.regen ? 'This spot needs time to recover.' : 'You have already taken it.');
    return { node, remaining, tool: equipment.requirement(world, node.action) };
  }

  function gather(world, nodeId, expectedTool, point) {
    const { node, remaining, tool } = checkGather(world, nodeId);
    if (expectedTool !== undefined && expectedTool !== (tool?.id ?? null)) {
      throw new Error('The equipped tool changed.');
    }
    world.nodes[node.id] = { remaining: remaining - 1,
      respawnAt: remaining === 1 && node.regen ? total(world) + node.regen : null };
    if(node.kind==='tree')world.nodes[node.id].harvestedAt=total(world);
    placeDrop(world,node.resource,node.amount,point,node.id,true);
    if (tool) world.equipment.instances[tool.id].durability -= tool.wear;
    return node.amount;
  }

  function quarryStage(world) {
    return world.excavations[QUARRY.id]?.stage ??
      (quest(world,'where-am-i').done?'unmarked':'hidden');
  }

  const openPit = stage => ['revealed','exhausted','preserved'].includes(stage);

  function surveyQuarry(world) {
    if(quarryStage(world)!=='unmarked')throw new Error('The Silverwater Cut has already been surveyed.');
    world.excavations[QUARRY.id]={stage:'marked',surveyedOn:world.clock.day};
  }

  function checkExcavation(world) {
    const current=quarryStage(world);
    const stage=QUARRY.stages.find(step=>step.id===current);
    if(!stage)throw new Error('There is no material to excavate here.');
    return {stage,tool:equipment.requirement(world,stage.action)};
  }

  function excavate(world, expectedStage, expectedTool, point) {
    const {stage,tool}=checkExcavation(world);
    if(stage.id!==expectedStage||tool.id!==expectedTool)throw new Error('The excavation or equipped tool changed.');
    const next=quarryStages[quarryStages.indexOf(stage.id)+1];
    world.excavations[QUARRY.id].stage=next;
    world.equipment.instances[tool.id].durability-=tool.wear;
    if(stage.resource) {
      placeDrop(world,stage.resource,stage.amount,point,QUARRY.id,true);
    }
    if(next==='exhausted') {
      if(!world.items['quarry-tablet'])placeDrop(world,'quarry-tablet',1,point,QUARRY.id,true);
    }
    return {stage:next,resource:stage.resource,amount:stage.amount};
  }

  function finishQuarry(world, mode) {
    const current=quarryStage(world);
    if(mode==='preserved'&&current==='exhausted' ||
        mode==='restored'&&(current==='exhausted'||current==='preserved')) {
      world.excavations[QUARRY.id].stage=mode;
      return;
    }
    throw new Error('Finish extracting the deposit before changing the site.');
  }

  function remember(world, person, key) {
    const memory = world.memories[person] ??= {};
    memory[key] ??= world.clock.day;
    const relationship = world.relationships[person] ??= {};
    const old = Number.isFinite(relationship.trust) ? relationship.trust : 0;
    relationship.trust = Math.min(100, old + 4);
  }

  function react(world, action, canRegrow) {
    switch (action) {
      case 'inspect-well':
        quest(world, 'where-am-i').well = true;
        break;
      case 'take-letter':
        if (!world.items['mysterious-letter']) world.items['mysterious-letter'] = 1;
        quest(world, 'where-am-i').letter = true;
        world.clues['letter-warning'] ??= { day: world.clock.day, text: 'A warning about the seventh bell.' };
        break;
      case 'meet-arven':
        if (!quest(world, 'where-am-i').arven) remember(world, 'arven', 'first-meeting');
        quest(world, 'where-am-i').arven = true;
        break;
      case 'meet-lyra':
        if (!quest(world, 'the-herbalist').lyra) remember(world, 'lyra', 'herbalist-lesson');
        quest(world, 'the-herbalist').lyra = true;
        break;
      case 'repair':
        if (warehouse(world).status !== 'damaged') throw new Error('The warehouse is already being restored.');
        if (!quest(world, 'the-herbalist').done) throw new Error('Finish Lyra’s first lesson.');
        if (!hasCost(world)) throw new Error('Bring 20 wood, 10 stone and 8 food.');
        for (const [id, amount] of Object.entries(COST)) world.resources[id] -= amount;
        warehouse(world).status = 'repairing';
        warehouse(world).repairStartedAt = total(world);
        warehouse(world).repairEndsAt = total(world) + 25;
        remember(world, 'arven', 'started-repair');
        break;
      case 'open-warehouse':
        if (!ready(world)) throw new Error('The warehouse must be repaired first.');
        if (warehouse(world).status === 'completed') warehouse(world).status = 'operational';
        break;
      case 'light-fire':
        if (!quest(world, 'restore-the-warehouse').done) throw new Error('Finish the warehouse first.');
        if (!world.campfire.lit) {
          if ((world.resources.wood || 0) < 2) throw new Error('Find two pieces of wood for the hearth.');
          world.resources.wood -= 2;
          world.campfire.lit = true;
        }
        break;
      case 'wait-for-dusk':
        if (!world.campfire.lit) throw new Error('Light the fire first.');
        step(world, Math.max(0, 1140 - world.clock.minuteOfDay),canRegrow);
        break;
      case 'hear-the-bell':
        if (!world.campfire.lit || !isDark(world.clock)) throw new Error('Wait by the fire until dusk.');
        if (!quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark').heard) {
          quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark').heard = true;
          world.events['first-echo'] = { day: world.clock.day, minute: world.clock.minuteOfDay };
          world.clues['the-echo'] = { day: world.clock.day, text: 'A bell from the highlands. Arven recognized it.' };
          remember(world, 'arven', 'heard-first-echo');
        }
        break;
      case 'rest':
        if (!quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark').heard) throw new Error('Speak with Arven by the fire first.');
        quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark').rested = true;
        world.campfire.lit = false;
        step(world, 1440 - world.clock.minuteOfDay + 420,canRegrow);
        break;
      case 'wayfarer':
        world.clues['wayfarer-stone'] ??= {
          day: world.clock.day,
          text: 'Two marks on the toll arch match the chalk signs on the old warehouse.',
        };
        break;
      case 'lodge-mara':
        lodge(world);
        break;
      default:
        throw new Error('Unknown Valley action.');
    }
    settle(world);
    equipment.reconcile(world);
  }

  function arrive(world) {
    if (!world.visitor && world.clock.day >= 2 && quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark')?.done) {
      world.visitor = { arrivedOn: world.clock.day };
    }
  }

  function lodge(world) {
    if (!world.visitor) throw new Error('Nobody has arrived from the south road.');
    if (world.residents.mara) throw new Error('Mara already has a place to stay.');
    if (!ready(world)) throw new Error('Mara needs a dry room in the storehouse.');
    if (!Object.entries(LODGING).every(([key, amount]) => (world.resources[key] || 0) >= amount)) {
      throw new Error('Bring 3 wood and 2 food for a cot and a meal.');
    }
    for (const [key, amount] of Object.entries(LODGING)) world.resources[key] -= amount;
    world.residents.mara = { home: WAREHOUSE, job: 'joiner' };
    remember(world, 'mara', 'offered-lodging');
  }

  function settle(world) {
    const start = quest(world, 'where-am-i');
    if (start.well && start.letter && start.arven) start.done = true;
    const supply = quest(world, 'a-place-to-begin');
    if (start.done && reached(world)) supply.done = true;
    const lesson = quest(world, 'the-herbalist');
    if (supply.done && lesson.lyra &&
        (world.resources.herbs || 0) >= 1 && (world.resources.water || 0) >= 1) lesson.done = true;
    const repair = quest(world, 'restore-the-warehouse');
    if (lesson.done && ready(world)) repair.done = true;
    const evening = quest(world, 'a-light-in-the-dark');
    if (repair.done && evening.heard && evening.rested && !evening.done) {
      evening.done = true;
      add(world.resources, 'copper', 5);
      world.story.mysteryPressure = Math.max(world.story.mysteryPressure, 10);
      world.town.trust = Math.min(100, world.town.trust + 5);
    }
    arrive(world);
  }

  function transfer(world, key, quantity, toWarehouse) {
    if (!ready(world)) throw new Error('Repair the warehouse first.');
    if (!Object.hasOwn(equipment.limits,key) || !count(quantity) || quantity < 1) {
      throw new Error('This item cannot be moved to the warehouse.');
    }
    const from = toWarehouse ? world.resources : world.storage;
    const to = toWarehouse ? world.storage : world.resources;
    if ((from[key] || 0) < quantity) throw new Error('There is not enough stock.');
    if (toWarehouse && Object.values(world.storage).reduce((sum, item) => sum + item, 0) + quantity > 120) {
      throw new Error('The warehouse is full.');
    }
    if(!toWarehouse&&equipment.space(world,key)<quantity)throw new Error('The backpack has no room for that amount.');
    from[key] -= quantity;
    add(to, key, quantity);
    equipment.reconcile(world);
    if (warehouse(world).status === 'completed') warehouse(world).status = 'operational';
  }

  function transferAll(world,key,toWarehouse) {
    const available=(toWarehouse?world.resources:world.storage)[key]||0;
    const free=toWarehouse?120-Object.values(world.storage).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0):equipment.space(world,key);
    const quantity=Math.max(0,Math.min(available,free));
    if(!quantity)throw new Error('There is no room to move that stock.');
    transfer(world,key,quantity,toWarehouse);
  }

  function upgradePack(world) {
    const recipe=packUpgrades[world.backpack.tier-1];
    if(!recipe||!ready(world))throw new Error('Lyra can upgrade your pack once the warehouse is repaired.');
    if(!Object.entries(recipe).every(([key,n])=>(world.resources[key]||0)>=n))throw new Error('Bring the listed materials to Lyra.');
    for(const [key,n] of Object.entries(recipe))world.resources[key]-=n;
    world.backpack.tier++;
    equipment.reconcile(world);
  }

  function step(world, minutes, canRegrow, manualId) {
    if (!count(minutes)) throw new Error('Invalid clock advance.');
    const priorTime=total(world);
    const clock = world.clock;
    const end = clock.minuteOfDay + minutes;
    const days = Math.floor(end / 1440);
    clock.day += days;
    clock.minuteOfDay = end % 1440;
    if (days > 0) {
      const pop = Object.keys(world.residents).length;
      const pantry = world.resources.food || 0;
      const stores = world.storage.food || 0;
      const need = days * pop;
      world.resources.food = Math.max(0, pantry - need);
      if (stores) world.storage.food = Math.max(0, stores - Math.max(0, need - pantry));
      const shortfall = Math.max(0, need - pantry - stores);
      world.town.foodSupply = Math.min(100, Math.floor((world.resources.food + (world.storage.food || 0)) * 30 / Math.max(1, pop)));
      world.town.morale = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, world.town.morale + (shortfall ? -4 : 2)));
    }
    const storehouse = warehouse(world);
    if (storehouse.status === 'repairing' && total(world) >= storehouse.repairEndsAt) {
      storehouse.status = 'completed';
      world.town.prosperity = Math.min(100, world.town.prosperity + 8);
    }
    const kind = weatherAt(clock);
    if (kind !== world.weather.kind) world.weather = { kind, previous: world.weather.kind, observedAt: total(world) };
    regrow(world,canRegrow);
    globalThis.LQValleyDevelopment.tick(world,priorTime,weatherAt,manualId);
    settle(world);
    equipment.reconcile(world);
  }

  const isDark = clock => clock.minuteOfDay >= 1120 || clock.minuteOfDay < 380;
  function phase(clock) {
    const minute = clock.minuteOfDay;
    return minute < 380 || minute >= 1310 ? 'night' : minute < 630 ? 'morning' :
      minute < 1020 ? 'day' : 'dusk';
  }

  function objective(world) {
    const current = QUESTS.find(([id]) => !quest(world, id).done);
    if (!current) {
      const waiting=world.visitor&&!world.residents.mara;
      return { title: 'A new morning', detail: waiting ? 'Speak to Mara where the south road enters the hamlet.' :
        'Explore the valley. The warehouse can hold shared supplies.' };
    }
    const [id, title, instruction] = current;
    if (id === 'where-am-i') {
      const first = quest(world, id);
      const detail = !first.well ? 'Inspect the old well north of where you woke.' :
        !first.letter ? 'Pick up the folded letter just southwest of the well.' : 'Talk to Arven near the well or his house.';
      return { id, title, detail };
    }
    if (id === 'a-place-to-begin') {
      const stock = progress(world);
      return { id, title, detail: 'Gather ' + Object.entries(COST).map(([key, needed]) =>
        Math.min(stock[key], needed) + '/' + needed + ' ' + key).join(' · ') };
    }
    if (id === 'restore-the-warehouse' && warehouse(world).status === 'repairing') {
      return { id, title, detail: 'Repairs underway · ' + Math.max(0, warehouse(world).repairEndsAt - total(world)) + ' min left.' };
    }
    if (id === 'a-light-in-the-dark') {
      const detail = quest(world, id).heard ? 'Rest at the campfire to begin the next day.' :
        !world.campfire.lit ? 'Light the campfire south of Lyra’s House with 2 wood.' :
        isDark(world.clock) ? 'Listen to Arven beside the fire.' : 'Wait for dusk beside the fire.';
      return { id, title, detail };
    }
    return { id, title, detail: instruction };
  }

  function discover(world, region) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(REGION, region)) world.regions[region] ??= { day: world.clock.day };
  }

  return { model: { id: ID, schema: SCHEMA, legacySchemas: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], needsRecovery: w => w?.schema === SCHEMA && w.worldVersion === WORLD && (!Object.hasOwn(w,'development')||!Object.hasOwn(w,'labor')), migrate, validate },
    LODGING, total, weatherAt, seasonAt, SEASONS, WEATHER, nodeStock, checkGather, gather, blocksResource, treeStage,
    pickup, dropSupplies, upgradePack, packUpgrades, transferAll,
    quarryStage, openPit, surveyQuarry, checkExcavation, excavate, finishQuarry,
    react, transfer, step, isDark, phase, objective, ready, discover };
});

</script>
<script id="lq82-forgotten-valley">

(function (root, factory) {
  'use strict';
  const { createWorldStateManager } = factory();
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) {
    module.exports = { createWorldStateManager };
  } else {
    root.LQForgottenValley = createWorldStateManager(() => root.__lifequestGetScene?.(), root.LQValleySimulation?.model);
  }
})(globalThis, function () {
  'use strict';

  const AREA_ID = 'english-town';
  const SAVE_FIELD = 'forgottenValley';
  const SCHEMA = 1;
  const INITIAL_TOWN = Object.freeze({
    foodSupply: 100, communityHealth: 80, morale: 65, security: 50,
    cleanliness: 65, prosperity: 20, trust: 50, stability: 60,
  });

  function newWorld() {
    return {
      schema: SCHEMA,
      clock: { day: 1, minuteOfDay: 480 },
      resources: { wood: 10, stone: 5, food: 12, copper: 20 },
      items: { 'basic-tools': 1, journal: 1, 'mysterious-letter': 1 },
      residents: {
        player: { job: null, home: null },
        arven: { job: 'historian', home: 'arven-home' },
        lyra: { job: 'herbalist', home: 'lyra-home' },
      },
      buildings: {
        'arven-home': { type: 'arven-home', level: 1, status: 'standing', position: null },
        'lyra-home': { type: 'lyra-home', level: 1, status: 'standing', position: null },
        'ruined-warehouse': { type: 'warehouse', level: 1, status: 'damaged', position: null },
      },
      town: { ...INITIAL_TOWN },
      relationships: {},
      memories: {},
      quests: {},
      events: {},
      clues: {},
      story: { chapter: 1, branch: null, decisions: {}, mysteryPressure: 0 },
    };
  }

  const record = value => value !== null && typeof value === 'object' &&
    [Object.prototype, null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(value));
  const isId = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(value);
  const optionalId = value => value === null || isId(value);
  const count = value => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
  const positive = value => count(value) && value > 0;
  const rating = value => Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 100;
  const dictionary = (value, validEntry) => record(value) &&
    Object.entries(value).every(([id, entry]) => isId(id) && validEntry(entry));
  const position = value => value === null ||
    (record(value) && isId(value.region) && count(value.x) && count(value.y));

  function validate(state, schema = SCHEMA) {
    if (!record(state) || !positive(state.schema)) throw new Error('Invalid Forgotten Valley save.');
    if (state.schema !== schema) throw new Error('Unsupported Forgotten Valley save version.');
    const { clock, resources, items, residents, buildings, town, story } = state;
    if (!record(clock) || !positive(clock.day) || !count(clock.minuteOfDay) || clock.minuteOfDay >= 1440 ||
        !dictionary(resources, count) || !dictionary(items, count) ||
        !dictionary(residents, entry => record(entry) && optionalId(entry.job) && optionalId(entry.home)) ||
        !dictionary(buildings, entry => record(entry) && isId(entry.type) && positive(entry.level) &&
          isId(entry.status) && position(entry.position)) ||
        !record(town) || !Object.keys(INITIAL_TOWN).every(key => rating(town[key])) ||
        !['relationships', 'memories', 'quests', 'events', 'clues'].every(key => dictionary(state[key], record)) ||
        !record(story) || !positive(story.chapter) || !optionalId(story.branch) ||
        !dictionary(story.decisions, isId) || !rating(story.mysteryPressure)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid Forgotten Valley save.');
    }
    for (const { home } of Object.values(residents)) {
      if (home !== null && !Object.hasOwn(buildings, home)) throw new Error('Invalid resident home.');
    }
  }

  function copy(state) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state, (_key, value) => {
      if (value === null || typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'boolean' ||
          Number.isFinite(value) || record(value) || Array.isArray(value)) return value;
      throw new TypeError('Forgotten Valley state must contain JSON data.');
    }));
  }

  function createWorldStateManager(getScene, model) {
    const inProgress = new WeakSet();
    const failures = new WeakMap();
    const areaId = model?.id ?? AREA_ID;
    const schema = model?.schema ?? SCHEMA;

    function townStore(scene) {
      const store = scene?.store;
      if (scene?.area?.id !== areaId || store?.game?.area !== areaId || store.frozen) return null;
      return store;
    }

    function activeStore() {
      const scene = getScene();
      return scene?.switching ? null : townStore(scene);
    }

    // Recovery is explicitly scoped to missing fields. Existing malformed data is rejected.
    const needsMigration = original => !!model && (
      model.legacySchemas?.includes(original?.schema) ||
      (!model.legacySchemas && original?.schema === SCHEMA) ||
      model.needsRecovery?.(original) === true);

    function prepare(original, fresh = false) {
      if (needsMigration(original)) {
        validate(original, original.schema);
        const before = copy(original);
        const next = model.migrate(copy(original), fresh);
        validate(next, schema);
        model.validate(next);
        // For schema 6+ only schema/world version and missing development/ground/excavations may change.
        if (original.schema >= 6) {
          for (const key of Object.keys(before)) {
            if (key === 'schema' || key === 'worldVersion') continue;
            if (JSON.stringify(before[key]) !== JSON.stringify(next[key])) {
              throw new Error('Migration would change existing Valley data: ' + key);
            }
          }
        }
        return next;
      }
      validate(original, schema);
      model?.validate(original);
      return copy(original);
    }

    function read(store) {
      if (failures.has(store)) throw failures.get(store);
      const fresh = !Object.hasOwn(store.game, SAVE_FIELD);
      const original = fresh ? newWorld() : store.game[SAVE_FIELD];
      return prepare(original, fresh);
    }

    function backupEntries(entries, storage = globalThis.localStorage) {
      if (!storage) throw new Error('Recovery storage unavailable. Export progress before retrying.');
      const keyPrefix = 'lq-valley-recovery-v1-';
      const raw = JSON.stringify({format:'LQ_VALLEY_RECOVERY_V1', entries});
      // Deduplicate byte-identical snapshots. Never overwrite a different snapshot.
      let hash = 2166136261;
      for (let i=0;i<raw.length;i++) hash = Math.imul(hash ^ raw.charCodeAt(i),16777619);
      const stem = keyPrefix + (hash>>>0).toString(16) + '-' + raw.length;
      for (let suffix=0;suffix<100;suffix++) {
        const key=stem+(suffix?'-'+suffix:'');
        const prior=storage.getItem(key);
        if(prior===raw)return key;
        if(prior!==null)continue;
        storage.setItem(key,raw);
        if(storage.getItem(key)!==raw)throw new Error('Could not verify recovery backup.');
        return key;
      }
      throw new Error('Recovery backup name collision.');
    }
    function backupStore(store) {
      // Preserve entire game state (including location outside the Valley object).
      // No settings or credentials are copied into the recovery snapshot.
      const raw=JSON.stringify({game:store.game,mem:store.mem || {}});
      return backupEntries([['english-town-save-v1',raw]]);
    }
    function prepareGame(game) {
      const next = copy(game);
      if (!record(next)) throw new Error('Invalid game save.');
      if (Object.hasOwn(next,SAVE_FIELD)) next[SAVE_FIELD] = prepare(next[SAVE_FIELD]);
      return next;
    }
    function prepareImport(entries, storage = globalThis.localStorage) {
      if (!storage || !Array.isArray(entries)) throw new Error('Save storage unavailable.');
      const normalized=entries.map(([key,raw])=>{
        if(key!=='english-town-save-v1')return [key,raw];
        const data=JSON.parse(raw);
        if(!record(data)||!record(data.game)||!['day','money','rep','minutes'].every(k=>Number.isFinite(data.game[k])))throw new Error('Invalid game backup.');
        data.game=prepareGame(data.game);
        return [key,JSON.stringify(data)];
      });
      if(new Set(normalized.map(([k])=>k)).size!==normalized.length)throw new Error('Duplicate backup entry.');
      const previous=normalized.map(([k])=>[k,storage.getItem(k)]);
      if(previous.some(([,v])=>v!==null))backupEntries(previous,storage);
      const written=[];
      try {
        for(const [key,raw] of normalized){
          written.push(key);storage.setItem(key,raw);
          if(storage.getItem(key)!==raw)throw new Error('Could not verify imported data.');
        }
      }catch(error){
        let restored=true;
        for(const [key,raw] of previous.filter(([k])=>written.includes(k)).reverse()){
          try{if(storage.getItem(key)!==raw){if(raw===null)storage.removeItem(key);else storage.setItem(key,raw);}if(storage.getItem(key)!==raw)restored=false;}catch{restored=false;}
        }
        throw new Error(restored?'Import failed; previous progress retained: '+error.message:'Import failed and rollback could not be verified. Use the recovery backup.');
      }
    }
    function recoveryList(storage=globalThis.localStorage){
      const keys=[];
      if(!storage)return keys;
      for(let i=0;i<storage.length;i++){const key=storage.key(i);if(key?.startsWith('lq-valley-recovery-v1-'))keys.push(key);}
      return keys.sort();
    }
    function recoveryExport(key,storage=globalThis.localStorage){
      if(!recoveryList(storage).includes(key))throw new Error('Recovery snapshot not found.');
      const raw=storage.getItem(key),data=JSON.parse(raw);
      if(data.format!=='LQ_VALLEY_RECOVERY_V1'||!Array.isArray(data.entries))throw new Error('Invalid recovery snapshot.');
      // Export in the existing Progress backup input format. Null entries mean the key was absent.
      return JSON.stringify({format:'LIFEQUEST_V8_BACKUP',data:Object.fromEntries(data.entries.filter(([,v])=>v!==null))},null,2);
    }

    function commit(store, next) {
      const game = store.game;
      const existed = Object.hasOwn(game, SAVE_FIELD);
      const previous = game[SAVE_FIELD];
      game[SAVE_FIELD] = next;
      try {
        if (store.save(true) !== true) throw new Error('Could not save Forgotten Valley progress.');
      } catch (error) {
        if (existed) game[SAVE_FIELD] = previous;
        else delete game[SAVE_FIELD];
        throw error;
      }
    }

    return Object.freeze({
      enter(scene) {
        const store = townStore(scene);
        if (!store) return false;
        failures.delete(store);
        try {
          const fresh = !Object.hasOwn(store.game, SAVE_FIELD);
          const original = fresh ? newWorld() : store.game[SAVE_FIELD];
          if (!fresh && needsMigration(original)) backupStore(store);
          const next = prepare(original, fresh);
          // Re-entering a valid current save must not write, migrate, or reset it.
          if (fresh || needsMigration(original)) commit(store,next);
          return true;
        } catch (error) {
          failures.set(store,error);
          console.warn('[Forgotten Valley]', error.message);
          return false;
        }
      },
      prepareGame, prepareImport, recoveryList, recoveryExport,
      getState() {
        const store = activeStore();
        return store ? read(store) : null;
      },
      readScene(scene) {
        const store = townStore(scene);
        return store ? read(store) : null;
      },
      update(change) {
        const store = activeStore();
        if (!store) throw new Error('Forgotten Valley is not active.');
        if (inProgress.has(store)) throw new Error('Forgotten Valley update already in progress.');
        inProgress.add(store);
        try {
          const draft = read(store);
          const result = change(draft);
          if (typeof result?.then === 'function') {
            Promise.resolve(result).catch(() => {});
            throw new TypeError('Forgotten Valley updates must be synchronous.');
          }
          if (activeStore() !== store) throw new Error('Forgotten Valley is not active.');
          const next = copy(draft);
          validate(next, schema);
          model?.validate(next);
          if(!Object.hasOwn(store.game[SAVE_FIELD]?.development||{}, "construction102")&&Object.hasOwn(next.development||{}, "construction102"))backupStore(store);
          commit(store, next);
        } finally {
          inProgress.delete(store);
        }
      },
    });
  }

  return { createWorldStateManager };
});

</script>

<script id="lq841-valley-art">

(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const C = root.LQValleyContent;
  const { W, H, TILE: T, HOUSES, POINTS, TREES_BY_ID, ROCKS, TRAILS, riverAt, center } = C;
  const dirt = ['#786f53', '#8c7958', '#9f8861', '#a88d63'];
  function random(seed) {
    return () => {
      seed = (Math.imul(1664525, seed) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
      return seed / 4294967296;
    };
  }
  const pick = (a, rnd) => a[Math.floor(rnd() * a.length)];
  function box(ctx, color, x, y, w, h) {
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(x), Math.floor(y), Math.ceil(w), Math.ceil(h));
  }
  function shape(ctx, color, points) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
      const [x, y] = points[i];
      if (i) ctx.lineTo(Math.round(x), Math.round(y));
      else ctx.moveTo(Math.round(x), Math.round(y));
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    if (color) {
      ctx.fillStyle = color;
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  function line(ctx, color, x, y, x2, y2, width = 2) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx.lineWidth = width;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(Math.floor(x), Math.floor(y));
    ctx.lineTo(Math.floor(x2), Math.floor(y2));
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  function shadow(ctx, x, y, w, h) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#152b2a48';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, y, w, h, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  function texture(scene, key, w, h, paint) {
    const name = 'valley-v841-' + key;
    if (scene.textures.exists(name)) return name;
    const tex = scene.textures.createCanvas(name, w, h);
    const ctx = tex.getContext();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    paint(ctx);
    tex.refresh();
    tex.setFilter(1);
    return name;
  }

  // V102.7: render selected environmental textures at twice the linear texel density.
  // World coordinates, sprite origin, pathfinding and collision remain in 32-unit tiles.
  function detailScale(scene,w,h){
    const low=typeof location!=='undefined'&&new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('detail')==='1';
    const renderer=scene.game?.renderer||scene.sys?.game?.renderer,gl=renderer?.gl;
    const max=gl?gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE):(renderer?.config?.maxTextureSize||4096);
    return !low&&Math.max(w,h)*2<=max?2:1;
  }
  function detailPaint(ctx,scale,paint){ctx.save();ctx.scale(scale,scale);ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;ctx.__lqDetailScale=scale;try{paint(ctx);}finally{delete ctx.__lqDetailScale;ctx.restore();}}
  function detailTexture(scene,key,w,h,paint){const d=detailScale(scene,w,h),name='valley-v841-hd'+d+'-'+key;if(scene.textures.exists(name))return name;const t=scene.textures.createCanvas(name,w*d,h*d);detailPaint(t.getContext(),d,paint);t.refresh();t.setFilter(1);return name;}
  function detailFactor(key){return key.startsWith('valley-v841-hd2-')?2:1;}
  function fineRect(ctx,c,x,y,w,h){ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.fillRect(Math.round(x*2)/2,Math.round(y*2)/2,Math.max(.5,Math.round(w*2)/2),Math.max(.5,Math.round(h*2)/2));}
  // Authored micro-clusters: distinct moss blades, stone chips, banks and gravel.
  // At most five marks per 8x8 logical cell; no expensive full-image postprocess.
  function detailGround(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water,bank){
    if(ctx.__lqDetailScale!==2)return;
    const n=C.hash(x,y,102701),m=C.hash(x,y,102702),px=x+1+Math.floor(n*10)/2,py=y+1+Math.floor(m*10)/2;
    const R=(c,X,Y,W,H)=>fineRect(ctx,rgb(c),X,Y,W,H),dark=col.map(v=>v-10),light=col.map(v=>v+12),mid=col.map((v,i)=>v+(i===1?8:3));
    if(water){if(bank<.3&&n>.40){R(blend(col,[167,195,165],.34),px,py,2,.5);R(blend(col,[40,78,80],.25),px+.5,py+.5,2,.5);}else if(n>.9)R(blend(col,[137,189,188],.32),x+1,y+3,3.5,.5);return;}
    if(road&&road.distance<road.width+.3){if(n>.55&&road.distance<road.width*.75){R(dark,px,py+.5,1.5,.5);R(light,px,py,1,.5);}return;}
    const rocky=(weights.ridge||0)+(weights.frost||0)+(weights.coppervale||0)>.45;
    if(n>.48){if(rocky){R(dark,px,py+1,2.5,1);R(mid,px+.5,py+.5,1.5,1);R(light,px+.5,py,1,.5);}else{R(dark,px,py+2,2.5,.5);R(mid,px,py+.5,.5,1.5);R(light,px+.5,py,.5,1.5);R(mid,px+1.5,py+1,.5,1.5);}}else if(n<.16){R(dark,px,py,1.5,.5);R(mid,px+.5,py-.5,1,.5);}
  }
  function legacyMicro(ctx,x,y,palette,r){if(ctx.__lqDetailScale!==2)return;const n=C.hash(x,y,102703);if(n<.46)return;const a=x+1+Math.floor(n*8)/2,b=y+1+Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,102704)*8)/2;fineRect(ctx,palette[Math.max(0,r-1)],a,b+1,2,.5);fineRect(ctx,palette[Math.min(palette.length-1,r+1)],a+.5,b,.5,1.5);fineRect(ctx,palette[r],a+1.5,b+.5,.5,1);}
  function detailFloor(ctx,l,id){if(ctx.__lqDetailScale!==2)return;const seed=String(id).split('').reduce((a,c)=>a+c.charCodeAt(0),0);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.16;for(let y=68;y<l.h*T-32;y+=8)for(let x=12;x<l.w*T-12;x+=12){const n=C.hash(x,y,seed+102710);if(n<.68)continue;fineRect(ctx,'#f0d9a6',x+.5,y+.5,2.5,.5);fineRect(ctx,'#263e37',x+2,y+1.5,3,.5);}ctx.restore();}

  function pathStroke(ctx, steps, width, color) {
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx.lineWidth = width;
    ctx.beginPath();
    const points = steps.map(([x,y]) => center({x,y}));
    ctx.moveTo(points[0].x, points[0].y);
    for(let i=1;i<points.length-1;i++) {
      const p=points[i], next=points[i+1];
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x,p.y,(p.x+next.x)/2,(p.y+next.y)/2);
    }
    ctx.lineTo(points.at(-1).x,points.at(-1).y);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  const surfaces = {
    forest: ['#344f41','#3c5845','#435e46','#496347','#50674b'],
    meadow: ['#456549','#4f6e4b','#55754f','#5c7a51','#637e52'],
    scrub: ['#485b4e','#4f624f','#566951','#5b6d54','#62755a'],
    scree: ['#5a6857','#697361','#747b67','#7a8069','#858671'],
    moss: ['#3f5743','#4b6148','#556b4c','#5e724f','#667753'],
    shale: ['#49534d','#565d51','#62675a','#6a6e5f','#777966'],
    riverbank: ['#666c54','#74745a','#81795f','#898060','#968267'],
    water: ['#3a6564'],
  };
  const surfaceSamples = [[0,0],[.22,.12],[-.22,.15],[.17,-.21],[-.14,-.18]];
  // A separate cached native-pixel trail surface. Use maximum wear at junctions,
  // not repeated translucent circles, to avoid artificial bead-like intersections.
  function trailVisualRoad(x,y){return root.LQValleyDevelopment.trailBuilt(x,y)?C.landscapeRoad(x,y):null;}
  const TRAIL_CHUNK=256,TRAIL_PIXEL=2;
  function paintTrailChunk(ctx,cx,cy,list){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,N=TRAIL_CHUNK/TRAIL_PIXEL,ox=cx*TRAIL_CHUNK,oy=cy*TRAIL_CHUNK,alpha=new Float32Array(N*N),rank=new Uint8Array(N*N),depth=new Float32Array(N*N),colors=new Uint8ClampedArray(N*N*3),surface=new Uint8Array(N*N);ctx.clearRect(0,0,TRAIL_CHUNK,TRAIL_CHUNK);
    function stamp(px,py,a,l,ratio,col,road=0){const x=Math.floor((px-ox)/TRAIL_PIXEL),y=Math.floor((py-oy)/TRAIL_PIXEL);if(x<0||y<0||x>=N||y>=N||a<.015)return;const i=y*N+x;if(road<surface[i]||road===surface[i]&&a<=alpha[i])return;surface[i]=road;alpha[i]=a;rank[i]=l;depth[i]=ratio;colors.set(col,i*3);}
    for(const[p,l,road=0]of list){const s=D.trailStyle(l),mx=(p.x+p.bx)/2,my=(p.y+p.by)/2,tx=mx/T,ty=my/T,weights=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty);let col=mixField(weights,k=>roadPal[k]);if(C.legacy(tx,ty))col=blend(col,[153,134,99],.5);col=blend(col,[147,121,84],.21);const dx=p.bx-p.x,dy=p.by-p.y,den=dx*dx+dy*dy;
      if(l<=3){const angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx),n=l+1;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const t=(i+.5)/n,side=(i%2?1:-1)*2,px=Math.round(p.x+dx*t-Math.sin(angle)*side),py=Math.round(p.y+dy*t+Math.cos(angle)*side);stamp(px,py,s.alpha,l,0,col);stamp(px+1,py+2,s.alpha*.84,l,0,col);if(l>=2)stamp(px-2,py,s.alpha*.45,l,.6,col);}continue;}
      const radius=s.width*.52+road*2.4,margin=radius+5;for(let py=Math.max(oy,Math.floor((Math.min(p.y,p.by)-margin)/2)*2);py<Math.min(oy+TRAIL_CHUNK,Math.max(p.y,p.by)+margin);py+=2)for(let px=Math.max(ox,Math.floor((Math.min(p.x,p.bx)-margin)/2)*2);px<Math.min(ox+TRAIL_CHUNK,Math.max(p.x,p.bx)+margin);px+=2){const x=px+1,y=py+1,u=clamp01(((x-p.x)*dx+(y-p.y)*dy)/den),dist=Math.hypot(x-p.x-u*dx,y-p.y-u*dy),edge=(wildNoise(x,y,15,9837)-.5)*5+(C.hash(px,py,9751)-.5)*1.7,w=radius+edge;const fade=smooth((w+2.5-dist)/4.5),ratio=clamp01(dist/Math.max(1,w));const wear=(road?.98:s.alpha)*fade*(.94+.06*C.hash(px,py,4511));stamp(px,py,wear,l,ratio,col,road);}}
    for(let i=0;i<alpha.length;i++){if(alpha[i]<.015)continue;const x=ox+(i%N)*2,y=oy+Math.floor(i/N)*2,l=rank[i],r=depth[i],n=C.hash(x,y,9988),m=wildNoise(x,y,18,9989);let col=Array.from(colors.subarray(i*3,i*3+3));const edge=smooth((r-.55)/.45),grain=(m-.5)*18+(n-.5)*11;col=col.map(v=>v+grain-edge*25+(l-10)*.55);let a=alpha[i];
      // Remaining grass survives near irregular edges and disappears with repeated use.
      const style=D.trailStyle(l),grass=style.grass;
      if(l>=4&&n<grass*(.31+edge*.26))a*=.17+style.compaction*.2;
      if(l>=7){const packed=style.compaction*(1-edge)*(.65+.35*m);col=col.map(v=>v+packed*9);}
      if(l>=10&&n>.96-style.gravel*.035){col=col.map(v=>v+14+style.gravel*14);a*=.93;}
      if(l>=12&&n<.035+style.gravel*.035){col=col.map(v=>v-14-style.gravel*9);}
      if(l>=14){const worn=style.rut*Math.exp(-Math.pow((r-.40)/.17,2))*(.65+.35*m);col=col.map(v=>v-worn*10);}
      if(l>=17&&n>.987){col=col.map(v=>v+22);}if(surface[i]){const stage=surface[i],border=r>.76,grit=C.hash(x,y,7643);if(stage===1){col=blend(col,[145,135,113],.55);if(grit>.78)col=blend(col,[208,198,172],.68);if(grit<.13)col=blend(col,[73,76,67],.65);if(border)col=blend(col,[86,83,63],.34);}else{const gx=Math.floor(x/9),gy=Math.floor(y/8);let best=Infinity,second=Infinity,dx=0,dy=0,tone=0;for(let j=-1;j<=1;j++)for(let k=-1;k<=1;k++){const cx=gx+k,cy=gy+j,px=cx*9+3+C.hash(cx,cy,8261)*4,py=cy*8+2+C.hash(cx,cy,8262)*4,xx=x-px,yy=y-py,d=xx*xx+yy*yy*1.25;if(d<best){second=best;best=d;dx=xx;dy=yy;tone=C.hash(cx,cy,8263);}else if(d<second)second=d;}const joint=second-best<12,highlight=dy<0&&dx<1?9:0,shadow=dy>2?7:0;col=joint?blend([78,85,74],[105,111,94],grit*.5):[126+tone*31+highlight-shadow,133+tone*29+highlight-shadow,120+tone*26+highlight-shadow];const crack=C.hash(x,y,8264);if(!joint&&crack<.12)col=col.map(v=>v-8);if(border)col=blend(col,[94,91,70],.4);}a=alpha[i];}ctx.globalAlpha=a;box(ctx,rgb(col),x-ox,y-oy,2,2);}
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  function removeTrailChunk(view,key){const v=view.trailLayer,b=v?.chunks.get(key);if(!b)return;const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(b.sprite);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(b.sprite.scene)b.sprite.destroy();if(view.scene.textures.exists(b.name))view.scene.textures.remove(b.name);v.chunks.delete(key);}
  function clearTrailLayer(view){if(!view?.trailLayer)return;for(const key of [...view.trailLayer.chunks.keys()])removeTrailChunk(view,key);view.trailLayer=null;}
  function trailLayer(scene,view,w){if(scene.inside){clearTrailLayer(view);return;}const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,source=w.development.desirePaths||null,roads=w.development.pathCare?.roads||null,r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,cell=TRAIL_CHUNK,cx=Math.floor(r.x/cell),cy=Math.floor(r.y/cell),ex=Math.floor(r.right/cell),ey=Math.floor(r.bottom/cell);let v=view.trailLayer;if(!v)v=view.trailLayer={source:null,index:new Map(),chunks:new Map(),sig:''};
    if(source!==v.source||roads!==v.roads){v.source=source;v.roads=roads;v.index=new Map();for(const [id,n]of Object.entries(source||{})){const p=D.trailDecode(id);if(!p)continue;for(let y=Math.floor((Math.min(p.y,p.by)-24)/cell);y<=Math.floor((Math.max(p.y,p.by)+24)/cell);y++)for(let x=Math.floor((Math.min(p.x,p.bx)-24)/cell);x<=Math.floor((Math.max(p.x,p.bx)+24)/cell);x++){const k=x+','+y;(v.index.get(k)||v.index.set(k,[]).get(k)).push([p,n,roads?.[id]||0]);}}}
    const wanted=new Set();for(let y=cy-1;y<=ey+1;y++)for(let x=cx-1;x<=ex+1;x++){const k=x+','+y,list=v.index.get(k);if(!list?.length)continue;wanted.add(k);const sig=list.map(([p,n,road])=>p.id+':'+n+':'+road).join(',');let b=v.chunks.get(k);if(b?.sig===sig)continue;if(!b){const name='valley-desire-wear-'+k;const tex=scene.textures.createCanvas(name,cell,cell);tex.setFilter(1);const sprite=scene.add.image(x*cell,y*cell,name).setOrigin(0).setDepth(.08);scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);b={tex,sprite,name,sig:''};v.chunks.set(k,b);}paintTrailChunk(b.tex.getContext(),x,y,list);b.tex.refresh();b.sig=sig;}
    for(const k of [...v.chunks.keys()])if(!wanted.has(k))removeTrailChunk(view,k);
  }

  function ground(ctx) {
    const rnd = random(994);
    const width = C.WorldConfig.baseWidth * T;
    const height = C.WorldConfig.baseHeight * T;
    for (let y = 0; y < height; y += 8) {
      for (let x = 0; x < width; x += 8) {
        const [dx,dy]=pick(surfaceSamples,rnd);
        const {surface}=C.groundSample(x/T+dx,y/T+dy);
        const palette=surfaces[surface];
        const drift=Math.sin(x/62+y/85)*.65+Math.sin(y/122-x/113);
        const color=Math.max(0,Math.min(palette.length-1,Math.floor(2.4+drift*.65+(rnd()-.5)*.58)));
        box(ctx,palette[color],x,y,8,8);legacyMicro(ctx,x,y,palette,color);
      }
    }

    ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(16*T,17*T,23*T,22*T);ctx.rect(24*T,39*T,4*T,7*T);ctx.rect(38*T,22*T,6*T,4*T);for(const [x,y,r]of root.LQValleyDevelopment.trailRuins){ctx.moveTo((x+r)*T,y*T);ctx.arc(x*T,y*T,r*T,0,Math.PI*2);}ctx.clip();
    for (const [i, trail] of TRAILS.entries()) {
      const width = i < 2 ? 50 : 24;
      const highland=i===5;
      pathStroke(ctx, trail, width + 15, highland?'#596458':'#5a694d');
      pathStroke(ctx, trail, width + 5, highland?'#666f60':dirt[0]);
      pathStroke(ctx, trail, width, highland?'#7b7d68':dirt[1]);
      pathStroke(ctx, trail, width * .66, highland?'#888872':dirt[2]);
      pathStroke(ctx, trail, width * .32, highland?'#908d74':dirt[3]);
      // Break up the edges of each trail with overlapping chips of its local ground.
      for(let j=0;j<trail.length-1;j++){
        const a=center({x:trail[j][0],y:trail[j][1]}),b=center({x:trail[j+1][0],y:trail[j+1][1]});
        const dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y,length=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
        for(let d=10;d<length-9;d+=7+rnd()*11){
          const edge=(width/2+3+rnd()*8)*(rnd()<.5?-1:1);
          const x=a.x+dx*d/length-dy*edge/length,y=a.y+dy*d/length+dx*edge/length;
          const colors=surfaces[C.groundSample(x/T,y/T).surface];
          box(ctx,pick(colors,rnd),x,y,2+rnd()*5,2+rnd()*3);
        }
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
    hamletGround(ctx);
    ctx.globalAlpha = .52;
    for (let i = 0; i < 12500; i++) {
