      const x = Math.floor(rnd() * width / 2) * 2;
      const y = Math.floor(rnd() * height / 2) * 2;
      box(ctx, pick(['#31483d', '#7b8d60', '#b29b68', '#d2bb85', '#4a5b42', '#756651'], rnd),
        x, y, 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 5), 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 3));
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    river(ctx, rnd);
    for (const rock of ROCKS) {
      if(rock.solid) talus(ctx,rock);
      else ruinsWall(ctx,rock,rnd);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < 860; i++) {
      const x = 20 + rnd() * (width - 40);
      const y = 86 + rnd() * (height - 116);
      const [left, right] = C.shore(y/T);
      if (x > left * T - 2 && x < right * T + 2) continue;
      const withinHamlet = x > 545 && x < 1160 && y > 390 && y < 925;
      if (withinHamlet && (i % 5 || Math.abs(x - 833) < 108 || Math.abs(y - 780) < 43)) continue;
      const wet = Math.min(Math.abs(x - left * T), Math.abs(x - right * T)) < 30;
      const surface=C.groundSample(x/T,y/T).surface;
      if(['scree','shale'].includes(surface) && i%4!==0)continue;
      plant(ctx, x, y, wet ? 12 + (i % 5) : i % 14, rnd);
    }
    garden(ctx);
    bridge(ctx);
    legacyRoadJoin(ctx);
    legacyTransition(ctx);
    forestGroundFinish(ctx,0,0,width,height,true);
  }

  function hamletGround(ctx) {
    const rng = random(82083);
    const flags = ['#827a60','#8a8065','#a09475','#77735d','#89876b'];
    // The well stands in a worn ring laid by the same masons as the old toll arch.
    const {x:wx,y:wy}=center(POINTS.well);
    for(let ring=1;ring<3;ring++) {
      const n=9+7*ring,r=24+ring*15;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++) {
        if((i+ring*3)%7===0) continue;
        const a=(i+.33*ring)*Math.PI*2/n,x=wx+Math.cos(a)*r,y=wy+Math.sin(a)*r*.57;
        masonryBlock(ctx,x-8,y-5,12+rng()*8,6+rng()*4,pick(flags,rng));
      }
    }
    // The paved sections are authored along the old freight approach and around door aprons.
    const patches=[[817,659,63,100],[807,522,94,75],[633,719,94,36],[1055,740,48,34],
      [771,935,46,208],[647,968,90,65]];
    for(const [x,y,w,h] of patches) {
      for(let row=0;row<h;row+=9) {
        const edge=Math.sin((y+row)/24)*8;
        for(let col=4;col<w-5;col+=13) {
          if(rng()<.29+Math.abs(col/w-.5)*.9)continue;
          masonryBlock(ctx,x+edge+col+(row%2)*4,y+row,7+rng()*6,4+rng()*3,pick(flags,rng));
        }
      }
    }
    for(const house of HOUSES) {
      const x=house.x*T,y=(house.y+house.h)*T;
      for(let i=0;i<82;i++) {
        const a=x+rng()*house.w*T,b=y-6+rng()*33;
        if(Math.abs(a-(house.door+.5)*T)<30)continue;
        box(ctx,pick(['#475c3f','#536841','#65804a','#7b8451','#746d51'],rng),a,b,3+rng()*9,2+rng()*3);
      }
    }
    for(const [x,y,kind] of [[608,854,0],[714,785,1],[714,987,2],[758,966,1],
      [933,890,0],[1015,806,2],[1086,844,1],[1150,780,2],[885,620,1],[762,663,0]]) {
      for(let i=0;i<9;i++) {
        const a=x+(rng()-.5)*29,b=y+(rng()-.5)*21;
        if(kind===0)fern(ctx,a,b,8+rng()*10,i%2===0);
        else plant(ctx,a,b,kind===1?4+i%4:12+i%5,rng);
      }
    }
  }

  function masonryBlock(ctx,x,y,w,h,color) {
    shape(ctx,'#4d5044',[[x,y+2],[x+3,y],[x+w-2,y],[x+w,y+2],[x+w-2,y+h],[x+1,y+h]]);
    box(ctx,color,x+2,y+1,w-4,h-3);
    box(ctx,'#b1a586',x+3,y+1,Math.max(2,w*.43),1);
  }

  function fern(ctx,x,y,h,bright) {
    const colors=bright?['#507047','#8d9a58']:['#385a40','#6c8250'];
    for(const side of [-1,1])for(let i=0;i<4;i++) {
      const dy=i*h/5;
      line(ctx,colors[0],x,y-dy,x+side*(4+i),y-dy-3,2);
      box(ctx,colors[1],x+side*(4+i),y-dy-5,2,2);
    }
    line(ctx,colors[0],x,y,x+1,y-h,1);
  }

  function river(ctx, rnd) {
    for (let py = 0; py < C.WorldConfig.baseHeight * T; py += 2) {
      const [a,b]=C.shore((py+1)/T);
      const x = a*T,right=b*T;
      const span = right - x;
      const shore=9+Math.round(Math.sin(py/27)*3);
      box(ctx, '#82785e', x - shore, py, span + shore*2, 2);
      box(ctx, '#527c70', x, py, span, 2);
      box(ctx, '#416c70', x + 5, py, span - 10, 2);
      box(ctx, '#316067', x + 23, py, span - 43, 2);
      box(ctx, '#2d535e', x + 49, py, Math.max(4, span - 91), 2);
      if (py % 16 === 0) {
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          const px = x + 2 + rnd() * (span - 10);
          box(ctx, pick(['#5b8076', '#45777b', '#598d8b', '#365f68'], rnd), px, py, 3 + rnd() * 15, 2);
        }
      }
      if (py % 24 === 0) {
        box(ctx, '#a69e72', x - 8, py, 5, 2);
        box(ctx, '#6f9785', right - 8, py + 2, 5, 2);
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 95; i++) {
      const y = 18 + rnd() * (C.WorldConfig.baseHeight * T - 32);
      const [left, right] = C.shore(y/T);
      const x = i % 2 ? left * T + rnd() * 19 : right * T - rnd() * 18;
      box(ctx, '#344f4e', x - 2, y + 2, 6 + rnd() * 7, 4);
      box(ctx, '#779083', x, y, 3 + rnd() * 7, 2);
    }
  }

  function bridge(ctx) {
    const banks = [23,23.5,24.5,25.5,26].map(C.shore);
    const x = Math.min(...banks.map(([left]) => left)) * T - 16;
    const width = Math.max(...banks.map(([, right]) => right)) * T + 16 - x;
    const y = 23 * T;
    box(ctx, '#2b4242', x - 7, y + 10, width + 14, 86);
    box(ctx, '#554635', x, y + 3, width, 87);
    const rnd = random(72);
    for (let dx = 0; dx < width; dx += 8) {
      box(ctx, pick(['#987550', '#a58159', '#b18a62', '#967553'], rnd), x + dx, y + 8, 7, 76);
      box(ctx, '#c5a174', x + dx, y + 11, 1, 69);
      box(ctx, '#604d38', x + dx + 3, y + 19 + rnd() * 48, 2, 4 + rnd() * 11);
    }
    for (const dy of [3, 86]) {
      box(ctx, '#624934', x - 4, y + dy, width + 8, 7);
      box(ctx, '#b38d61', x - 4, y + dy, width + 8, 2);
      for (let j = 1; j * 31 < width; j++) {
        box(ctx, '#473b2d', x + j * 31, y + dy - 6, 7, 14);
        box(ctx, '#ccab75', x + j * 31, y + dy - 6, 7, 3);
        box(ctx, '#999286', x + j * 31 + 2, y + dy - 2, 2, 2);
      }
    }
  }

  function ruinsWall(ctx, rock, rnd) {
    const x = rock.x * T, y = rock.y * T, w = rock.w * T, h = rock.h * T;
    box(ctx,'#444b3e',x,y,w,h);
    const top = [[x, y + 11], [x + 11, y + 2], [x + w * .4, y], [x + w * .79, y + 3],
      [x + w - 1, y + 10], [x + w - 2, y + h - 13], [x + w * .55, y + h - 11],
      [x + w * .22, y + h - 15], [x + 2, y + h - 13]];
    shape(ctx,'#686b58',top);
    for (let ly = y + 15; ly < y + h - 13; ly += 10) {
      for (let lx = x + 3 + (ly % 8); lx < x + w - 8; lx += 12 + rnd() * 16) {
        const rw = Math.min(w + x - lx - 4, 7 + rnd() * 17);
        box(ctx,pick(['#848271','#717765','#9b9278','#566148'],rnd),lx,ly,rw,2+rnd()*6);
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < w / 4; i++) {
      const px = x + rnd() * (w - 7);
      box(ctx, pick(['#385449', '#546949', '#64794b', '#78915a'], rnd),
        px, y + h - 17 + rnd() * 12, 4 + rnd() * 6, 2 + rnd() * 5);
    }
  }

  const geology = {
    granite: {top:'#747b6e', ledges:['#909585','#778071','#63736b'],
      faces:['#4b5755','#636d64','#758070','#414e4b'], fresh:'#a0a38d', talus:'#77816d'},
    limestone: {top:'#959381',ledges:['#b2ab90','#9c9b81','#818775'],
      faces:['#5b645e','#797f6e','#92937e','#555e53'],fresh:'#bcb395',talus:'#93947b'},
    blackstone: {top:'#667176',ledges:['#899393','#727f81','#56666a'],
      faces:['#343e4c','#4b5661','#606b72','#2b3745'],fresh:'#a0a5a1',talus:'#6c7470'},
  };
  const geologySeed = id => [...id].reduce((seed,letter)=>Math.imul(seed,31)+letter.charCodeAt(0),382);
  const interpolate = (a,b,t) => a.map((value,i)=>value+(b[i]-value)*t);

  function chip(ctx,x,y,w,h,stone) {
    shape(ctx,stone.faces[0],[[x-w*.5,y],[x-w*.33,y-h*.7],[x+w*.22,y-h],[x+w*.5,y-h*.25],[x+w*.36,y+2]]);
    shape(ctx,stone.talus,[[x-w*.31,y-h*.65],[x+w*.21,y-h],[x+w*.41,y-h*.28],[x,y-h*.19]]);
    line(ctx,stone.ledges[0],x-w*.26,y-h*.65,x+w*.19,y-h+1,1);
  }

  function talus(ctx,rock) {
    const rnd=random(geologySeed(rock.id));
    const stone=geology[rock.material];
    const p=rock.solid.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]);
    for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){
      const a=p[i],b=p[(i+1)%p.length];
      if(b[0]>a[0]+3)continue;
      const length=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
      const spread=17+rock.rise*4;
      for(let d=0;d<length;d+=10+rnd()*5){
        const [x,y]=interpolate(a,b,d/length);
        shadow(ctx,x+3,y+5,15,9);
        box(ctx,stone.faces[1],x-3,y+2,7+rnd()*10,2+rnd()*3);
        const size=4+rnd()*10;
        chip(ctx,x+(rnd()-.5)*22,y+7+rnd()*spread,size,2+rnd()*size*.45,stone);
        if(rnd()<.33)box(ctx,'#75885a',x-2,y+5+rnd()*12,5+rnd()*5,2);
      }
    }
  }

  function stratum(ctx,rock) {
    const rnd=random(geologySeed(rock.id));
    const stone=geology[rock.material];
    const base=rock.solid.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]);
    const crest=base.map(([x,y],i)=>[x+Math.round((rnd()-.5)*14),
      y-Math.round(T*rock.rise*(.84+rnd()*.26)+(i%3===0?6:0))]);

    // The cap is laid behind the south-facing and oblique exposed faces.
    shape(ctx,stone.faces[0],base);
    ctx.save();
    shape(ctx,stone.top,crest);
    ctx.clip();
    const view=C.bounds(crest);
    for(let i=0;i<view.w*view.h/185;i++){
      const x=view.x+rnd()*view.w,y=view.y+rnd()*view.h;
      const w=3+rnd()*29,h=2+rnd()*8;
      shape(ctx,pick(stone.ledges,rnd),[[x,y],[x+w*.47,y-h*.62],[x+w,y],[x+w*.62,y+h],[x+w*.22,y+h*.8]]);
      if(i%7===0)box(ctx,pick(['#5f754f','#788957','#596d54'],rnd),x,y,4+rnd()*9,3);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<view.h/9;i++){
      const x=view.x+rnd()*view.w,y=view.y+rnd()*view.h,w=13+rnd()*30;
      const k=rock.material==='blackstone'?-.23:.13;
      line(ctx,stone.ledges[2],x,y,x+w*.46,y+k*w-2,1);
      line(ctx,stone.ledges[2],x+w*.46,y+k*w-2,x+w,y+k*w,2);
    }
    ctx.restore();

    for(let i=0;i<base.length;i++){
      const j=(i+1)%base.length, a=base[i],b=base[j],c=crest[j],d=crest[i];
      const dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];
      if(dx>Math.abs(dy)*.09)continue;
      const length=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
      const side=dy>Math.abs(dx)*.75;
      const shade=side?stone.faces[0]:dy<-Math.abs(dx)*.65?stone.faces[2]:stone.faces[1];
      ctx.save();
      shape(ctx,shade,[d,c,b,a]);
      ctx.clip();
      const cut=(t,u)=>interpolate(interpolate(d,a,u),interpolate(c,b,u),t);
      for(let band=0;band<4;band++){
        const level=.17+band*.218;
        const jag=rock.material==='blackstone'?.1:.063;
        const row=Array.from({length:6},(_,k)=>cut(k/5,level+(rnd()-.5)*jag));
        const bottom=row.map(([x,y])=>[x,y+2+(band%2?4:2)]).reverse();
        shape(ctx,stone.faces[band%2?0:3],row.concat(bottom));
        for(let k=0;k<row.length-1;k++){
          const [x,y]=row[k],[xx,yy]=row[k+1];
          line(ctx,stone.faces[2],x,y-2,xx,yy-2,1);
        }
      }
      for(let k=0;k<length/15;k++){
        const t=(k+.7)/(length/15+1),u=.12+rnd()*.67;
        const p=cut(t,u),q=cut(Math.min(1,t+.045),u+.2);
        const step=cut(Math.min(1,t+.06),u+.33);
        line(ctx,stone.faces[3],...p,...q,2);
        line(ctx,stone.faces[3],...q,...step,1);
        if(k%3===0) {
          const [x,y]=cut(t,.84+rnd()*.09);
          shape(ctx,'#455b479f',[[x-8,y+5],[x-5,y-2],[x+2,y-3],[x+9,y+1],[x+6,y+7]]);
          box(ctx,'#728354',x-2,y-2,4,2);
        }
        if(rock.material==='blackstone'&&k%5===1) {
          const [x,y]=cut(t,.41);
          shape(ctx,'#aa8f78',[[x,y],[x+3,y-4],[x+8,y-6],[x+6,y-2]]);
        }
      }
      shape(ctx,'#253b396a',[cut(0,.88),cut(.35,.92),cut(.7,.84),cut(1,.88),b,a]);
      ctx.restore();
      line(ctx,side?stone.ledges[1]:stone.fresh,...d,...c,2);
    }
  }

  const flowers = ['#dcb883', '#b67e81', '#9d8abb', '#becc8e', '#e4ddbb'];
  function plant(ctx, x, y, variant, rnd) {
    const color = pick(['#334f3d', '#668352', '#81905a', '#74864d'], rnd);
    if (variant < 6) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        const dx = 3 * (k - 1);
        line(ctx, color, x, y, x + dx, y - 3 - (variant + k) % 6, 1);
        if (variant > 1) box(ctx, flowers[(variant + k) % flowers.length], x + dx, y - 5 - (variant + k) % 6, 2, 2);
      }
    } else if (variant < 11) {
      const h = 3 + variant % 4;
      box(ctx, '#a18c70', x, y - h, 2, h);
      box(ctx, variant % 2 ? '#b18e68' : '#a97865', x - 2, y - h - 3, 6, 3);
      box(ctx, '#d4b98d', x - 1, y - h - 3, 3, 1);
    } else {
      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
        const dx = 2 * (k - 2), h = 4 + (variant * (k + 1)) % 9;
        line(ctx, color, x, y, x + dx, y - h, 1);
        box(ctx, variant < 14 ? '#879754' : '#a6a76b', x + dx, y - h, 2, 3);
      }
    }
  }

  function garden(ctx) {
    for (let row = 0; row < 3; row++) {
      const x = 1121 + row * 19, y = 747;
      box(ctx, '#493f34', x, y, 16, 49);
      box(ctx, '#705744', x + 2, y + 2, 12, 45);
      for (let dy = 4; dy < 43; dy += 8) {
        box(ctx, '#3f644d', x + 4, y + dy, 7, 6);
        box(ctx, '#a5a765', x + 5, y + dy + 1, 5, 2);
      }
    }
    for (let x = 1480; x < 1606; x += 21) {
      box(ctx, '#615942', x, 885, 15, 103);
      box(ctx, '#78654a', x + 2, 889, 11, 94);
    }
  }

  // Organic foliage lobes instead of scattered rectangular confetti. Shared by the existing
  // tree species and local shrubs; no catalog or collider changes. Every texture is cached.
  function mass(ctx,x,y,r,palette,rnd){
    const p=palette,seed=rnd()*Math.PI*2;
    const outline=[];for(let i=0;i<32;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/16,rad=r*(.85+.07*Math.sin(a*5+seed)+.06*Math.sin(a*9+seed*2)+.055*(rnd()-.5));outline.push([x+Math.cos(a)*rad,y+Math.sin(a)*rad*.78-2]);}
    shape(ctx,p[0],outline);ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,outline);ctx.clip();
    const tuft=(cx,cy,rr,c)=>{const pts=[[cx-rr,cy+1],[cx-rr*.74,cy-rr*.45],[cx-rr*.27,cy-rr*.68],[cx+rr*.32,cy-rr*.64],[cx+rr*.78,cy-rr*.27],[cx+rr,cy+1],[cx+rr*.66,cy+rr*.37],[cx+rr*.20,cy+rr*.52],[cx-rr*.45,cy+rr*.40]];shape(ctx,c,pts);};
    tuft(x-1,y-2,r*.83,p[1]);tuft(x-r*.22,y-r*.26,r*.67,p[2]);
    // Ordered overlapping foliage masses are shaded by their height and lighting direction.
    const lobes=[];for(let i=0;i<Math.max(7,Math.ceil(r*.53));i++){const a=(i*2.399963+seed),d=r*(.18+.59*Math.sqrt(rnd()));const lx=x+Math.cos(a)*d,ly=y+Math.sin(a)*d*.72;lobes.push({x:lx,y:ly,r:r*(.17+.17*rnd()),light:(1-(ly-y)/r)*.55-(lx-x)/r*.24});}
    lobes.sort((a,b)=>a.y-b.y);
    for(const l of lobes){const high=l.light>.64,mid=l.light>.35;tuft(l.x+1,l.y+2,l.r*.99,p[0]);tuft(l.x,l.y,l.r,p[mid?2:1]);tuft(l.x-l.r*.13,l.y-l.r*.19,l.r*.72,p[high?3:mid?2:1]);
      const counts=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(l.r*.7));for(let k=0;k<counts;k++){const dx=(rnd()-.5)*l.r*1.45,dy=(rnd()-.65)*l.r*.95;if(dx*dx+dy*dy>l.r*l.r*.55)continue;const px=Math.round(l.x+dx),py=Math.round(l.y+dy);box(ctx,p[high&&k%4===0?4:high?3:mid?2:1],px,py,Math.max(1,Math.round(l.r*.26)),1);if(k%3===0&&l.r>4)box(ctx,p[2],px+1,py+1,2,1);}
    }
    // A few coherent rim leaves on the lit side; dark curled bottom lobes.
    for(let i=0;i<Math.ceil(r*.48);i++){const a=Math.PI+(i/Math.max(1,Math.ceil(r*.48)-1))*Math.PI,rr=r*(.70+.1*rnd()),px=x+Math.cos(a)*rr,py=y+Math.sin(a)*rr*.75-2;box(ctx,p[i%5===0?4:3],px,py,2+(i%3===0?1:0),1);}
    for(let i=0;i<Math.ceil(r*.35);i++){const a=i*2.399963+seed,px=x+Math.cos(a)*r*Math.sqrt(rnd())*.67,py=y+Math.sin(a)*r*.48;if(py>y-r*.08){box(ctx,p[1],px,py,2,1);if(i%3===0)box(ctx,p[0],px+1,py+1,2,1);}}
    ctx.restore();
  }

  const leaf = [
    ['#294332','#3c5b3c','#416644','#587447','#759354'],
    ['#27443e','#34594a','#416551','#4c7560','#698c68'],
    ['#3b563d','#557145','#67834e','#779556','#9ba262'],
    ['#365548','#517a62','#62886c','#77977a','#98ab85'],
    ['#3a4932','#57633a','#6e7744','#8f8751','#ab9b5e'],
    ['#293e36','#3b5d48','#4f7150','#638459','#829765'],
  ];

  // Explicit species profiles: distinct crown topology, trunk, branch and foliage palettes.
  const newTreeProfiles={
    2:{bark:['#3d463e','#a8aca0','#ded5bb'],palette:['#344d3a','#536c48','#728a52','#8fa263','#b0b777'],trunk:[[-3,-90],[1,-106],[6,-108],[7,-65],[10,-16]],shape:'birch'},
    3:{bark:['#3c483a','#726e48','#a39562'],palette:['#294b3e','#466c50','#67875b','#88a371','#a4bc84'],shape:'willow'},
    6:{bark:['#333e35','#655646','#937c5c'],palette:['#304c45','#426359','#587d68','#769680','#93a88a'],shape:'cedar'},
    7:{bark:['#453830','#765840','#b18155'],palette:['#583b29','#875135','#b2793f','#cb9f51','#e0bb64'],shape:'maple'},
    8:{bark:['#323830','#4b4c38','#777756'],palette:['#293d2e','#3e5438','#59683d','#7a8250','#9a9d63'],shape:'ironwood'},
    9:{bark:['#3b4b48','#8b9b8c','#c9cfc1'],palette:['#364b48','#547167','#74938a','#94b1a5','#bbccc0'],shape:'silverwood'},
    10:{bark:['#393b49','#5a5c66','#93968b'],palette:['#3d405a','#5a607b','#7c8298','#9aa2b1','#c5c9cc'],shape:'moonleaf'},
    11:{bark:['#3d403a','#6b6550','#a69c7d'],palette:['#3e4938','#59634a','#777c53','#93966c','#b4b184'],shape:'dead'},
    12:{bark:['#3c382b','#6c5a39','#a68a57'],palette:['#3b4c2e','#526440','#6e7f4b','#949b5d','#aeb66e'],shape:'hollow'},
    13:{bark:['#394641','#6a7b60','#acaa83'],palette:['#304f43','#4f795d','#70a17b','#93b38b','#adc29b'],shape:'river'},
    14:{bark:['#3e4633','#706547','#a59064'],palette:['#394e2e','#586e3d','#7b8f51','#9ca962','#bac27b'],shape:'fruit'},
  };
  for(const [id,profile] of Object.entries(newTreeProfiles))leaf[Number(id)]=profile.palette;
  function speciesTree(ctx,variant){
    const setting=newTreeProfiles[variant],kind=setting.shape,rnd=random(301+variant*871),p=setting.bark,c=setting.palette,x=65,y=112;
    const branch=(x1,y1,x2,y2,w)=>{line(ctx,p[0],x+x1,y+y1,x+x2,y+y2,w+3);line(ctx,p[1],x+x1,y+y1-1,x+x2,y+y2-1,w);line(ctx,p[2],x+x1-1,y+y1-1,x+x2-1,y+y2-1,Math.max(1,w*.23));};
    const crown=(dx,dy,r)=>mass(ctx,x+dx,y+dy,r,c,rnd);
    shadow(ctx,kind==='river'?x+15:x+8,y+2,kind==='willow'?49:37,10);
    if(kind==='birch'||kind==='silverwood'){
      branch(-7,-3,-1,-94,7);branch(-1,-67,-24,-91,4);branch(0,-71,20,-91,4);branch(2,-40,25,-62,4);
      for(let yy=20;yy<98;yy+=6){box(ctx,p[0],x-5+(yy%5),y-yy,5,2);box(ctx,p[2],x-5,y-yy-2,3,1);}
      const clusters=kind==='birch'?[[-18,-81,17],[0,-93,18],[22,-80,19],[-11,-66,18],[15,-56,17]]:[[-25,-75,18],[-16,-90,18],[12,-94,23],[32,-74,16],[12,-64,19]];
      for(const a of clusters)crown(...a);
      if(kind==='silverwood')for(let i=0;i<12;i++)box(ctx,'#cad9ce',x-29+rnd()*56,y-90+rnd()*27,2,2);
    }else if(kind==='willow'||kind==='river'){
      branch(-10,2,-4,-62,13);branch(-4,-55,-27,-77,7);branch(-3,-51,27,-77,7);branch(1,-37,kind==='river'?36:19,-57,5);
      const center=kind==='river'?10:0;
      for(const a of [[-26,-70,25],[0,-88,24],[26,-71,25],[-7,-60,23],[31,-56,16]])crown(a[0]+center,a[1],a[2]);
      for(let i=0;i<29;i++){
        const xx=-42+i*3,top=-61-Math.floor(Math.sin(i*.3)*8),end=-18-rnd()*25;
        if(kind==='river'&&i<9)continue;
        const x0=x+xx+center;for(let yy=top;yy<end;yy+=5){const drift=Math.sin(yy*.18+i*.72)*2,dx=x0+drift;line(ctx,c[0],dx,y+yy,dx-1,y+yy+5,2);shape(ctx,c[(i+Math.floor(yy))%2+1],[[dx-2,y+yy],[dx+2,y+yy-1],[dx+3,y+yy+2],[dx+1,y+yy+5],[dx-3,y+yy+3]]);box(ctx,c[3],dx-2,y+yy,2,1);}
      }
    }else if(kind==='cedar'){
      branch(-3,0,0,-103,9);
      // Broad horizontal shelves, unlike the triangular pine silhouette.
      for(let i=5;i>=0;i--){const yy=-91+i*13,w=18+i*5,off=i%2?4:-3;shape(ctx,c[0],[[x+off-4,y+yy-13],[x+off+w,y+yy+6],[x+off+w-4,y+yy+13],[x+off-w,y+yy+10],[x+off-w+3,y+yy+3]]);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const dx=off-w+8+j*(w*2-16)/4;crown(dx,yy+5,7+i*.7);}}
    }else if(kind==='dead'){
      branch(-6,1,-1,-98,12);branch(-1,-74,-29,-90,7);branch(-27,-89,-35,-104,4);branch(-1,-57,33,-83,6);branch(28,-80,36,-99,4);branch(-1,-34,-36,-57,7);branch(-34,-55,-41,-43,4);branch(-1,-40,20,-53,5);branch(20,-53,37,-54,3);
      for(let i=0;i<10;i++)box(ctx,c[2],x-10+rnd()*19,y-15+rnd()*11,4,2);
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const xx=x-31+i*9;box(ctx,'#7f7452',xx,y-3,3,6);box(ctx,'#b6a17a',xx-1,y-5,5,3);}
    }else if(kind==='hollow'||kind==='ironwood'){
      branch(-11,2,-4,-55,20);branch(-5,-48,-14,-82,15);branch(-3,-51,22,-84,12);branch(-14,-64,-35,-73,9);branch(10,-53,35,-66,9);
      for(const a of kind==='hollow'?[[-30,-66,22],[-24,-82,23],[5,-91,26],[30,-76,22],[24,-54,21]]:[[-31,-77,25],[-9,-85,25],[19,-85,26],[34,-68,20],[-5,-64,26]])crown(...a);
      if(kind==='hollow'){shape(ctx,'#302d26',[[x-10,y-14],[x-9,y-34],[x-4,y-42],[x+3,y-37],[x+5,y-19],[x+2,y-12]]);line(ctx,p[2],x-10,y-15,x-10,y-32,2);box(ctx,'#534b30',x-4,y-17,7,3);}
      else for(let i=0;i<10;i++)line(ctx,p[2],x-12+i*2,y-5,x-7+i*2,y-26,1);
    }else if(kind==='moonleaf'){
      branch(-4,0,4,-92,8);branch(2,-50,-26,-77,6);branch(4,-62,34,-77,5);branch(-1,-30,-20,-59,4);
      for(const a of [[-25,-76,18],[-12,-91,17],[13,-96,19],[31,-78,16],[-18,-53,16],[12,-62,20]])crown(...a);
      for(let i=0;i<22;i++){const xx=x-32+rnd()*62,yy=y-94+rnd()*38;box(ctx,'#c0c8cb',xx,yy,2,2);if(i%4===0)box(ctx,'#e1dcc7',xx+1,yy-1,1,1);}
    }else{
      const fruit=kind==='fruit';branch(-5,0,0,-67,11);branch(-1,-45,-25,-75,7);branch(0,-52,28,-75,7);branch(-1,-34,-31,-49,6);branch(1,-31,26,-49,5);
      for(const a of fruit?[[-29,-58,20],[-14,-77,23],[16,-79,25],[31,-56,18],[0,-52,23]]:[[-30,-66,24],[-13,-87,27],[14,-85,26],[35,-59,20],[6,-56,24]])crown(...a);
      for(let i=0;i<(fruit?21:12);i++){const xx=x-35+rnd()*70,yy=y-80+rnd()*37;box(ctx,fruit?'#9e5142':'#e1ad58',xx,yy,3,3);box(ctx,fruit?'#d98c65':'#ebc47c',xx,yy,1,1);}
    }
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const dx=(i-2)*7;line(ctx,p[0],x+dx,y+3,x+dx*.45,y-7,2);line(ctx,p[2],x+dx,y+1,x+dx*.45,y-7,1);}
  }

  function treeOriginal(ctx, variant) {
    if(newTreeProfiles[variant])return speciesTree(ctx,variant);
    const old = variant === 5, pine = variant === 1, birch = variant === 3;
    const x = old ? 105 : 65, y = old ? 180 : 112;
    const rnd = random(11 + variant * 887);
    shadow(ctx, x + 9, y - 1, old ? 61 : 41, 12);
    const bark = birch ? ['#444c41', '#c5b69d', '#e7d0ab'] : ['#34342d', '#6a5741', '#947456'];
    shape(ctx, bark[0], [[x - 31,y+7],[x-13,y-9],[x-10,y-45],[x-23,y-66],
      [x-20,y-75],[x-5,y-62],[x-1,y-99],[x+12,y-97],[x+13,y-64],
      [x+31,y-88],[x+35,y-83],[x+19,y-51],[x+11,y-15],[x+32,y+2],[x+21,y+7],[x+3,y],[x-15,y+9]]);
    shape(ctx, bark[1], [[x-9,y-8],[x-7,y-45],[x-2,y-60],[x+1,y-96],[x+8,y-99],
      [x+8,y-57],[x+6,y-43],[x+5,y-12],[x-1,y+1],[x-30,y+8]]);
    for (let j = 0; j < 18; j++) {
      const dy = rnd() * 66;
      box(ctx, j % 3 ? bark[2] : bark[0], x - 6 + rnd() * 13, y - 10 - dy, birch ? 7 : 2, birch ? 2 : 3 + rnd() * 9);
    }
    if(pine){
      // Needle clusters in irregular bough layers, not six identical triangles.
      const p=['#243b34','#35574a','#416a55','#628669','#8aa187'];
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
        const yy=y-99+i*12,r=11+i*6,dx=Math.round((rnd()-.5)*7),cx=x+dx;
        const poly=[[cx-2,yy-8],[cx+5,yy-4],[cx+10,yy+3],[cx+r*.57,yy+5],[cx+r*.72,yy+10],[cx+r,yy+16],[cx+r*.73,yy+16],[cx+r*.45,yy+21],[cx+r*.19,yy+18],[cx,yy+23],[cx-r*.32,yy+17],[cx-r*.62,yy+20],[cx-r,yy+15],[cx-r*.73,yy+11],[cx-r*.44,yy+4],[cx-6,yy+2]];
        shape(ctx,p[0],poly);ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,poly);ctx.clip();
        shape(ctx,p[1],[[cx-3,yy-8],[cx+5,yy+4],[cx+r*.7,yy+14],[cx+r*.32,yy+17],[cx-1,yy+14],[cx-r,yy+14]]);
        shape(ctx,p[2],[[cx-2,yy-6],[cx-5,yy+5],[cx-r*.7,yy+13],[cx-r*.27,yy+12],[cx+1,yy+8]]);
        // Tufts follow descending branches at a different pitch on each side.
        for(let j=0;j<15+i*7;j++){const f=.17+rnd()*.82,px=cx+(rnd()<.5?-1:1)*r*f,py=yy+4+f*9+(rnd()-.5)*8;const color=rnd()<.36?p[3]:rnd()<.5?p[2]:p[0];box(ctx,color,px,py,2+Math.floor(rnd()*5),1+Math.floor(rnd()*2));if(j%5===0){box(ctx,p[4],px,py-1,3,1);box(ctx,p[2],px-2,py+2,3,1);}}
        for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const px=cx+(rnd()-.5)*r*1.45,py=yy+12+rnd()*6;box(ctx,p[0],px,py,2,3);}
        ctx.restore();
      }
    } else {
      const canopy = old ? [[-43,-87,42],[44,-86,43],[-33,-118,47],[30,-113,51],[-3,-121,51],
        [-12,-84,40],[26,-75,37],[-48,-71,32],[48,-67,28]] :
        [[-22,-64,29],[23,-70,30],[-15,-85,31],[16,-86,28],[-4,-67,32],[-27,-52,23],[23,-54,22]];
      for (const [dx, dy, r] of canopy) mass(ctx, x + dx, y + dy, r, leaf[variant], rnd);
    }
    if (old) {
      const runes = ['#c3bc73', '#8aa66a', '#e5d795'];
      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) box(ctx, runes[i % 3], x - 7 + i * 2, y - 31 + (i % 2) * 3, 1, 5);
      box(ctx, '#628970', x - 12, y - 26, 6, 4);
    }
  }

  // Each site has a composed, irregular contour, a ring of protected planting beds,
  // central access and style-specific flora. No solid obstacle is invented by the art.
  const reservePal={fern:['#314d3b','#52764b','#809e5d','#b4c78b'],meadow:['#3e5734','#6e8a45','#a4b661','#e4ce81'],rock:['#46544b','#667a50','#8b9f6a','#d2cf93'],reed:['#344d43','#5e805a','#98ad70','#c9c088'],alpine:['#546866','#739180','#aec2a7','#e5ddd1'],glade:['#385342','#6d8d5c','#98b570','#dec99c']};
  const bedShapes={fern:[[-59,-36],[-37,-51],[1,-47],[33,-54],[66,-30],[74,-7],[61,20],[39,30],[4,22],[-38,30],[-70,12],[-76,-12]],meadow:[[-74,-23],[-61,-43],[-24,-52],[16,-40],[53,-46],[82,-22],[73,12],[49,37],[13,30],[-20,37],[-63,22]],rock:[[-73,-14],[-52,-45],[-22,-41],[5,-57],[47,-47],[76,-19],[70,7],[43,34],[11,24],[-29,38],[-64,20]],reed:[[-65,-35],[-36,-50],[2,-43],[31,-55],[66,-27],[76,5],[58,32],[32,34],[-8,25],[-50,37],[-77,6]],alpine:[[-64,-28],[-37,-48],[-5,-57],[27,-42],[52,-40],[77,-14],[64,25],[32,29],[-8,24],[-48,35],[-77,9]],glade:[[-75,-30],[-45,-49],[-12,-47],[20,-54],[64,-36],[77,-6],[60,23],[34,37],[-5,29],[-40,38],[-70,13]]};
  function reservePlant(ctx,x,y,style,stage,rng){const p=reservePal[style],small=stage===2;shadow(ctx,x+1,y+1,small?4:9,3);
    if(style==='fern'){const len=small?5:11+rng()*5;for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=-Math.PI+.15+i*.55,dx=Math.cos(a)*len,dy=Math.sin(a)*len;line(ctx,p[0],x,y,x+dx,y+dy,2);line(ctx,p[1],x,y-1,x+dx,y+dy,1);for(let k=2;k<=5;k++){const f=k/6,xx=x+dx*f,yy=y+dy*f,r=(1-f)*len*.28;line(ctx,p[2],xx-r,yy-1,xx+r,yy-1,1);if(!small)box(ctx,p[3],xx-1,yy-2,2,1);}}}
    else if(style==='reed'){for(let i=0;i<(small?3:7);i++){const xx=x-7+i*2,hh=(small?5:12)+rng()*8,dx=(rng()-.5)*8;line(ctx,p[0],xx,y,xx+dx,y-hh,2);line(ctx,p[2],xx+1,y,xx+dx,y-hh,1);if(!small&&i%2===0){box(ctx,'#6e5940',xx+dx-1,y-hh-4,3,6);box(ctx,'#c6b18b',xx+dx-1,y-hh-4,1,3);}}}
    else if(style==='rock'||style==='alpine'){const r=small?4:8+rng()*4;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const dx=(i-1.5)*r*.6;mass(ctx,x+dx,y-(i%2)*3,r*.55,[p[0],p[1],p[2],p[3], '#f0e7c9'],rng);}if(!small){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const xx=x-5+rng()*10,yy=y-2-rng()*5;box(ctx,style==='alpine'?'#e6d0d9':'#e6c385',xx,yy,3,2);box(ctx,'#f9e9ba',xx+1,yy,1,1);}}}
    else{const h=small?3:6+rng()*8;for(let i=0;i<(small?3:7);i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*15,yy=y+(rng()-.5)*7,ht=h*(.6+rng()*.4);line(ctx,p[0],xx,yy,xx+1,yy-ht,2);line(ctx,p[2],xx-1,yy-2,xx-4,yy-ht*.55,1);if(!small){const c=pick(style==='glade'?['#d4b4db','#a9b5d8','#f4dec3']:['#f0d86e','#e6a298','#e8eabd'],rng);box(ctx,c,xx-1,yy-ht-1,4,3);box(ctx,c,xx,yy-ht-2,2,5);box(ctx,'#9b7845',xx+1,yy-ht,1,1);}}}}
  function reservePaint(ctx,r,stage){const x=96,y=74,p=reservePal[r.style],rng=random(9981+r.x*43+r.y*811),pts=bedShapes[r.style].map(([a,b])=>[a+x,b+y]);const fill=stage===0?'#76694f':stage===1?'#716148':stage===2?'#66754e':p[1];
    shape(ctx,'#263f3533',pts.map(([a,b])=>[a+3,b+5]));shape(ctx,fill,pts);ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,pts);ctx.clip();
    for(let i=0;i<250;i++){const xx=18+rng()*157,yy=17+rng()*105;const a=stage===0?['#8f7f5c','#665b48','#9c8e6b']:stage===1?['#867455','#5f503d','#9d8860']:[p[0],p[1],p[2]];box(ctx,pick(a,rng)+'80',xx,yy,2+rng()*7,1+rng()*3);}
    // distinct conservation layout, two planted wings and an open diagonal passage.
    const beds=r.style==='reed'?[[-48,-13],[-30,-27],[-7,-35],[25,-27],[48,-12],[-39,17],[36,16]]:r.style==='rock'||r.style==='alpine'?[[-53,-12],[-26,-31],[12,-34],[48,-19],[-38,16],[24,19],[52,7]]:[[-53,-14],[-29,-33],[1,-36],[30,-32],[54,-16],[-42,14],[-19,23],[33,19],[53,8]];
    if(stage===0){for(const [a,b] of beds){const xx=x+a,yy=y+b;wildPatch(ctx,xx,yy,'stone',[p[0],p[1],p[2],p[3]],rng);if(rng()>.5)line(ctx,'#776a4d',xx-5,yy+5,xx+4,yy+8,2);}}
    else for(const [a,b] of beds){const xx=x+a,yy=y+b;shape(ctx,'#51493855',[[xx-12,yy],[xx-8,yy-5],[xx+8,yy-5],[xx+12,yy],[xx+8,yy+5],[xx-8,yy+5]]);if(stage===1){for(let j=-7;j<=7;j+=5){line(ctx,'#ab9870',xx+j,yy-3,xx+j+3,yy+2,1);}}else{reservePlant(ctx,xx,yy,r.style,stage,rng);if(stage===3)reservePlant(ctx,xx-7,yy+4,r.style,stage,rng);}}
    ctx.restore();
    // Low retaining stones, intentionally discontinuous around access; not a wall.
    for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[(i+1)%pts.length],d=Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1]);for(let f=.1;f<.9;f+=.22){if((i===6||i===7)&&f>.2)continue;const xx=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f,yy=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f;if(stage>0)masonryBlock(ctx,xx-3,yy-1,7+rng()*5,4,pick(['#918c75','#737b69','#a6a18a'],rng));else if(rng()>.64)box(ctx,'#85775c',xx,yy,4,2);}}
    // Central low survey marker, owns the interaction affordance, not an invisible collider.
    shadow(ctx,x,y+3,12,5);shape(ctx,'#655c44',[[x-10,y+2],[x-8,y-8],[x+6,y-8],[x+10,y+2]]);box(ctx,'#b1a587',x-7,y-8,13,3);box(ctx,stage===3?'#729c65':stage===2?'#cebc79':'#8a8d7b',x-5,y-4,9,4);box(ctx,'#e8dcb0',x-2,y-3,4,1);
  }
  function reserveApproach(ctx,r){const p=r.path,points=p.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]);const loX=Math.floor(Math.min(...points.map(v=>v[0]))/3)*3-12,hiX=Math.max(...points.map(v=>v[0]))+12,loY=Math.floor(Math.min(...points.map(v=>v[1]))/3)*3-12,hiY=Math.max(...points.map(v=>v[1]))+12;for(let y=loY;y<=hiY;y+=3)for(let x=loX;x<=hiX;x+=3){let best=Infinity;for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++){const a=points[i-1],b=points[i],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],t=clamp01(((x-a[0])*dx+(y-a[1])*dy)/(dx*dx+dy*dy||1)),d=Math.hypot(x-a[0]-dx*t,y-a[1]-dy*t);best=Math.min(best,d);}if(Math.hypot(x-r.x*T,y-r.y*T)>58)continue;const road=trailVisualRoad(x/T,y/T),merge=road?smooth((road.distance-road.width*.55)/.65):1,f=smooth((10.5-best+(C.hash(x,y,801)-.5)*3)/4)*merge;if(f<.03)continue;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=f*.81;box(ctx,best<4?'#ab9670':'#968762',x,y,3,3);if(best<6&&C.hash(x,y,802)>.87){ctx.globalAlpha=f*.54;box(ctx,'#d2bf91',x+1,y+1,2,1);}ctx.restore();}}
  function reserveViews(scene,view,w,t,quality,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,cache=view.reserveSprites??=new Map(),visible=new Set();for(const r of D.reserves){const x=r.x*T,y=r.y*T;if(scene.inside||x<rect.x-230||x>rect.right+230||y<rect.y-230||y>rect.bottom+230)continue;visible.add(r.id);let v=cache.get(r.id);const stage=D.reserveState(w,r.id).stage,key=texture(scene,'reserve-'+r.id+'-'+stage,192,148,c=>reservePaint(c,r,stage));if(!v){const a=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,.5).setDepth(2);scene.areaObjs.push(a);v={a};cache.set(r.id,v);}if(v.a.texture.key!==key)v.a.setTexture(key);v.a.setVisible(true);
      if(stage>=2&&!reduced){const p=reservePal[r.style],col=parseInt(p[2].slice(1),16),bright=parseInt(p[3].slice(1),16),f=stage===3?1:.5;for(let i=0;i<(quality.ambient>=12?14:7);i++){const dx=Math.sin(i*2.399963)*47,dy=Math.cos(i*2.399963)*30,phase=t*1.9+i*.9,xx=x+dx,yy=y+dy; if(Math.abs(dx)<14&&Math.abs(dy)<14)continue;const d=Math.round(Math.sin(phase)*(w.weather.kind==='rain'?3:1));view.nearFog.lineStyle(1,col,.68).lineBetween(xx,yy,xx+d,yy-5*f);view.nearFog.fillStyle(bright,.75).fillRect(Math.round(xx+d),Math.round(yy-5*f),2,1);}}
      if(stage===3&&w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170&&['fern','reed','glade'].includes(r.style)&&!reduced){for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const f=(t*.16+i*.21)%1,xx=x+Math.cos(f*6.28+i)*42,yy=y+Math.sin(f*6.28+i)*23-8;view.light.fillStyle(0xd8eb8e,.08*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillCircle(xx,yy,7);view.light.fillStyle(0xf1edaf,.65*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy),2,1);}}}
    for(const [id,v] of cache)if(!visible.has(id))v.a.setVisible(false);
  }
  function releaseReserves(view){if(!view?.reserveSprites)return;for(const {a} of view.reserveSprites.values()){const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(a);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(a.scene)a.destroy();}view.reserveSprites.clear();}
  // Broad ground refinement: natural coherent color patches in the legacy region,
  // rather than a checker of per-cell disconnected random tones. Subtle, world anchored.
  function groundCluster(ctx,x,y,p,rng){const xx=Math.floor(x),yy=Math.floor(y),len=5+rng()*6;for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const dx=(j-1)*3;line(ctx,p[0],xx+dx,yy,xx+dx+(j-1)*3,yy-len*(.6+rng()*.4),1);box(ctx,p[1],xx+dx,yy-3,2,1);}}
  function forestGroundFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h,legacy=false){const rng=random(9811),pal=['#68834d','#93a267'];for(let y=Math.floor(oy/28)*28;y<oy+h;y+=28)for(let x=Math.floor(ox/28)*28;x<ox+w;x+=28){const dx=C.hash(x,y,912)*18,dy=C.hash(x,y,913)*18,px=x+dx,py=y+dy,tx=px/T,ty=py/T;if(px<ox||py<oy||px>=ox+w||py>=oy+h)continue;if((trailVisualRoad(tx,ty)?.distance??Infinity)<2.0||C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty)||C.groundSample(tx,ty).surface==='water'||C.hash(x,y,916)>.46)continue;const reg=C.wildernessRegion(tx,ty);if(legacy){let avoid=false;for(const r of TRAILS)for(let i=1;i<r.length;i++){const a=r[i-1],b=r[i],vx=b[0]-a[0],vy=b[1]-a[1],d=clamp01(((tx-a[0]-.5)*vx+(ty-a[1]-.5)*vy)/(vx*vx+vy*vy||1));if(Math.hypot(tx-a[0]-.5-d*vx,ty-a[1]-.5-d*vy)<1.5)avoid=true;}if(avoid)continue;}
    if(C.hash(x,y,917)>.55&&reg!=='frost'&&reg!=='ridge'){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.66;groundCluster(ctx,px,py,reg==='marsh'?['#4c7055','#9eac74']:pal,rng);ctx.restore();}}
  }
  // Broad canopy/wood grain refinement for all original tree species. The source
  // silhouette, shape and texture size are retained; only visible trunk pixels change.
  function treeWoodFinish(ctx,variant){const W=variant===5?210:130,H=variant===5?194:126,im=ctx.getImageData(0,0,W,H),a=im.data,y0=variant===5?180:112,x0=W/2;for(let y=Math.max(0,y0-65);y<Math.min(H,y0+9);y++)for(let x=Math.max(0,x0-34);x<Math.min(W,x0+35);x++){const i=(y*W+x)*4,R=a[i],G=a[i+1],B=a[i+2];if(a[i+3]<220||R<G+4||R<B+11||G<B+3||R<45||R>200)continue;const phase=(x+Math.sin(y*.21+(variant+1)*.8)*2+Math.sin(y*.07)*3)*1.6,n=C.hash(x,y,531+variant),grain=Math.sin(phase),k=grain>.73?12:grain<-.65?-15:n>.88?7:-2;const lit=(x-x0)<-4?4:-3;a[i]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,R+k+lit));a[i+1]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,G+k*.8+lit));a[i+2]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,B+k*.6+lit));}ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);}

  function tree(ctx,variant){treeOriginal(ctx,variant);treeWoodFinish(ctx,variant);}
  function shutters(ctx, x, y, w, ambient = false) {
    box(ctx, '#2c2a27', x - 4,y-3,w+8,26);
    box(ctx, '#ab8364', x - 4,y-3,w+8,2);
    box(ctx, ambient ? '#eca969' : '#668682', x,y,w,18);
    box(ctx, ambient ? '#ffdc9b' : '#a5bca1', x+2,y+2,w-4,4);
    box(ctx, '#4a443b', x+w/2-1,y,2,18);
    box(ctx, '#4a443b', x,y+8,w,2);
    box(ctx, '#c19870', x-5,y+20,w+10,3);
  }
  function planks(ctx, x, y, w, h, palette, rnd) {
    box(ctx, palette[0], x,y,w,h);
    for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy += 7) {
      box(ctx, '#322e29', x,y+dy+6,w,1);
      box(ctx, pick(palette, rnd), x,y+dy+1,w,5);
      for (let dx = 4; dx < w - 6; dx += 11 + rnd() * 15)
        box(ctx, pick(palette, rnd), x+dx,y+dy+3, 3 + rnd() * 10,1);
    }
  }
  function roof(ctx, polygon, palette, rnd) {
    const minX = Math.min(...polygon.map(p => p[0]));
    const maxX = Math.max(...polygon.map(p => p[0]));
    const minY = Math.min(...polygon.map(p => p[1]));
    const maxY = Math.max(...polygon.map(p => p[1]));
    shape(ctx, palette[0], polygon);
    ctx.save();
    shape(ctx, null, polygon);
    ctx.clip();
    for (let row=0,y=minY; y<maxY; row++,y+=7) {
      for (let x=minX-10+(row%2)*6; x<maxX;) {
        const w=8+Math.floor(rnd()*8),dy=rnd()<.19?1:0;
        box(ctx,pick(palette,rnd),x,y+dy,w-1,6);
        box(ctx,rnd()<.33?palette[2]:palette[3],x,y+dy,w-2,1);
        box(ctx,palette[0],x+1,y+5+dy,w-2,1);
        if(rnd()<.21)box(ctx,palette[4],x+2,y+2,3+rnd()*4,1);
        if(rnd()<.08)box(ctx,palette[0],x+w-3,y+4,2,2);
        x+=w;
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
    for (let i = 0; i < polygon.length; i++) {
      const [x,y] = polygon[i], [a,b] = polygon[(i+1) % polygon.length];
      line(ctx, i === 0 || i === 1 ? palette[3] : palette[0], x,y,a,b,3);
    }
  }
  const slate = ['#353a4a','#4a5366','#626978','#818593','#ac9d97'];
  const cedar = ['#343f31','#4d533b','#67644a','#898061','#a29473'];
  const barn = ['#3b312c','#654636','#8d5d43','#a77856','#be9569'];
  const woods = ['#755343','#805941','#956a4a','#ac7d54'];

  function door(ctx, x, y, wide = 29, damaged = false) {
    box(ctx, '#665243', x - wide/2 - 4, y - 46, wide + 8, 46);
    box(ctx, '#322b2a', x - wide/2, y - 43, wide, 42);
    for (let dx = -wide/2+4; dx < wide/2-2; dx += 5) {
      box(ctx, damaged ? '#564335' : '#8a684d', x+dx,y-39,3,36);
    }
    line(ctx, '#342d29', x-wide/2+4,y-7, x+wide/2-4,y-36,3);
    box(ctx, '#ccae74', x+wide/2-8,y-22,3,3);
    box(ctx, '#a88d69', x-wide/2-6,y,wide+12,4);
    box(ctx, '#736750', x-wide/2-9,y+4,wide+18,4);
  }
  function chimney(ctx, x, y) {
    box(ctx, '#554440', x - 2,y,20,34);
    box(ctx, '#8b6961', x,y+1,13,32);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      box(ctx, '#ba8d74', x + (i%2)*3,y+4+i*7,9,1);
      box(ctx, '#493d3b', x,y+7+i*7,17,1);
    }
    box(ctx, '#383336', x-5,y-4,23,6);
    box(ctx, '#ad8570', x-5,y-4,23,2);
  }

  function stonework(ctx,x,y,w,h,rng) {
    const tones=['#6b6c5e','#8c8772','#9f977f','#797e6e','#737264'];
    box(ctx,'#53584d',x,y,w,h);
    for(let row=0;row<h-3;row+=8)for(let a=0;a<w-5;) {
      const width=Math.min(w-a,8+Math.floor(rng()*14));
      masonryBlock(ctx,x+a,y+row+(a%3===0?1:0),width-1,7,pick(tones,rng));
      a+=width;
    }
  }

  function moss(ctx,x,y,width,rng) {
    for(let i=0;i<width/2;i++) {
      const a=rng()*width,b=(rng()+Math.cos(a/9)*.3)*11;
      box(ctx,pick(['#42533a','#596342','#69764b','#899461'],rng),x+a,y+b,2+rng()*6,2+rng()*3);
    }
  }

  function weathering(ctx,x,y,w,h,rng) {
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++) {
      const a=x+rng()*w;
      box(ctx,'#38342b46',a,y,2+rng()*4,7+rng()*h);
      box(ctx,'#939c7753',a+1,y+2,1,4+rng()*h*.62);
    }
  }

  function crate(ctx,x,y,color='#805d43') {
    shadow(ctx,x+12,y+16,14,5);
    shape(ctx,'#4e4034',[[x,y+3],[x+4,y],[x+20,y],[x+24,y+4],[x+24,y+18],[x,y+18]]);
    box(ctx,color,x+1,y+5,22,11);
    box(ctx,'#b59668',x+3,y+1,17,3);
    box(ctx,'#b18c61',x+2,y+6,2,10);box(ctx,'#5c4834',x+17,y+6,3,12);
    line(ctx,'#563e30',x+4,y+14,x+16,y+6,1);
    box(ctx,'#d4bf89',x+10,y+8,4,2);
  }

  function vine(ctx,x,y,span,rng) {
    let last=x;
    for(let i=0;i<span;i+=5) {
      const a=x+Math.sin(i*.18)*5;
      line(ctx,'#55593a',last,y+i-5,a,y+i,1);
      const dx=i%2?4:-4;
      shape(ctx,pick(['#566a40','#7b8750','#8f9357'],rng),[[a,y+i],[a+dx,y+i-3],[a+dx*1.5,y+i],[a+dx,y+i+3]]);
      last=a;
    }
  }

  function brokenRoof(ctx,polygon,rng) {
    shape(ctx,'#2c302b',polygon);
    ctx.save();
    shape(ctx,null,polygon);
    ctx.clip();
    shape(ctx,'#1e2624',[[118,36],[207,38],[232,79],[175,86],[141,72]]);
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++) {
      const x=119+i*15;
      line(ctx,'#69513e',x,29,x+30,98,4);
      line(ctx,'#b0865c',x+1,31,x+29,95,1);
      if(i%2===0)line(ctx,'#3b352e',x+6,47,x+14,63,3);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<20;i++)box(ctx,pick(['#4a3c31','#533d2e','#84714e'],rng),139+rng()*72,22+rng()*68,3+rng()*6,2);
    ctx.restore();
    for(let i=0;i<polygon.length;i++) {
      const [x,y]=polygon[i],[a,b]=polygon[(i+1)%polygon.length];
      line(ctx,i<4?'#ac8058':'#58463a',x,y,a,b,2);
    }
  }
  function arvenHouse(ctx, w, h, d) {
    const rnd = random(98);
    const y = h-9;
    shadow(ctx,w*.52,y-3,w*.47,13);
    stonework(ctx,12,95,w-27,y-94,rnd);
    planks(ctx,108,93,w-123,36,woods,rnd);
    box(ctx,'#4d3d33',107,124,w-119,7);
    shape(ctx,'#605647',[[0,78],[63,15],[104,54],[119,94],[119,109],[6,109]]);
    roof(ctx, [[1,82],[61,14],[105,54],[117,91],[101,100],[24,102]], slate,rnd);
    shape(ctx,'#59483a',[[24,96],[62,39],[100,94],[100,117],[24,117]]);
    shape(ctx,'#87725b',[[27,96],[62,47],[95,96],[95,109],[27,108]]);
    line(ctx,'#544738',62,47,62,111,4);
    line(ctx,'#514335',29,99,91,99,5);
    roof(ctx, [[91,92],[102,55],[137,48],[171,55],[190,88],[186,103],[111,107]], slate,rnd);
    line(ctx,'#9a9186',29,61,62,15,2);
    line(ctx,'#363a42',105,55,125,96,5);
    chimney(ctx,145,28);
    shutters(ctx,46,y-60,19);
    shutters(ctx,145,y-51,17);
    line(ctx,'#383b38',155,y-48,152,y-39,1);
    line(ctx,'#383b38',152,y-39,159,y-35,1);
    weathering(ctx,28,112,55,32,rnd);
    box(ctx,'#a99273',129,111,23,4);
    box(ctx,'#bca589',127,116,29,3);
    moss(ctx,15,y-8,62,rnd);moss(ctx,150,y-7,27,rnd);
    door(ctx,d,y);
    for(let j=0;j<3;j++)for(let i=0;i<4-j;i++) {
      const x=19+i*8+j*3,a=y-4-j*5;
      box(ctx,'#573f31',x,a,9,5);box(ctx,'#b59368',x,a,4,4);
      box(ctx,'#735637',x+1,a+1,2,2);
    }
  }
  function lyraHouse(ctx, w, h, d) {
    const rnd = random(76);
    const y = h-9;
    shadow(ctx,w*.55,y-3,w*.44,12);
    stonework(ctx,10,140,w-22,y-139,rnd);
    box(ctx,'#ac926d',12,70,128,y-87);
    box(ctx,'#c4ab86',32,89,92,62);
    shape(ctx,'#a08d6d',[[12,134],[35,126],[49,134],[66,131],[78,143],[66,151],[13,154]]);
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++) {
      const x=16+rnd()*45,py=134+rnd()*16;
      box(ctx,'#887968',x,py,5+rnd()*7,2+rnd()*2);
    }
    for(const x of [14,58,121,137])box(ctx,'#594536',x,77,6,y-77);
    line(ctx,'#705846',18,119,56,89,4);
    line(ctx,'#705846',83,91,122,119,4);
    roof(ctx, [[4,79],[20,39],[70,24],[117,43],[153,73],[140,92],[12,95]], cedar,rnd);
    roof(ctx, [[120,96],[141,68],[175,72],[190,90],[188,112],[129,114]], cedar,rnd);
    box(ctx,'#4b4433',136,110,5,y-108);box(ctx,'#534735',181,109,6,y-106);
    chimney(ctx,42,29);
    shutters(ctx,28,y-52,22);
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const x=144+i*9,py=115+(i%2)*3;
      box(ctx,'#a68d59',x+1,108,1,11);box(ctx,'#718654',x-2,py,7,8);
      box(ctx,'#a49a61',x,py+2,3,2);
    }
    vine(ctx,14,90,71,rnd);vine(ctx,178,106,64,rnd);
    door(ctx,d,y,28);
    for (let k=0;k<2;k++) {
      const x=19+k*(w-62);
      box(ctx,'#544036',x,y-8,31,11);
      for (let i=0;i<6;i++) plant(ctx,x+3+i*4,y-7,3+i%3,rnd);
    }
    box(ctx,'#7c6247',143,135,31,6);
    for(const [i,color] of ['#a7b69a','#a8a474','#8b9a80'].entries()){
      box(ctx,color,146+i*9,128,5,7);box(ctx,'#dac69b',147+i*9,126,3,2);
    }
    box(ctx,'#4c4e42',169,y-21,15,21);box(ctx,'#947755',171,y-20,10,19);
    box(ctx,'#b29467',170,y-22,12,3);box(ctx,'#3f635f',172,y-21,8,2);
    box(ctx,'#45483e',170,y-14,13,2);box(ctx,'#45483e',170,y-5,13,2);
  }
  function warehouse(ctx,w,h,d,stage) {
    const rnd = random(42);
    const y = h-9;
    shadow(ctx,w*.52,y-5,w*.48,15);
    const damaged = stage === 'damaged';
    const repair = stage === 'repairing' || stage === 'framing';
    stonework(ctx,15,y-18,w-30,20,rnd);
    planks(ctx,11,66,w-22,y-82,['#5c4c3b','#70543f','#896647','#a17a54'],rnd);
    for (const x of [17,65,112,179,w-24]) box(ctx,'#453a32',x,71,7,y-71);
    roof(ctx, [[3,93],[33,56],[69,26],[110,19],[149,28],[181,25],[218,47],[w-3,77],
      [w-4,99],[183,106],[153,101],[106,106],[9,105]], barn,rnd);
    if (damaged || stage === 'repairing') {
      brokenRoof(ctx,[[118,29],[134,35],[149,31],[159,40],[173,35],[195,38],[198,46],[211,48],
        [206,56],[225,63],[214,70],[221,78],[204,86],[184,77],[172,81],[160,73],[153,78],
        [143,60],[132,62],[136,53],[123,47]],rnd);
      moss(ctx,27,88,53,rnd);
    }
    shutters(ctx,35,y-54,27);
    shutters(ctx,177,y-54,34);
    door(ctx,d,y,52,damaged);
    weathering(ctx,87,108,44,38,rnd);
    box(ctx,'#554133',91,113,36,9);
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++)box(ctx,'#a89972',96+i*5,115+(i%2),2,4);
    for (let i=0;i<4;i++) {
      box(ctx,'#4a4340',11+i*14,y-3,12,5);
      box(ctx,'#928476',11+i*14,y-5,11,2);
    }
    if (damaged) {
      line(ctx,'#342f29',20,99,26,y-2,7);
      line(ctx,'#a17f57',25,116,33,y-3,4);
      line(ctx,'#ae875d',36,y-65,61,y-42,5);
      line(ctx,'#765942',36,y-44,61,y-62,3);
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(ctx,'#b28f64',42+i*9,y+2,25+i*14,y+7+(i%2)*5,3);
      moss(ctx,20,y-12,46,rnd);moss(ctx,205,y-11,25,rnd);
      crate(ctx,183,y-15,'#675740');
    } else if (repair) {
      for (const x of [12,57,111,161,w-15]) box(ctx,'#b4895f',x,77,5,y-69);
      for (const sy of [96,133]) {
        box(ctx,'#654d37',9,sy,w-17,9);
        box(ctx,'#c6a274',9,sy,w-17,3);
      }
      for (const x of [12,65,124,180]) line(ctx,'#a57b56',x,y+2,x+35,131,4);
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++)box(ctx,'#c4a37d',34+j*3,y+5-j*4,55-j*4,3);
      crate(ctx,203,y-18);
    } else {
      roof(ctx,[[130,33],[173,30],[201,47],[212,66],[171,75],[149,67]],
        ['#473a31','#765742','#8e684d','#ac885e','#ba9a74'],rnd);
      line(ctx,'#836347',129,34,148,67,2);
      box(ctx,'#554b39',d+33,y-44,10,15);
      box(ctx,'#ae906c',d+35,y-42,6,10);
      crate(ctx,191,y-20);
      crate(ctx,213,y-19,'#8f7453');
      crate(ctx,204,y-35,'#9b7e59');
    }
  }

  function well(ctx) {
    const rnd = random(33);
    shadow(ctx,49,71,39,10);
    const rim = [[17,41],[31,30],[63,28],[82,37],[83,54],[68,64],[32,65],[16,55]];
    shape(ctx,'#505853',rim.map(([x,y])=>[x,y+11]));
    shape(ctx,'#9a9680',rim);
    shape(ctx,'#454f49',[[24,42],[35,35],[62,33],[73,40],[74,48],[61,56],[37,57],[25,50]]);
    shape(ctx,'#37696b',[[29,45],[42,40],[61,40],[68,45],[59,50],[41,51]]);
    for(let i=0;i<30;i++) {
      const x=20+rnd()*58,y=52+rnd()*16;
      box(ctx,pick(['#747a69','#b2a183','#5d6658','#80836f'],rnd),x,y,4+rnd()*6,2+rnd()*3);
    }
    box(ctx,'#3e4339',24,16,8,37);
    box(ctx,'#8a735a',25,16,4,34);
    box(ctx,'#3e4339',71,14,8,39);
    box(ctx,'#9c8060',71,15,4,34);
    box(ctx,'#4e4a3f',20,13,62,9);
    box(ctx,'#ae8f65',20,12,62,3);
    box(ctx,'#c7ab77',48,17,2,23);
    box(ctx,'#c8a371',46,36,10,4);
    box(ctx,'#856c52',47,40,8,8);
    box(ctx,'#b1cb9b',32,48,8,2);
  }

  function standingStone(ctx, type) {
    const tower = type === 'tower';
    const ruin = type === 'ruins';
    const rnd = random(tower ? 44 : 193);
    const base = tower ? 174 : 116;
    shadow(ctx,100,base-4,80,15);
    const stone = tower ? ['#4c5a5f','#69757a','#89908a','#abb1a2'] : ['#3e4848','#646f68','#81897b','#a7ad8b'];
    function pier(x,w,h) {
      shape(ctx,stone[0],[[x,base],[x-5,base-h+12],[x+8,base-h],[x+w-5,base-h],[x+w+3,base-h+12],[x+w,base]]);
      box(ctx,stone[1],x+4,base-h+10,w-11,h-10);
      for(let y=base-h+12;y<base;y+=8) {
        box(ctx, pick(stone,rnd),x+6+(y%3),y,w-18,3);
        box(ctx,stone[0],x+w-10,y,2,5);
      }
    }
    if (tower) {
      pier(66,64,131);
      shape(ctx,stone[0],[[54,57],[63,43],[91,17],[100,7],[115,27],[142,55],[132,63],[63,63]]);
      line(ctx,stone[2],62,52,99,10,3);
      shape(ctx,'#293737',[[83,93],[83,71],[92,62],[105,63],[112,72],[112,93]]);
      shape(ctx,'#b69b65',[[88,85],[91,78],[92,69],[102,69],[104,78],[109,85]]);
      box(ctx,'#b9a977',97,86,3,4);
      box(ctx,'#2b383a',88,143,22,31);
      box(ctx,'#929280',69,115,58,3);
    } else if (ruin) {
      pier(22,26,56);
      pier(74,31,43);
      pier(148,29,67);
      for (let i=0;i<23;i++) box(ctx,pick(stone,rnd),20+rnd()*147,base-15+rnd()*18,5+rnd()*15,3+rnd()*5);
      line(ctx,'#948f75',42,base+2,138,base+2,5);
    } else {
      pier(26,35,81);
      pier(135,30,84);
      shape(ctx,stone[1],[[27,45],[31,29],[48,18],[75,16],[104,14],[128,21],[150,29],[160,42],[152,59],
        [134,55],[121,43],[105,35],[78,35],[61,46],[53,62],[36,60]]);
      line(ctx,stone[3],47,20,76,17,3);
      line(ctx,stone[2],78,17,105,15,2);
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++) box(ctx,'#9a9b75',77+i*7,23+i%2,3,6);
    }
  }

  function wayfarerArch(ctx) {
    const rng=random(157);
    shadow(ctx,86,99,76,12);
    for(const [x,w,h] of [[18,29,73],[89,24,86]]) {
      stonework(ctx,x,99-h,w,h,rng);
      box(ctx,'#b0a38a',x-3,99-h,w+6,6);
      box(ctx,'#5e654f',x-5,96,w+10,6);
      moss(ctx,x-2,90,w+8,rng);
    }
    const points=[[31,37],[36,21],[50,11],[72,8],[85,13],[97,12],[110,24],[114,41],[99,41],
      [93,32],[80,27],[70,22],[57,25],[48,31],[44,44]];
    shape(ctx,'#565c50',points);
    for(const [x,y,w] of [[30,32,15],[38,24,16],[49,14,16],[63,11,14],[77,15,15],[91,18,13],[100,27,13]]) {
      masonryBlock(ctx,x,y,w,11,pick(['#a09b83','#8a8c78','#aba287','#747f69'],rng));
    }
    line(ctx,'#414d42',76,11,73,19,2);line(ctx,'#414d42',73,19,79,25,2);
    vine(ctx,25,25,66,rng);vine(ctx,103,29,65,rng);
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++)box(ctx,'#c7b899',37+i%4*2,61+(i%3)*3,1,4);
    masonryBlock(ctx,113,87,19,8,'#787d6c');masonryBlock(ctx,4,91,13,7,'#94917c');
    fern(ctx,14,103,15,true);fern(ctx,123,100,18,false);
  }

  function timber(ctx,material,rnd) {
    if(material==='twigs'||material==='broken-bough') {
      const wide=material==='broken-bough';
      const color=wide?'#776043':'#a28457';
      shadow(ctx,30,32,wide?26:18,5);
      line(ctx,'#414b36',wide?6:15,33,45,19,wide?7:4);
      line(ctx,color,wide?7:16,31,wide?51:42,17,wide?5:2);
      line(ctx,wide?'#967b50':'#bda16d',26,25,wide?35:31,wide?10:17,wide?4:2);
      line(ctx,'#735b3e',30,25,38,31,2);
      line(ctx,'#c3a071',wide?39:17,wide?21:31,wide?53:21,wide?17:30,1);
      if(wide) {
        line(ctx,'#b49669',35,10,30,6,1);
        box(ctx,'#687d50',12,28,8,2);
      } else {
        line(ctx,'#8f734e',19,34,37,32,2);
        box(ctx,'#988254',37,32,5,1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if(material==='driftwood') {
      shadow(ctx,34,33,29,5);
      shape(ctx,'#505951',[[3,27],[11,24],[22,25],[36,18],[41,9],[48,7],[43,17],[58,14],[63,17],
        [49,22],[41,28],[24,33],[10,34],[7,31]]);
      shape(ctx,'#8b8973',[[4,27],[13,24],[25,26],[37,19],[43,9],[47,7],[43,17],[60,15],[57,18],
        [45,22],[31,29],[18,30],[9,32]]);
      line(ctx,'#b5ab89',11,27,25,28,2);
      line(ctx,'#b5ab89',26,27,43,20,1);
      line(ctx,'#6a6c5c',17,31,31,28,2);
      line(ctx,'#b5ab89',44,19,57,16,1);
      line(ctx,'#698a6b',30,30,40,26,3);
      box(ctx,'#afbcaa',22,31,4,1);
      return;
    }
    const birch=material==='birch';
    const cut=birch?'#e1c59a':'#c09a69';
    const bark=birch?'#c4c7a7':'#7d6546';
    const dark=birch?'#737e6a':'#514436';
    shadow(ctx,32,33,26,7);
    ctx.save();
    if(birch){ctx.translate(62,-2);ctx.scale(-1,1);}
    shape(ctx,dark,[[6,28],[11,20],[43,12],[54,13],[59,19],[56,24],[17,35],[10,34]]);
    shape(ctx,bark,[[9,26],[13,20],[44,12],[53,13],[57,17],[49,21],[17,31]]);
    line(ctx,birch?'#e2dfc3':'#aa8860',16,22,48,15,2);
    line(ctx,dark,19,31,55,21,2);
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
      const x=21+i*3.6,y=22-(x-21)*.24+rnd()*6;
      if(birch)box(ctx,i%3?'#4d554c':'#8d9580',x,y,3,2+i%3);
      else line(ctx,i%3?'#5c4c3a':'#b19866',x,y,x+6+rnd()*6,y-2,1);
    }
    shape(ctx,cut,[[7,25],[11,20],[18,21],[22,26],[19,32],[13,35],[7,32]]);
    shape(ctx,birch?'#ae9c76':'#907651',[[10,25],[13,23],[17,24],[19,28],[15,32],[11,31]]);
    line(ctx,cut,12,25,15,29,2);
    line(ctx,dark,16,28,19,32,1);
    if(!birch) {
      line(ctx,'#8a674a',33,19,37,11,4);
      box(ctx,'#bea077',36,10,4,2);
      box(ctx,'#708254',42,23,10,2);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function deposit(ctx,node,state,rnd) {
    const material=C.NODE_MATERIAL[node.id];
    const stone=geology[material==='limestone'?'limestone':
      material==='blackstone'||material==='iron'?'blackstone':'granite'];
    const loose=node.kind==='stone';
    const spent=state==='spent',worked=state==='worked';
    if(loose) {
      if(!spent) {
        chip(ctx,21,31,16,8,stone);
        chip(ctx,33,30,18,10,stone);
        chip(ctx,43,34,10,5,stone);
        if(material==='moss')for(let i=0;i<6;i++)box(ctx,'#82945c',19+rnd()*19,23+rnd()*5,3,2);
      } else box(ctx,'#717b59',24,33,14,2);
      return;
    }
    if(node.kind==='fragment') {
      shape(ctx,spent?'#69765b':'#6e746c',[[21,30],[24,20],[37,18],[42,23],[38,34],[25,36]]);
      if(!spent)for(let i=0;i<5;i++)line(ctx,'#d6bd82',26+i*2,25,27+i*2,22,1);
      return;
    }
    shadow(ctx,34,34,27,7);
    const lip=spent?[[7,26],[19,23],[31,27],[49,21],[56,25]]:
      worked?[[7,26],[17,15],[27,18],[33,23],[44,15],[55,22]]:
      [[7,26],[15,17],[23,13],[31,8],[43,9],[53,16],[58,26]];
    shape(ctx,stone.faces[0],[...lip,[54,34],[35,38],[17,36],[8,32]]);
    shape(ctx,stone.ledges[1],[...lip,[53,28],[39,30],[30,25],[18,27]]);
    line(ctx,stone.faces[3],10,32,27,31,2);
    line(ctx,stone.fresh,42,16,51,21,1);
    if(worked||spent) {chip(ctx,32,35,8,4,stone);chip(ctx,45,39,7,3,stone);}
    const minerals={'copper-ore':['#567365','#9ab694'],silverstone:['#8a9da4','#d1d9d4'],
      mooncrystal:['#777098','#d9c7ec'],iron:['#966b5e','#d1a884']};
    if(node.kind==='iron'&&!spent) {
      const seams=worked?[[45,19],[49,23]]:[[18,18],[25,17],[33,13],[47,18],[50,23]];
      const [dark,light]=minerals[material]||minerals.iron;
      for(const [x,y] of seams) {
        if(material==='mooncrystal')shape(ctx,dark,[[x-3,y+4],[x-1,y-7],[x+3,y-13],[x+7,y-7],[x+5,y+3]]);
        else shape(ctx,dark,[[x-3,y+4],[x-2,y+1],[x+7,y-3],[x+5,y+1]]);
        line(ctx,light,x,y+2,x+4,y-(material==='mooncrystal'?9:2),1);
      }
    }
    if(material==='clay'&&!spent)shape(ctx,'#bc856a',worked?[[13,31],[24,25],[33,27],[31,34]]:
      [[12,27],[21,18],[39,15],[51,21],[54,29],[42,33],[20,34]]);
    if(material==='ancient-stone'&&!spent)for(let i=0;i<(worked?2:5);i++)box(ctx,'#a68e64',20+i*5,19-i,2,7);
  }

  function quarry(ctx,stage) {
    const {outline,pit,visualSeed}=C.QUARRY;
    const rnd=random(visualSeed);
    const profile = points=>points.map(([x,y])=>[48+x*T,34+y*T]);
    const rim=profile(outline), floor=profile(pit);
    const limestone=geology.limestone;
    if(stage==='restored') {
      shape(ctx,'#667150',rim);
      for(let i=0;i<22;i++)box(ctx,pick(['#75865b','#8d9464','#6e7c54'],rnd),24+rnd()*49,27+rnd()*19,3,2);
      return;
    }
    const covered=stage==='marked'||stage==='unmarked';
    shape(ctx,covered?'#82765a':'#665d46',rim);
    shape(ctx,'#8e8060',rim.map(([x,y])=>[x+(48-x)*.19,y+(34-y)*.19-2]));
    for(let k=0;k<19;k++) {
      const edge=rim[k%rim.length],next=rim[(k+1)%rim.length];
      const [x,y]=interpolate(edge,next,rnd());
      chip(ctx,x,y,3+rnd()*5,2+rnd()*3,limestone);
    }
    if(covered) {
      shape(ctx,'#a29170',[[22,33],[31,20],[45,18],[57,25],[67,35],[45,39]]);
      line(ctx,'#ddd1a5',26,32,34,24,2);
    } else {
      shape(ctx,stage==='cleared'?'#685b47':'#36383a',floor);
      const bottom=floor.map(([x,y])=>[x+(48-x)*.28,y+(34-y)*.36+5]);
      shape(ctx,stage==='cleared'?'#a69573':'#67665b',bottom);
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++)line(ctx,k%2?'#6b593f':'#ae9872',28+k*2,27+k*2,68-k*3,25+k*3,2);
      if(stage==='cleared') {
        shape(ctx,'#b0a184',[[33,38],[35,27],[51,25],[64,32],[59,40],[45,43]]);
        line(ctx,'#6d6959',51,26,47,33,2);
        line(ctx,'#6d6959',47,33,52,39,1);
      } else if(stage==='revealed') {
        shape(ctx,'#77635a',[[31,39],[36,32],[49,29],[62,34],[65,42],[50,44]]);
        for(let j=0;j<5;j++)line(ctx,j%2?'#e0ae84':'#a8765e',35+j*5,37+j%2*3,40+j*5,33+j%2*4,2);
        box(ctx,'#d5c295',56,35,5,4);
      } else {
        for(let k=0;k<9;k++)box(ctx,pick(['#9e9174','#5a554a','#777064'],rnd),34+rnd()*27,32+rnd()*12,2,2);
      }
    }
    if(stage==='marked'||stage==='preserved') {
      for(const x of [20,76]) {
        line(ctx,'#423e30',x,29,x+1,42,3);
        box(ctx,'#d2aa66',x-2,28,6,3);
      }
      if(stage==='preserved')line(ctx,'#ceb587',21,33,75,33,1);
    }
  }

  function resource(ctx, node, state) {
    const x=26,y=27;
    const rnd=random(geologySeed(node.id));
    const kind=node.kind;
    const depleted=state==='spent';
    if (kind==='water') {
      box(ctx,depleted?'#667a67':'#a5c6ac',19,26,depleted?6:14,2);
      if(!depleted)box(ctx,'#70a4a0',23,29,state==='worked'?6:12,1);
      return;
    }
    if (kind==='fish') {
      box(ctx,'#4b4738',21,9,4,22);box(ctx,'#b5a270',18,13,13,6);
      if(!depleted){line(ctx,'#adbd9b',31,16,45,21,1);box(ctx,'#e1bb86',42,22,4,2);}
      return;
    }
    if (depleted && ['log','branch','fragment','herb'].includes(kind)) {
      box(ctx,'#64734d',19,31,11,2);box(ctx,'#7d8655',25,29,8,2);
      return;
    }
    if (kind==='log'||kind==='branch') {
      return timber(ctx,C.NODE_MATERIAL[node.id],rnd);
    }
    if (['stone','rock','iron','fragment'].includes(kind)) {
      deposit(ctx,node,state,rnd);easternDepositDetail(ctx,node,state,rnd);return;
    }
    shadow(ctx,x+2,30,20,6);
    if (kind==='berries') {
      mass(ctx,x,y-8,16,['#334d38','#456747','#4c7350','#658855','#8ba062'],rnd);
      if(!depleted)for(let i=0;i<10;i++)box(ctx,i%3?'#a5546a':'#e59491',x+(rnd()-.5)*21,y-14+rnd()*13,3,3);
    } else {
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++) {
        const dx=(i-3)*4,dy=Math.abs(dx)*.33;
        line(ctx,'#a2ab71',x,y+4,x+dx,y-13+dy,2);
        box(ctx,i%2?'#699057':'#9bb476',x+dx-3,y-10+dy,7,4);
        box(ctx,'#c3a4b4',x+dx,y-15+dy,2,3);
      }
    }
  }

  function icon(ctx,type) {
    if(type==='letter') {
      shadow(ctx,26,30,13,4);
      shape(ctx,'#dac9a3',[[13,24],[26,18],[39,23],[36,31],[23,35],[12,30]]);
      line(ctx,'#9f7d63',14,23,25,28);line(ctx,'#9f7d63',25,28,38,23);
      box(ctx,'#974f52',24,27,4,4);
    } else if(type==='fire') {
      shadow(ctx,27,34,20,7);
      for(const [x,y] of [[13,27],[18,20],[29,20],[35,26],[30,33],[21,34],[12,32]]) {
        box(ctx,'#576160',x,y,7,5);box(ctx,'#8b8e7d',x,y,6,2);
      }
      line(ctx,'#8a6747',20,29,34,22,3);line(ctx,'#ac845c',21,23,32,31,3);
    }
  }

  const itemIcons=new Map();
  function developmentItem(ctx,id){
    const rng=random(id.split('').reduce((n,c)=>n+c.charCodeAt(0),19));
    shadow(ctx,28,35,19,5);
    if(id==='plank'){
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=8+i*5,y=30-i*5;shape(ctx,'#514434',[[x,y-5],[x+29,y-12],[x+39,y-8],[x+8,y],[x,y-2]]);shape(ctx,'#b18b5e',[[x+1,y-5],[x+29,y-11],[x+36,y-8],[x+8,y-2]]);line(ctx,'#d4b180',x+5,y-5,x+30,y-10,1);line(ctx,'#7e5c3b',x+4,y-3,x+28,y-8,1);}
    }else if(id==='brick'){
      for(const [x,y] of [[9,24],[27,24],[19,15]]){shape(ctx,'#48524d',[[x,y],[x+16,y-5],[x+25,y],[x+25,y+8],[x+8,y+12],[x,y+7]]);shape(ctx,'#9da597',[[x+1,y],[x+16,y-4],[x+23,y],[x+8,y+4]]);shape(ctx,'#737f75',[[x+8,y+5],[x+23,y+1],[x+23,y+7],[x+8,y+11]]);line(ctx,'#bfc0ae',x+1,y,x+16,y-4,1);}
    }else if(id.endsWith('-seed')){
      shape(ctx,'#5f5238',[[17,7],[38,7],[42,32],[35,37],[17,35],[13,30]]);shape(ctx,'#ccba8c',[[18,8],[37,8],[40,31],[34,35],[18,33],[15,29]]);box(ctx,'#928461',17,10,21,3);box(ctx,'#e4d4aa',19,15,17,13);
      const col=C.INVENTORY[id][6];for(let j=0;j<5;j++){box(ctx,col,23+j%3*4,20+Math.floor(j/3)*4,3,2);box(ctx,'#6b6650',24+j%3*4,22+Math.floor(j/3)*4,2,1);}
    }else if(id==='wheat'){
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const x=16+i*6;line(ctx,'#68764b',28,34,x,12+i%2*3,2);line(ctx,'#b6ae64',28,32,x,13+i%2*3,1);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){box(ctx,'#d3ab57',x-3+j%2*2,9+j*4+i%2*3,5,3);box(ctx,'#edcc79',x-3+j%2*2,9+j*4+i%2*3,2,1);}}box(ctx,'#94734b',23,26,10,2);
    }else if(id==='carrot'){
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=16+i*11,y=16+i%2*4;shape(ctx,'#874c31',[[x-5,y],[x+5,y-1],[x+4,y+10],[x-2,y+19],[x-6,y+10]]);shape(ctx,'#d08b48',[[x-4,y],[x+3,y],[x+2,y+9],[x-2,y+16],[x-4,y+9]]);box(ctx,'#e4b46d',x-4,y+3,3,2);for(const dx of [-5,0,6])line(ctx,'#557a4d',x,y,x+dx,y-9,2);}
    }else if(id==='potato'){
      for(const [x,y,r] of [[19,23,9],[34,25,8],[29,15,8]]){mass(ctx,x,y,r,['#765a41','#a08965','#bca47a','#d0b890','#d6bd96'],rng);box(ctx,'#806b51',x+2,y-2,2,2);}
    }else if(id==='mint'){
      line(ctx,'#4f7c55',28,35,29,10,2);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const y=29-j*4;shape(ctx,'#4c7d57',[[28,y],[16,y-6],[19,y-9],[26,y-6],[28,y-2],[37,y-8],[42,y-7],[38,y-3]]);line(ctx,'#9cc596',20,y-6,27,y-2,1);line(ctx,'#85af80',30,y-3,38,y-6,1);}
    }else if(id==='cabinet'){
      devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.wood,13,5,30,30,rng);devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.wood,16,9,24,9,rng);devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.wood,16,20,24,12,rng);box(ctx,'#e1bd77',26,13,4,2);box(ctx,'#e1bd77',26,25,4,2);box(ctx,'#473b32',16,35,4,3);box(ctx,'#473b32',36,35,4,3);
    }else if(id==='fitting'){
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=12+i*10,y=13+i%2*8;shape(ctx,'#454d4a',[[x,y],[x+12,y-1],[x+13,y+5],[x+7,y+6],[x+7,y+16],[x,y+16]]);shape(ctx,'#9da9a2',[[x+2,y+1],[x+10,y],[x+10,y+4],[x+5,y+5],[x+5,y+14],[x+2,y+14]]);box(ctx,'#393f40',x+3,y+2,2,2);box(ctx,'#393f40',x+3,y+10,2,2);}
    }else if(id==='research-scroll'){
      shape(ctx,'#756b50',[[12,11],[31,7],[45,15],[40,32],[18,36],[9,29]]);shape(ctx,'#d4c69d',[[13,12],[31,9],[42,16],[38,31],[18,34],[11,29]]);line(ctx,'#e1d6b5',13,12,31,9,2);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)line(ctx,'#8d8267',17,16+j*4,35-j*2,14+j*4,1);box(ctx,'#8c6652',34,29,6,4);
    }else return false;
    return true;
  }

  function inventoryIcon(id) {
    if(itemIcons.has(id))return itemIcons.get(id);
    const art=document.createElement('canvas');
    art.width=56;art.height=42;
    const ctx=art.getContext('2d');
    const kind={wood:'log',stone:'rock',food:'berries',herbs:'herb',water:'water',iron:'iron','ancient-fragment':'fragment'}[id];
    if(id==='flour'){shape(ctx,'#6e5f45',[[15,33],[15,18],[21,11],[20,7],[36,7],[35,11],[42,19],[42,32],[36,36],[18,36]]);shape(ctx,'#d7bd88',[[17,31],[18,18],[23,12],[33,12],[39,20],[39,31],[35,34],[20,34]]);box(ctx,'#8a6e49',21,10,15,3);box(ctx,'#e8d5a6',24,15,11,2);box(ctx,'#a88a57',24,23,10,8);box(ctx,'#f0d99c',26,25,6,4);}else if(kind) resource(ctx,{id,kind},'full');
    else if(id==='mysterious-letter') icon(ctx,'letter');
    else if(['copper-ore','clay','silverstone','mooncrystal','ancient-stone'].includes(id)) {
      ctx.translate(9,5);
      ctx.scale(1.13,1.13);
      groundArt(ctx,id);
    }
    else if(id==='quarry-tablet') {
      shape(ctx,'#454d44',[[13,19],[19,9],[36,7],[44,12],[42,30],[34,35],[15,33]]);
      shape(ctx,'#8a8870',[[16,18],[21,11],[35,9],[41,13],[38,28],[32,31],[17,31]]);
      line(ctx,'#b5aa84',22,12,35,10,2);
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
        const x=21+i*2.6;
        line(ctx,i===6?'#d3bc87':'#514a3d',x,16,x-1,24-i%2,1);
      }
      line(ctx,'#625f50',22,28,31,27,1);
    }
    else if(['plank','brick','wheat-seed','carrot-seed','potato-seed','mint-seed','wheat','carrot','potato','mint','cabinet','fitting','research-scroll'].includes(id))developmentItem(ctx,id);
    else if(id==='journal') {
      box(ctx,'#4e3b36',12,8,31,27);box(ctx,'#9d7854',16,8,24,25);
      box(ctx,'#d0ad77',20,12,14,5);box(ctx,'#7c5a43',38,10,4,21);
    } else {
      shape(ctx,'#9c673e',[[22,9],[34,12],[38,20],[34,31],[23,34],[16,26],[16,16]]);
      shape(ctx,'#dfa56a',[[22,13],[32,15],[34,21],[30,28],[23,30],[20,25],[20,18]]);
      box(ctx,'#cca069',25,19,4,7);
    }
    const pixel=document.createElement('canvas');
    pixel.width=26;pixel.height=21;
    const out=pixel.getContext('2d');
    out.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    out.drawImage(art,0,0,26,21);
    const url=pixel.toDataURL();
    itemIcons.set(id,url);
    return url;
  }

  const toolIcons = new Map();
  function drawTool(ctx, icon, condition) {
    const metal = condition === 'broken' ? '#8d7761' : condition === 'worn' ? '#a99a78' : '#becac2';
    const wood = '#ad865a';
    const outline = '#302c28';
    if (icon === 'bucket') {
      line(ctx,outline,5,8,6,4,2);line(ctx,outline,6,4,11,4,2);line(ctx,outline,11,4,13,8,2);
      shape(ctx,outline,[[3,8],[15,8],[14,17],[11,19],[6,19],[4,16]]);
      shape(ctx,wood,[[5,10],[13,10],[12,17],[6,17]]);
      box(ctx,'#72553e',7,10,1,7);box(ctx,'#72553e',10,10,1,7);
      box(ctx,metal,4,9,10,2);box(ctx,metal,5,15,9,1);
      if (condition==='broken')box(ctx,outline,10,15,2,3);
      return;
    }
    if (icon === 'rod') {
      line(ctx,outline,4,18,14,3,3);line(ctx,wood,4,17,14,3,1);
      box(ctx,'#c5a476',3,16,3,3);box(ctx,'#779382',9,11,2,1);
      if (condition !== 'broken') {
        line(ctx,'#e5d9ad',14,3,15,12,1);line(ctx,'#e5d9ad',15,12,14,14,1);
        box(ctx,condition==='worn'?'#8d6b46':'#c86a56',14,12,2,3);
      }
      return;
    }
    line(ctx,outline,5,18,12,7,4);line(ctx,wood,5,18,12,7,2);
    box(ctx,'#c79b69',6,14,1,3);
    if(icon==='hammer'){
      shape(ctx,outline,[[8,3],[15,3],[17,6],[15,11],[12,11],[11,9],[7,7]]);
      shape(ctx,metal,[[9,4],[15,4],[16,6],[14,9],[11,8],[8,6]]);
      box(ctx,'#777f75',14,6,1,3);
    }else{
      shape(ctx,outline,[[8,5],[12,4],[16,3],[18,4],[17,10],[14,13],[11,11],[12,8],[8,8]]);
      shape(ctx,metal,[[9,6],[14,5],[17,4],[16,9],[14,11],[12,10],[13,7],[9,7]]);
      box(ctx,'#e2d9ac',15,7,1,3);
    }
    if(condition==='broken'){
      box(ctx,outline,8,11,3,2);box(ctx,'#5d4533',8,11,1,1);
    }else if(condition==='worn')box(ctx,'#78664a',14,4,2,1);
  }

  function toolIcon(icon,condition) {
    const key=icon+'-'+condition;
    if(!toolIcons.has(key)){
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width=20;canvas.height=21;
      drawTool(canvas.getContext('2d'),icon,condition);
      toolIcons.set(key,canvas.toDataURL());
    }
    return toolIcons.get(key);
  }

  const grips={
    down:[7,0,.41,false],up:[-7,-4,-.53,true],
    side:[8,-1,.65,false],dside:[8,0,.49,false],uside:[7,-4,.32,true],
  };

  function toolPose(scene,view,selection,condition,task,show) {
    view.heritageWork=show&&task?.heritage?task:null;const repair=!!(task?.heritage&&!task.heritage.service&&task.heritage.stage>=2||task?.frontier&&task.frontier.stage>=2||task?.east&&task.east.stage>=2);if(repair){selection={definition:{icon:'hammer'}};condition='good';}
    if(show && task?.phase==='impact') {
      if(!view.toolImpact) {
        view.toolImpact=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(30);
        scene.areaObjs.push(view.toolImpact);
      }
      const pulse=view.toolImpact;
      const p=(task.elapsed/task.duration-.62)/.19;
      const color=['water','fish'].includes(task.node.kind)?0xa6d6d2:
        task.quarry||['stone','iron','rock'].includes(task.node.kind)?0xd4c6ad:0xdbb37e;
      const {x,y}=center(task.quarry?C.QUARRY:task.node);
      pulse.clear().setVisible(true);
      pulse.fillStyle(color,.78*(1-p));
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++)pulse.fillRect(x+(i-2)*(3+p*5),y-4-(i%3)*(4+p*5),2,2);
    } else if(view.toolImpact?.visible) view.toolImpact.clear().setVisible(false);
    const sprite=view.heldTool;
    if(!show||!selection || (task&&!task.toolId&&!repair)){
      sprite?.setVisible(false);
      return;
    }
    const icon=selection.definition.icon;
    const key=texture(scene,'tool-'+icon+'-'+condition,20,21,ctx=>drawTool(ctx,icon,condition));
    if(!sprite){
      view.heldTool=scene.add.image(0,0,key).setOrigin(.24,.88);
      view.heldTool.valleyEquipment=true;
      scene.areaObjs.push(view.heldTool);
    }
    const held=view.heldTool;
    const player=scene.player;
    const face=scene.pFace;
    const dir=face?.dir || 'down';
    const [dx,dy,angle,back]=grips[dir] || grips.down;
    const flip=dir!=='down'&&dir!=='up'&&!!face?.flip;
    const mirror=flip?-1:1;
    const scale=scene.inside?.k||1;
    const progress=task?task.elapsed/task.duration:0;
    const arc=task?Math.sin(progress*Math.PI):0;
    const contact=task&&task.phase==='impact';
    const sweep=task&&progress>.24 ? Math.sin((progress-.24)*Math.PI*1.4) : -2*arc;
    const gentle=icon==='bucket'||icon==='rod';
    if(held.texture.key!==key)held.setTexture(key);
    held.setVisible(true)
      .setPosition(player.x+mirror*(dx+(contact?3:2*arc))*scale,player.y+(dy-3*arc)*scale)
      .setScale(.77*scale)
      .setFlipX(flip)
      .setRotation(mirror*(angle+sweep*(gentle?.25:.82)))
      .setDepth(player.depth+(back?-.002:.002));
  }

  function map(scene) {
    const image = (key,w,h,paint,x,y,ox=.5,oy=.5,depth=0) => {
      const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,texture(scene,key,w,h,paint)).setOrigin(ox,oy).setDepth(depth);
      scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);
      return sprite;
    };
    const depthAt=y=> 10+y/1000;
    const terrainKey=detailTexture(scene,'terrain-desire-paths',C.WorldConfig.baseWidth*T,C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T,ground);const terrainSprite=scene.add.image(0,0,terrainKey).setOrigin(0).setDepth(0).setScale(1/detailFactor(terrainKey));scene.areaObjs.push(terrainSprite);
    for(const rock of ROCKS) {
      if(!rock.solid)continue;
      const {x,y,w,h}=C.bounds(rock.solid);
      const pad=16,head=Math.ceil(rock.rise*T*1.2)+10;
      const left=Math.floor(x*T)-pad,top=Math.floor(y*T)-head;
      image('cliff-'+rock.id,Math.ceil(w*T)+pad*2,Math.ceil(h*T)+head+pad,ctx=>{
        ctx.translate(-left,-top);
        stratum(ctx,rock);
      },left,top,0,0,depthAt((y+h)*T));
    }
    const trees=new Map();
    for(const entry of Object.values(TREES_BY_ID)) {
      if(!C.legacy(entry.x,entry.y))continue;
      const {x,y}=center(entry),kind=entry.species;
      const old=kind===5;
      const sprite=image('tree-'+kind,old?210:130,old?194:126,ctx=>tree(ctx,kind),
        x,y+4,.5,old?180/194:112/126,depthAt(y+4));
      if(!old && entry.visualSeed%5===0) sprite.setFlipX(true);
      sprite.valleyTree=entry.id;
      trees.set(entry.id,{sprite,mature:sprite.texture.key,entry,stage:'mature'});
    }
    const arch=center(POINTS.wayfarer);
    image('wayfarer-arch',156,114,wayfarerArch,arch.x,arch.y,.5,105/114,depthAt(arch.y));
    for(const [key,type] of [['arch','arch'],['ruins','ruins'],['tower','tower']]) {
      const {x,y}=center(POINTS[key]);
      image('landmark-'+type,200,type==='tower'?183:128,ctx=>standingStone(ctx,type),
        x,y,.5,type==='tower'?174/183:116/128,type==='tower'?depthAt(y)+1:depthAt(y));
    }
    for(const [index, house] of HOUSES.entries()) {
      const w=house.w*T,h=house.h*T+28,d=(house.door-house.x)*T+16;
      if(index===2) continue;
      image(house.id,w,h,ctx=>(index===0?arvenHouse:lyraHouse)(ctx,w,h,d),
        house.x*T,house.y*T-20,0,0,depthAt((house.y+house.h)*T));
    }
    const house=HOUSES[2],w=house.w*T,h=house.h*T+26,d=(house.door-house.x)*T+16;
    const warehouseImages = Object.fromEntries(['damaged','repairing','framing','completed'].map(key=>[key,
      texture(scene,'warehouse-'+key,w,h,ctx=>warehouse(ctx,w,h,d,key))]));
    const stockroom=image('warehouse-damaged',w,h,ctx=>warehouse(ctx,w,h,d,'damaged'),
      house.x*T,house.y*T-17,0,0,depthAt((house.y+house.h)*T));
    const wellPt=center(POINTS.well);
    image('well',100,92,well,wellPt.x,wellPt.y,.5,57/92,depthAt(wellPt.y+15));
    const firePt=center(POINTS.fire);
    image('fire',54,40,ctx=>icon(ctx,'fire'),firePt.x,firePt.y,.5,.69,depthAt(firePt.y));
    const letterPt=center(POINTS.letter);
    const letter=image('letter',52,40,ctx=>icon(ctx,'letter'),letterPt.x,letterPt.y,.5,.7,depthAt(letterPt.y));
    const resources = new Map(C.NODES.filter(node=>node.kind!=='tree'&&C.legacy(node.x,node.y)).map(node=>{
      const {x,y}=center(node);
      const key='node-'+(C.NODE_MATERIAL[node.id]||node.kind);
      const width=['log','rock','iron','branch','stone'].includes(node.kind)?66:56;
      const height=44;
      const sprite=image(key,width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,node,'full'),x,y,.5,30/height,depthAt(y));
      const resting=texture(scene,key+'-spent',width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,node,'spent'));
      const worked=node.stock>1?texture(scene,key+'-worked',width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,node,'worked')):null;
      return [node.id,{sprite,normal:sprite.texture.key,resting,worked}];
    }));
    const pit=center(C.QUARRY);
    const quarryImages=Object.fromEntries(['unmarked','marked','cleared','revealed','exhausted','preserved','restored'].map(stage=>
      [stage,texture(scene,'quarry-'+stage,96,64,ctx=>quarry(ctx,stage))]));
    const quarrySprite=image('quarry-marked',96,64,ctx=>quarry(ctx,'marked'),pit.x,pit.y,.5,34/64,8);
    quarrySprite.setVisible(false);
    const shadows=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(7);
    const nearFog=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(9);
    const effects=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(32);
    const tint=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(37);
    const light=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(38);
    scene.areaObjs.push(shadows,nearFog,effects,tint,light);
    return {scene,stream:new Map(),streamKey:"",stockroom, warehouseImages, letter, resources, quarryImages, quarrySprite,
      shadows,nearFog,effects,tint,light,trees,depthAt,piles:new Map(),freePiles:[]};
  }

  function treeGrowth(ctx,kind,stage) {
    const x=65,y=112;
    shadow(ctx,x+1,y+3,stage==='young'?16:11,4);
    if(stage==='stump') {
      shape(ctx,'#524838',[[53,115],[58,108],[58,101],[72,99],[73,109],[79,114],[69,117]]);
      shape(ctx,'#aa8c61',[[57,103],[61,99],[70,98],[74,102],[70,106],[62,107]]);
      line(ctx,'#d0ab76',61,103,69,101,2);
      line(ctx,'#786046',63,108,61,114,2);
      box(ctx,'#7b9359',72,111,4,2);
      return;
    }
    const young=stage==='young',height=young?53:23;
    line(ctx,'#564c36',x,y,x+2,y-height,young?5:2);
    line(ctx,'#a08860',x-1,y,x+1,y-height,1);
    const rnd=random(1325+kind);
    const colors=leaf[kind];
    for(const side of [-1,1]) {
      const dx=side*(young?16:7),top=y-height*(side>0?.79:.55);
      line(ctx,'#7d7050',x,top+8,x+dx,top,young?3:1);
      mass(ctx,x+dx,top-1,young?12:5,colors,rnd);
    }
    mass(ctx,x+1,y-height,young?15:6,colors,rnd);
  }

  function fell(view,id,target) {
    const info=view.trees.get(id); if(!info)return;
    const scene=view.scene;
    const ghost=scene.add.image(info.sprite.x,info.sprite.y,info.mature)
      .setOrigin(info.sprite.originX,info.sprite.originY).setFlipX(info.sprite.flipX).setDepth(info.sprite.depth+.005);
    scene.areaObjs.push(ghost);
    const direction=ghost.x<target.x?-1:1;
    scene.tweens.add({targets:ghost,rotation:direction*1.37,alpha:.03,duration:660,ease:'Cubic.easeIn',
      onComplete:()=>{
        const index=scene.areaObjs.indexOf(ghost);
        if(index>=0)scene.areaObjs.splice(index,1);
        if(ghost.scene)ghost.destroy();
      },
    });
  }

  function groundArt(ctx,item) {
    const color=C.INVENTORY[item][6];
    if(['plank','brick','wheat-seed','carrot-seed','potato-seed','mint-seed','wheat','carrot','potato','mint','cabinet','fitting','research-scroll'].includes(item)){ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,-2);ctx.scale(.60,.60);developmentItem(ctx,item);ctx.restore();return;}
    shadow(ctx,17,19,13,4);
    if(item==='wood'){
      line(ctx,'#493e33',7,20,25,11,6);
      line(ctx,color,8,18,25,10,4);
      line(ctx,'#93724e',9,19,26,12,2);
      box(ctx,'#e5ba83',5,16,5,5);
      line(ctx,'#ac8a5d',15,14,17,8,2);
    } else if(item==='food'||item==='herbs') {
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
        const x=9+i*4,y=16+(i%2)*2;
        box(ctx,'#394e3b',x-1,y-1,6,6);
        box(ctx,color,x,y,4,4);
      }
      line(ctx,'#a0ad6f',13,16,18,10,1);
    } else if(item==='water') {
      shape(ctx,'#4a523e',[[9,13],[15,10],[22,11],[25,14],[22,21],[12,22]]);
      box(ctx,color,12,13,10,4);
      line(ctx,'#b2c6a2',13,15,19,14,1);
    } else {
      shape(ctx,'#353f3b',[[6,16],[11,11],[18,9],[25,13],[28,17],[24,22],[10,22]]);
      shape(ctx,color,[[9,15],[16,10],[21,11],[25,15],[20,18],[12,19]]);
      line(ctx,'#babc9a',12,15,17,12,1);
      if(C.INVENTORY[item][4]==='unique')for(let i=0;i<4;i++)box(ctx,'#675548',16+i*2,13+i%2,1,4);
    }
  }

  function drawPiles(view,world) {
    const {scene,piles,freePiles}=view;
    const frame=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    const visible=([,d])=>!scene.inside&&d.x>=frame.left-60&&d.x<=frame.right+60&&
      d.y>=frame.top-60&&d.y<=frame.bottom+60;
    const nearby=Object.entries(world.ground.drops).filter(visible);
    const keep=new Set(nearby.map(([id])=>id));
    for(const [id,sprite] of piles) {
      if(keep.has(id))continue;
      sprite.setVisible(false);
      freePiles.push(sprite);
      piles.delete(id);
    }
    for(const [id,d] of nearby) {
      if(piles.has(id))continue;
      const key=texture(scene,'pile-'+d.item,34,26,ctx=>groundArt(ctx,d.item));
      const sprite=freePiles.pop()||scene.add.image(0,0,key).setOrigin(.5,.73);
      if(!sprite.valleyPile)scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);
      sprite.valleyPile=id;
      sprite.setTexture(key).setPosition(d.x,d.y).setDepth(view.depthAt(d.y)+.002)
        .setFlipX(d.variant%2===0).setVisible(true);
      piles.set(id,sprite);
    }
  }

  function refresh(view, world, sim) {
    const warehouse=world.buildings['ruined-warehouse'];
    const key=warehouse.status==='repairing'&&sim.total(world)>=(warehouse.repairStartedAt+warehouse.repairEndsAt)/2?
      'framing':(['damaged','repairing'].includes(warehouse.status)?warehouse.status:'completed');
    if(view.stockroom.texture.key!==view.warehouseImages[key])view.stockroom.setTexture(view.warehouseImages[key]);
    view.letter.setVisible(!world.quests['where-am-i'].letter);
    for(const [id,item] of view.resources){
      const node=C.NODE_BY_ID.get(id);if(!node)continue;const stock=sim.nodeStock(world,node);
      const textureKey=stock===0?item.resting:stock<node.stock&&item.worked?item.worked:item.normal;
      if(item.sprite.texture.key!==textureKey)item.sprite.setTexture(textureKey);
    }
    for(const info of view.trees.values()) {
      const {sprite,entry,mature}=info;
      const stage=sim.treeStage(world,entry);
      if(stage===info.stage)continue;
      info.stage=stage;
      sprite.setAlpha(1);
      sprite.valleyOccludes=false;
      const key=stage==='mature'?mature:texture(view.scene,'growth-'+entry.species+'-'+stage,
        130,126,ctx=>treeGrowth(ctx,entry.species,stage));
      sprite.setTexture(key);
    }
    drawPiles(view,world);
    developmentObjects(view,world);
    const stage=sim.quarryStage(world);
    view.quarrySprite.setVisible(stage!=='hidden');
    if(view.quarryImages[stage]&&view.quarrySprite.texture.key!==view.quarryImages[stage]){
      view.quarrySprite.setTexture(view.quarryImages[stage]);
    }
  }

  const QUALITY=[
    {name:'Low',interval:125,rain:26,fog:1,water:1,ambient:0},
    {name:'Medium',interval:85,rain:49,fog:2,water:2,ambient:6},
    {name:'High',interval:60,rain:78,fog:3,water:3,ambient:12},
    {name:'Ultra',interval:42,rain:110,fog:4,water:3,ambient:18},
  ];
  const dampSites=[[775,829,21],[874,814,15],[1044,766,24],[809,942,30],[734,750,17],[1110,784,14],[919,629,19]];

  // Small freight courts only appear at operational halls. They reflect storage and dispatch state.
  // Graphics are shared, viewport culled, no new textures per frame, no decorative collider.
  function freightLandscape(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,s=D.courierState(world),j=s.job,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.effects,shadow=view.shadows;for(const l of D.landmarks){if(D.heritageState(world,l.id).stage<8)continue;const x=Math.round((l.x+.5)*T+33),y=Math.round((l.y+.5)*T+11);if(x<rect.x-70||x>rect.right+70||y<rect.y-70||y>rect.bottom+70)continue;const stock=D.roomState(world,l.id).stock||{},units=Object.values(stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),n=Math.min(4,Math.ceil(units/9));
      // Cargo tally board, a copper cap, timber joinery, contact shadow.
      shadow.fillStyle(0x1d2e28,.21).fillEllipse(x+7,y+4,42,11);g.fillStyle(0x3e3328).fillRect(x-4,y-16,3,19).fillRect(x+13,y-16,3,19);g.fillStyle(0xb38a55).fillRect(x-3,y-15,1,17).fillRect(x+14,y-15,1,17);g.fillStyle(0x44352b).fillRect(x-7,y-25,27,14);g.fillStyle(0x8c704a).fillRect(x-6,y-24,25,12);g.fillStyle(0xc9a776).fillRect(x-5,y-23,22,1);g.fillStyle(0x38433c).fillRect(x-3,y-21,17,7);for(let k=0;k<4;k++)g.fillStyle(k<n?0xb5cd82:0x657166).fillRect(x-2+k*4,y-19,3,3);const keeper=D.guildPose(world,l.id);if(keeper){const status=keeper.phase==='blocked'?0xd4a05d:keeper.work?0xe5ce82:keeper.duty?0x99ba81:0x77808b;g.fillStyle(0x473a2c).fillRect(x+3,y-25,7,4);g.fillStyle(status).fillRect(x+4,y-24,5,2);if(keeper.work&&!reduced){const f=(t*.36+l.x*.13)%1;g.fillStyle(status,(1-f)*.4).fillEllipse(x+6,y-23,8+f*8,5+f*5);}}
