      for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const a=x+21+(k%2)*9,b=y+2-Math.floor(k/2)*8;g.fillStyle(0x49372a).fillRect(a-1,b-8,10,10);g.fillStyle(0xa67a47).fillRect(a,b-7,8,8);g.fillStyle(0xd8b078).fillRect(a,b-7,8,2);g.fillStyle(0x785435).fillRect(a+1,b-3,6,1);g.fillStyle(0x6b5137).fillRect(a+3,b-5,1,6);}
      if(j&&(j.from===l.id||j.to===l.id)||!j&&s.at===l.id&&D.courierReady(world)){const phase=reduced?0:Math.round(Math.sin(t*3.8+l.x)*2);g.fillStyle(0x40342a).fillRect(x+19,y-27,2,21);g.fillStyle(0xc8a06b).fillRect(x+19,y-27,1,15);g.fillStyle(j?.to===l.id?0xd9b16f:0x88a482).fillPoints([{x:x+21,y:y-26},{x:x+31,y:y-24+phase},{x:x+21,y:y-17}],true);g.fillStyle(0xefe0a1).fillRect(x+23,y-23,3,1);}
    }}

  // Fine bank rendering: the shoreline comes from the same collision geometry.
  // This overlays only the flowing river strip, not bridges or land paths.
  function riverFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    const endY=Math.min(H*T,oy+h),startY=Math.max(C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T,oy);
    for(let py=Math.floor(startY/2)*2;py<endY;py+=2){const ty=(py+1)/T;if(C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(ty)))continue;const [a,b]=C.shore(ty),l=a*T,r=b*T;if(r+8<ox||l-8>ox+w)continue;
      const width=r-l,mid=(l+r)/2;
      for(let px=Math.max(ox,Math.floor((l-4)/2)*2);px<Math.min(ox+w,r+4);px+=2){const bank=Math.min(px+1-l,r-px-1),u=(px-mid)/width,n=C.hash(px,py,18203),waves=.5+.22*Math.sin(py*.042+u*9)+.22*Math.cos(px*.061-py*.022),shall=smooth(1-Math.max(0,bank)/26);
        if(bank<0){if(bank>-4){const road=trailVisualRoad((px+1)/T,ty);if(!road||road.distance>road.width)box(ctx,bank>-2?'#678877':'#85947a',px,py,2,2);}continue;}
        let deep=[36+waves*12,84+waves*13,98+waves*14],c=blend(deep,[97+waves*14,140+waves*12,133+waves*8],shall);
        // Stratified water depth, with visible sand/pebbles close to the banks.
        if(bank<8&&n>.76)c=blend(c,[157,164,139],.30);if(bank<2)c=blend(c,[172,184,156],.45);
        if(n>.987)c=blend(c,[145,185,183],.52);box(ctx,rgb(c.map(v=>v+(n-.5)*5)),px,py,2,2);
      }
    }
    // Small clusters of water-worn pebbles, reeds and lily pads, grouped
    // by the river's real bends. They stay inside water and never block roads.
    for(let py=Math.max(startY,Math.floor(oy/13)*13);py<endY;py+=13){const ty=py/T;if(C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(ty))||C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(ty+.3)))continue;const [a,b]=C.shore(ty);const rng=random(53891+Math.floor(py/13)*1787);
      for(let side of [0,1]){const n=rng(),x=(side?b:a)*T+(side?-1:1)*(4+rng()*9);if(x<ox-10||x>ox+w+10)continue;
        if(n<.19){for(let k=0;k<2;k++){let q=x+(k-1)*5,v=py+k*3;shape(ctx,'#426970',[[q-4,v],[q-2,v-3],[q+4,v-2],[q+5,v+2],[q-2,v+3]]);line(ctx,'#96ac9c',q-2,v-2,q+3,v-1,1);}}
        else if(n>.92&&ty>91){for(let k=0;k<3;k++){let q=x+(side?-1:1)*(k*5),v=py+k*3;shape(ctx,'#37695f',[[q-4,v],[q-2,v-3],[q+3,v-3],[q+4,v+1],[q+1,v+3],[q-3,v+2]]);line(ctx,'#88aa80',q-2,v-2,q+2,v-2,1);if(k===1)box(ctx,'#d4c3a1',q,v-3,2,2);}}
      }
    }
  }
  function riverCurrent(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.effects;const start=Math.max(C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T,Math.floor(rect.y/19)*19),end=Math.min(H*T,rect.bottom+15),rain=world.weather.kind==='rain',night=world.clock.minuteOfDay<375||world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170;
    if(rect.right<33*T||rect.x>48*T||end<start)return;
    // Stable streamline lanes advect DOWN the river instead of moving sideways.
    const speed=rain?32:21,shift=reduced?0:t*speed;
    for(let py=start;py<end;py+=19){const y=py+shift%19,ty=y/T;if(C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(ty)))continue;const [a,b]=C.shore(ty),w=(b-a)*T;for(let j=0;j<(quality.ambient>6?5:3);j++){const seed=C.hash(Math.floor((py-shift)/19),j,9218),u=.12+.76*((j*.239+seed*.13)%1),px=a*T+u*w;if(px<rect.x||px>rect.right)continue;const len=3+Math.floor(seed*8);g.fillStyle(night?0xb1cad9:0xb4d8ce,.14+seed*.19).fillRect(Math.round(px),Math.round(y),1,len);if(seed>.75)g.fillStyle(0xe0ead5,.20).fillRect(Math.round(px+1),Math.round(y+len),5,1);}}
  }

  // Local water art, drawn at native pixel resolution in shared chunk coordinates.
  function waterwayTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    if(ox+w>=156*T&&ox<=162*T&&oy<=16*T){
      const a=C.CASCADE;
      const outline=(extra)=>a.map(p=>[(p.x-p.r-extra)*T,p.y*T]).concat(a.slice().reverse().map(p=>[(p.x+p.r+extra)*T,p.y*T]));
      shape(ctx,'#40585e',outline(.20));shape(ctx,'#829a9e',outline(.09));shape(ctx,'#396e82',outline(0));
      for(let y=Math.max(10,Math.floor(oy/2)*2);y<Math.min(15.65*T,oy+h);y+=2){const ty=(y+1)/T;for(let x=Math.max(156*T,Math.floor(ox/2)*2);x<Math.min(162*T,ox+w);x+=2){const v=C.cascadeSample((x+1)/T,ty);if(!v)continue;const n=C.hash(x,y,10171),s=v.bank;let color;
          if(v.fall)color=s>.82?'#b2d1ce':n>.84?'#d6ece6':s<.5?'#8ac4d1':'#5899b1';
          else if(s>.83)color=n>.45?'#80b5ba':'#588d97';else if(s>.62)color=n>.4?'#4e8f9e':'#477f90';else color=n>.86?'#5a9eb0':v.pool?'#39768c':'#3f879d';
          box(ctx,color,x,y,2,2);
      }}
      // Jagged wet rock lips, moss and snow shelves. Edge stones retain the same
      // deterministic global seed when neighbouring chunks are loaded separately.
      for(let j=1;j<a.length;j++){const p=a[j-1],q=a[j];const len=Math.ceil((q.y-p.y)*T/10);for(let i=0;i<len;i++){const u=(i+.5)/len,y=(p.y+(q.y-p.y)*u)*T,c=(p.x+(q.x-p.x)*u)*T,r=(p.r+(q.r-p.r)*u)*T;for(const side of [-1,1]){const rng=random(47091+j*101+i*11+side),x=c+side*(r+2+rng()*8);if(rng()<.23)continue;const size=3+rng()*8;shape(ctx,'#536d71',[[x-size,y],[x-2,y-4],[x+size-1,y-3],[x+size,y+3],[x,y+5]]);line(ctx,'#b1c6c4',x-size+1,y-1,x+size-2,y-3,1);if(rng()>.48)box(ctx,y<7*T?'#d8e4da':'#779078',x-1,y-3,3,2);}}}
      for(const b of C.ELEVATION_BANDS){const center=a.find(v=>v.y>b.y);if(!center)continue;const x=center.x*T,y=(b.y+.25)*T;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.56;shadow(ctx,x,y+9,19,6);ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<7;i++)box(ctx,i%2?'#bfdfd9':'#e5f1e9',x-14+i*4,y+3+(i%3)*2,3,2);}
    }
    const m=C.MILL,x=m.x*T,y=m.y*T;if(x+140<ox||x-130>ox+w||y+140<oy||y-110>oy+h)return;
    // Mill approach: worn cobblestone, drainage edge, carefully feathered into soil.
    for(let py=Math.floor(y-44);py<y+95;py+=4)for(let px=Math.floor(x-65);px<x+48;px+=4){const d=Math.hypot((px-x+8)/62,(py-y-16)/74);if(d>1.03||px/T>C.shore(py/T)[0]-.2)continue;const n=C.hash(px,py,10172),r=trailVisualRoad(px/T,py/T);if(r&&r.distance<r.width*.62)continue;ctx.globalAlpha=smooth((1.04-d)*4)*.82;const pal=['#7e8068','#969077','#aa9e80','#b4aa8e','#716f5e'];box(ctx,pal[Math.min(4,Math.floor(n*5))],px,py,4,4);if(n>.71)box(ctx,'#c3b69a',px+1,py,2,1);ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
    // Retaining wall and water-worn apron on the actual left bank.
    for(let yy=y-47;yy<y+47;yy+=5){const xx=C.shore(yy/T)[0]*T;box(ctx,'#405450',xx-7,yy,8,5);box(ctx,'#83928b',xx-7,yy,6,3);box(ctx,'#b2b5a2',xx-6,yy,4,1);}
  }
  // The mill is a full-size unique asset: 3.3 x 2.2 world-tile footprint,
  // offset wheel on the water, shingled roof, stone base, exposed beams,
  // operating shutter and bakery extension. Each stage replaces structure,
  // not a colour tint of the same silhouette.
  function millPaint(ctx,stage,gate,frame){
    const rnd=random(32190),x=90,fy=150;
    shadow(ctx,102,151,72,12);shadow(ctx,153,145,24,8);
    // stepped masonry foot and foundation
    const base=[[36,119],[128,119],[143,133],[141,151],[37,151],[26,138]];
    if(stage>=3){shape(ctx,'#4c514a',base);shape(ctx,'#84877a',[[28,130],[136,130],[143,137],[141,145],[35,145],[28,139]]);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const u=32+i*7+(j%2)*3,v=132+j*5;box(ctx,pick(['#666d65','#8c8e7d','#9c9a86','#777d70'],rnd),u,v,6,4);box(ctx,'#b6b19b',u,v,4,1);}}
    else {for(let i=0;i<(stage<2?35:11);i++){const px=30+rnd()*105,py=123+rnd()*26;shape(ctx,pick(['#5d655c','#899184','#777a69','#a6a08b'],rnd),[[px-3,py],[px,py-4],[px+5,py-2],[px+5,py+2],[px-1,py+4]]);line(ctx,'#b2b59b',px,py-4,px+4,py-2,1);}}
    if(stage<3){ // preserved wall fragments and collapsed beams
      const ps=stage===0?[[37,138],[37,105],[46,97],[55,105],[59,121],[72,117],[73,137]]:[[40,138],[40,120],[50,116],[57,132],[65,131],[65,138]];
      shape(ctx,'#505e53',ps);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const xx=39+i*8,yy=108+j*6;if(xx<54||yy>126)box(ctx,pick(['#8d9380','#6b7568','#a4a68b'],rnd),xx,yy,7,5);}
      if(stage<2){line(ctx,'#4e4635',52,115,114,139,6);line(ctx,'#b09b6c',53,112,113,136,2);line(ctx,'#65533d',93,104,129,139,5);for(let i=0;i<13;i++)box(ctx,'#6b895b',32+i*7,146-(i%3)*2,4,2);}
    }
    if(stage>=4){
      // Long diagonal roof elevation may be seen from behind. Timber posts and brace joints.
      if(stage>=5){shape(ctx,'#b7a284',[[36,71],[112,71],[137,83],[137,133],[36,133]]);shape(ctx,'#8e795e',[[112,71],[137,83],[137,133],[112,133]]);
        for(let j=0;j<7;j++)for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const xx=38+i*8,yy=77+j*7;box(ctx,pick(['#ae9c7b','#c5ad87','#bba883','#a99678'],rnd),xx,yy,7,6);if(rnd()>.5)box(ctx,'#d0b894',xx+1,yy,4,1);}
        for(let j=0;j<7;j++)for(let i=0;i<3;i++){box(ctx,pick(['#746a55','#97805e','#a38c6b'],rnd),114+i*7,85+j*7,6,5);}
      }
      for(const xx of [36,72,110,136]){box(ctx,'#514333',xx,66+(xx===136?13:0),5,68-(xx===136?13:0));box(ctx,'#ac895a',xx+1,67+(xx===136?13:0),2,64-(xx===136?13:0));}
      for(const yy of [79,107,131]){box(ctx,'#594634',34,yy,82,5);box(ctx,'#b08e60',35,yy,80,1);}
      line(ctx,'#6f533a',40,106,69,81,4);line(ctx,'#c09c66',41,104,69,80,1);line(ctx,'#6f533a',76,80,108,106,4);line(ctx,'#b39160',77,79,108,104,1);
      if(stage>=5){ // door, casing and front windows, each with inset and brass hardware
        box(ctx,'#4e4437',70,108,23,28);box(ctx,'#796448',73,111,17,25);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(ctx,'#4e4232',75+i*4,113,75+i*4,135,1);box(ctx,'#c5ab72',85,124,2,2);box(ctx,'#c5b297',68,106,26,3);
        for(const xx of [45,99]){box(ctx,'#493f33',xx,88,14,14);box(ctx,stage>=8?'#d6af68':'#596c6b',xx+2,90,10,10);box(ctx,'#695038',xx+6,90,2,10);box(ctx,'#695038',xx+2,94,10,2);box(ctx,'#d7bd84',xx-1,101,16,3);}
      }
      const roof=[[25,76],[48,43],[111,39],[148,70],[139,83],[31,83]];
      if(stage>=6){shape(ctx,'#4a4137',roof);shape(ctx,'#7f634b',[[28,73],[50,45],[110,41],[123,64],[111,78],[31,78]]);shape(ctx,'#5d5144',[[110,41],[146,70],[137,79],[111,78],[123,64]]);
        ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,[[28,73],[50,45],[110,41],[123,64],[111,78],[31,78]]);ctx.clip();for(let j=0;j<7;j++)for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const xx=25+i*8+(j%2)*4,yy=42+j*6;box(ctx,pick(['#9a7856','#87684d','#a27e57','#745b45','#af8a62'],rnd),xx,yy,7,5);box(ctx,'#ba966d',xx+1,yy,5,1);box(ctx,'#514537',xx,yy+5,7,1);}ctx.restore();
        ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,[[110,41],[146,70],[137,79],[111,78],[123,64]]);ctx.clip();for(let yy=43;yy<80;yy+=6){line(ctx,'#a0825d',112,yy,148,yy+15,1);line(ctx,'#493f36',112,yy+3,148,yy+18,1);}ctx.restore();
        line(ctx,'#d1b584',49,44,110,40,2);line(ctx,'#3d3a32',30,80,138,80,3);line(ctx,'#a88a62',32,78,136,78,2);
        // chimney with distinct brick courses and cap
        box(ctx,'#74665a',90,28,12,24);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){box(ctx,'#927b62',91,29+j*4,10,3);box(ctx,'#c1aa89',91+(j%2)*5,29+j*4,4,1);}box(ctx,'#504b40',88,26,16,4);box(ctx,'#a5a18b',89,26,14,1);
      }else {line(ctx,'#6a503b',27,76,49,43,5);line(ctx,'#6a503b',49,43,111,39,5);line(ctx,'#6a503b',111,39,148,70,5);line(ctx,'#ac8a5d',29,74,49,43,2);line(ctx,'#ae8c60',51,43,109,40,2);for(let k=0;k<4;k++)line(ctx,'#8a6d4c',49+k*18,44-k,38+k*24,80,3);}
    }
    // Water wheel: actual spokes and moving buckets, with a fixed hub and timber frame.
    const cx=160,cy=112,r=28,rot=gate?frame/16*Math.PI*2:0;
    if(stage>=7){box(ctx,'#4a4639',151,132,6,19);box(ctx,'#9b8256',152,132,2,17);box(ctx,'#4a4639',177,126,6,17);line(ctx,'#6c583f',153,139,180,130,4);}
    else {line(ctx,'#5a5749',136,133,181,143,5);line(ctx,'#998d71',137,131,180,141,2);}
    const count=stage>=7?12:5,pts=Array.from({length:12},(_,i)=>[cx+Math.cos(rot+i*Math.PI/6)*r,cy+Math.sin(rot+i*Math.PI/6)*r]);
    if(stage>=7){shape(ctx,'#493d30',pts);shape(ctx,'#44616a',pts.map(([u,v])=>[cx+(u-cx)*.77,cy+(v-cy)*.77]));}
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const a=rot+i*Math.PI/6,ux=Math.cos(a),uy=Math.sin(a),vx=-uy,vy=ux;const pt=[cx+ux*r,cy+uy*r];line(ctx,'#4d3c2a',cx,cy,pt[0],pt[1],5);line(ctx,'#bb935e',cx-1,cy-1,pt[0]-1,pt[1]-1,2);const p=[[-3,-6],[4,-6],[4,6],[-3,6]].map(([u,v])=>[pt[0]+ux*u+vx*v,pt[1]+uy*u+vy*v]);shape(ctx,'#5c4631',p);line(ctx,'#d0af74',p[0][0],p[0][1],p[3][0],p[3][1],2);}
    if(stage>=7){for(let i=0;i<12;i++)line(ctx,'#b68f57',pts[i][0],pts[i][1],pts[(i+1)%12][0],pts[(i+1)%12][1],2);}
    box(ctx,'#564e3e',cx-5,cy-5,10,10);box(ctx,'#b9a779',cx-3,cy-3,6,6);box(ctx,'#55594f',cx-1,cy-1,3,3);
    // Stage-specific scaffolding, gate, mill yard, grain bins and a bakery annex.
    if(stage>=3&&stage<7){for(const xx of [21,143]){box(ctx,'#6a573d',xx,80,3,68);box(ctx,'#c5a977',xx+1,80,1,65);}for(const yy of [95,119]){box(ctx,'#ae8b59',21,yy,18,3);box(ctx,'#ae8b59',129,yy,18,3);}line(ctx,'#94744d',20,145,36,96,2);for(let i=0;i<7;i++)box(ctx,'#c4a777',23+i*2,139-i*6,8,2);}
    if(stage>=8){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const xx=37+j*9,yy=139+j%2*3;shape(ctx,'#76654c',[[xx,yy],[xx+1,yy-9],[xx+7,yy-11],[xx+12,yy-7],[xx+12,yy],[xx+7,yy+3]]);box(ctx,'#c0a67d',xx+3,yy-8,6,2);}box(ctx,'#523f30',93,138,16,4);box(ctx,'#d0ad76',94,136,14,2);box(ctx,'#4e4939',143,130,7,15);box(ctx,gate?'#91a99a':'#786b50',145,gate?130:135,3,7);}
    if(stage>=9){ctx.save();ctx.translate(19,0);ctx.scale(.74,1);shape(ctx,'#665c4d',[[16,106],[42,106],[47,117],[47,140],[14,140]]);shape(ctx,'#a08c70',[[18,111],[39,111],[39,135],[18,135]]);for(let y=114;y<138;y+=4)line(ctx,'#685c4d',18,y,38,y,1);shape(ctx,'#594638',[[9,111],[28,94],[48,108],[44,114],[12,114]]);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)line(ctx,'#b68c61',14+j*3,109-j*3,44-j*2,109-j*3,1);box(ctx,'#414640',25,119,10,16);box(ctx,'#cb9654',27,121,6,6);ctx.restore();}
  }
  function removeMill(view){const s=view.waterMill;if(!s)return;const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(s);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(s.scene)s.destroy();delete view.waterMill;}
  function waterwayEffects(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){
    const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.effects,light=view.light,sh=view.shadows;const visible=(x,y,r=100)=>x>=rect.x-r&&x<=rect.right+r&&y>=rect.y-r&&y<=rect.bottom+r;
    const d=root.LQValleyDevelopment,s=d.millState(world),m=C.MILL,x=m.x*T,y=m.y*T+29;
    if(visible(x,y,240)){const moving=s.stage>=8&&s.gate&&!reduced,frame=moving?Math.floor(t*(world.weather.kind==='rain'?10:7))%16:0;
      const k=texture(scene,'watermill-'+s.stage+'-'+(s.gate?1:0)+'-'+frame,220,170,c=>millPaint(c,s.stage,s.gate,frame));
      let sp=view.waterMill;if(!sp){sp=view.waterMill=scene.add.image(x,y,k).setOrigin(90/220,150/170);scene.areaObjs.push(sp);}if(sp.texture.key!==k)sp.setTexture(k);sp.setDepth(view.depthAt(y));const p=scene.player;sp.setAlpha(p.y<y&&p.y>y-120&&Math.abs(p.x-x)<57?.58:1);
      const wx=x+70,wy=y-38;if(moving){for(let i=0;i<(quality.ambient>6?11:6);i++){const u=(t*.9+i/11)%1;g.fillStyle(i%2?0xe4f0dd:0x85c4cc,(1-u)*.7).fillRect(Math.round(wx+13+u*22+Math.sin(i*2.8)*10),Math.round(wy+28+u*17),2+i%3,1+i%2);}if(s.stage>=9){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const u=(t*.22+i*.25)%1;g.fillStyle(0xdadcce,(1-u)*.18).fillRect(x+5+u*17,y-123-u*32,4+u*8,3+u*6);}}}
      if((world.clock.minuteOfDay<380||world.clock.minuteOfDay>1130)&&s.stage>=8){for(let i=0;i<3;i++)light.fillStyle(0xffc77c,.055).fillEllipse(x-31,y-54,45-i*10,25-i*5);}
    }else removeMill(view);
    if(rect.right<155*T||rect.x>163*T||rect.y>16*T||rect.bottom<0)return;
    const start=Math.max(10,Math.floor(rect.y/6)*6),end=Math.min(15.65*T,rect.bottom+8);let n=0;
    for(let yy=start;yy<end;yy+=7){const phase=reduced?0:t*23;const lane=(yy+Math.floor(phase%7))/T;const p=C.CASCADE.find((a,i)=>i&&lane>=C.CASCADE[i-1].y&&lane<=a.y);if(!p)continue;const a=C.CASCADE[C.CASCADE.indexOf(p)-1],u=(lane-a.y)/(p.y-a.y),mid=a.x+(p.x-a.x)*u;const v=C.cascadeSample(mid,lane);if(!v)continue;const len=v.fall?16:7,lanes=quality.ambient>6?4:2;for(let j=0;j<lanes;j++){const offset=(j/(lanes-1)-.5)*v.width*1.3*T+Math.sin(yy*.13+j)*2;const px=mid*T+offset,py=lane*T;const f=(yy*.023+j*.17+t*(v.fall?1.1:.35))%1;g.fillStyle(j%2?0xcce9e6:0x9dcfdc,.25+.4*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(px),Math.round(py),v.fall?2:1,Math.round(len*(.5+f*.5)));n++;}}
    for(const b of C.ELEVATION_BANDS){const py=(b.y+.75)*T,m=C.CASCADE.find((v,i)=>i&&v.y>=py/T),pre=m&&C.CASCADE[C.CASCADE.indexOf(m)-1],mid=pre?pre.x+(m.x-pre.x)*(py/T-pre.y)/(m.y-pre.y):158,p=C.cascadeSample(mid,py/T);if(!p||!visible(p.center*T,py,30))continue;for(let j=0;j<(reduced?3:9);j++){const u=reduced?.4:(t*.65+j/9)%1,px=p.center*T+Math.sin(j*3.7)*u*24;g.fillStyle(j%2?0xd8ede6:0x9dcbd3,(1-u)*.65).fillRect(Math.round(px),Math.round(py+u*12),3+j%4,2);if(!reduced) view.nearFog.fillStyle(0xc6e4e4,(1-u)*.085).fillEllipse(px+u*9,py-u*13,12+u*15,6+u*7);}}
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const u=reduced?.5:(t*.34+i*.25)%1;g.lineStyle(1,0xb2d8da,(1-u)*.42).strokeEllipse(159.65*T,14.65*T,8+u*55,3+u*20);}
  }

  // Native-pixel hand-authored bridge components, rendered once per stage.
  // Independent support / deck / foreground rail layers distinguish over and under.
  // Six authored palettes and distinct structural silhouettes; all three image
  // passes use the exact same elevation function as the player's foot-plane.
  const spanPal={
    timber:['#3a352c','#594338','#856344','#baa06a','#e2c997','#7d8e55','#c88c51'],
    cord:['#383747','#5d5068','#827791','#b6a2bc','#eed9cb','#9f85b2','#e7c9ac'],
    sandstone:['#50382f','#83543e','#ad7651','#d5a179','#f0d0a0','#8e9560','#e5b77b'],
    water:['#2b4641','#47665d','#718b77','#a3b39a','#d7daba','#789d85','#bad6cd'],
    limestone:['#494c39','#797b59','#a3a178','#c8c399','#ede3bd','#9f9d5a','#e6c881'],
    silver:['#35434e','#566975','#82969a','#aec3bf','#d7dad6','#82a896','#c8d3ee']
  };
  function spanStone(ctx,x,y,w,h,p,rng){box(ctx,p[0],x,y,w,h);for(let yy=y+2;yy<y+h-1;){const rh=3+Math.floor(rng()*5);for(let xx=x+1-Math.floor(rng()*4);xx<x+w-1;){const rw=5+Math.floor(rng()*9),a=Math.max(x+1,xx),b=Math.min(x+w-1,xx+rw);if(b>a){box(ctx,p[2+(rng()<.28?1:0)],a,yy,b-a,Math.min(rh-1,y+h-yy));box(ctx,p[3],a+1,yy+1,Math.max(1,b-a-2),1);if(rng()<.33)box(ctx,p[1],b-2,yy+2,1,2);}xx+=rw;}yy+=rh;}box(ctx,p[3],x,y,2,h-2);box(ctx,p[1],x+w-3,y+1,2,h-1);}
  function spanDimensions(b){return {x:b.left*T-24,y:b.y*T-214,w:(b.right-b.left)*T+48,h:270,L:(b.right-b.left)*T,x0:24,cy:214};}
  function crossingPaint(ctx,b,stage,layer){const {L,x0,cy}=spanDimensions(b),p=spanPal[b.style],rng=random(15791+geologySeed(b.id)),stone=['sandstone','limestone','silver'].includes(b.style),wood=!stone,fx=u=>x0+u,fy=u=>Math.round(cy-C.crossingZ(b,b.left+u/T)),half=22;
    // Weather-worn banks and paired abutments, not a built road.
    if(layer==='base'){
      for(const u of [0,L]){const x=fx(u);shadow(ctx,x,cy+8,24,10);for(let j=0;j<17;j++){let xx=x-23+rng()*46,yy=cy-13+rng()*29;if(Math.abs(yy-cy)<7&&stage>=2)continue;chip(ctx,xx,yy,4+rng()*7,3+rng()*4,{faces:[p[0]],talus:p[2],ledges:[p[3]]});}spanStone(ctx,x-15,cy-12,30,24,p,rng);box(ctx,p[4],x-15,cy-12,30,2);if(stage===0){box(ctx,p[0],x-17,cy-10,4,11);box(ctx,p[3],x+9,cy-16,7,9);}}
      if(stage<1)return;
      for(const tile of b.piers){const u=(tile-b.left)*T,x=fx(u),top=fy(u)+15,bottom=cy+12;shadow(ctx,x+6,bottom,20,8);if(stone){spanStone(ctx,x-11,top,22,bottom-top,p,rng);box(ctx,p[1],x-15,bottom-7,30,7);box(ctx,p[3],x-15,bottom-8,30,2);box(ctx,p[0],x-14,top,28,6);box(ctx,p[3],x-14,top,28,2);for(let j=0;j<9;j++){const yy=bottom-1-rng()*17,xx=x-11+rng()*11;box(ctx,p[5],xx,yy,2+rng()*4,2);}}
        else{for(const k of [-7,7]){box(ctx,p[0],x+k-3,top,7,bottom-top);box(ctx,p[2],x+k-2,top+2,2,bottom-top-4);for(let j=top+7;j<bottom;j+=12)box(ctx,p[1],x+k-1,j,1,7);}line(ctx,p[0],x-7,bottom-8,x+7,top+12,4);line(ctx,p[2],x-7,bottom-8,x+7,top+12,1);box(ctx,p[0],x-13,top,26,7);box(ctx,p[3],x-12,top+1,24,2);}}
      // A continuous visible arcade face and genuine open arches below it.
      if(stone&&stage>=2){const a=(b.piers[0]-b.left)*T,d=(b.piers[1]-b.left)*T,mid=(a+d)/2,r=(d-a)/2;ctx.save();const cap=[];for(let u=a;u<=d;u+=2)cap.push([fx(u),fy(u)+10]);for(let u=d;u>=a;u-=2){const q=(u-mid)/r;cap.push([fx(u),cy+12-Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-q*q))*(b.rise-24)]);}shape(ctx,p[1],cap);ctx.clip();for(let yy=cy-b.rise;yy<cy+13;yy+=7)for(let xx=fx(a)-10;xx<fx(d)+10;xx+=12){box(ctx,p[rng()<.3?3:2],xx+(yy%3),yy,10,6);box(ctx,p[3],xx+(yy%3)+1,yy,8,1);}ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<=24;i++){const a0=Math.PI-i*Math.PI/24,u=mid+r*Math.cos(a0),v=cy+12-(b.rise-24)*Math.sin(a0);line(ctx,p[3],fx(u),v,fx(u)+Math.cos(a0)*7,v-Math.sin(a0)*7,2);}}
      if(stage<2){for(const u of [6,L-30]){const x=fx(u),yy=cy-12;line(ctx,p[1],x,yy,x+21,yy-7,5);line(ctx,p[3],x,yy-2,x+21,yy-9,1);}}
      return;
    }
    if(stage<2)return;
    // Layer one: deck and rear rails. Pixel slices are vertically projected
    // from the smooth ramp, not a painted flat strip on the ground.
    if(layer==='deck'){
      for(let u=0;u<L;u+=2){const x=fx(u),y=fy(u),pr=Math.floor(u/(stone?13:7)),rn=C.hash(pr,Math.floor(u/4),geologySeed(b.id));box(ctx,p[0],x,y-half-2,2,2*half+13);box(ctx,p[1],x,y+half-1,2,11);box(ctx,p[3],x,y-half,2,2);box(ctx,p[rn>.75?3:2],x,y-half+3,2,2*half-5);box(ctx,p[1],x,y+half-3,2,3);box(ctx,p[3],x,y+half,2,2);if(stone){for(let j=-half+5;j<half-5;j+=9){const off=(Math.floor(j/9)&1)*6;if((u+off)%14<2)box(ctx,p[1],x,y+j,2,8);box(ctx,p[1],x,y+j+8,2,1);if(rn>.87)box(ctx,p[4],x,y+j+1,2,1);}}else{if(u%8===0){box(ctx,p[1],x,y-half+3,1,2*half-6);box(ctx,p[3],x+1,y-half+3,1,2*half-6);}if(rn>.7){box(ctx,p[1],x,y-9+Math.floor(rn*15),2,1);box(ctx,p[4],x,y-10+Math.floor(rn*15),2,1);}}}
      // Incomplete deck remains blocked; exposed ends have unmistakable tape.
      if(stage===2){for(const u of [4,L-4]){const x=fx(u),y=fy(u);box(ctx,p[0],x-3,y-27,6,49);line(ctx,'#d2a15d',x-8,y-22,x+8,y+11,3);}return;}
      for(let u=3;u<L;u+=24){const x=fx(u),y=fy(u)-half;box(ctx,p[0],x-2,y-25,6,28);box(ctx,p[3],x-1,y-24,2,26);box(ctx,p[4],x-3,y-27,8,2);if(stone){box(ctx,p[1],x-4,y-10,10,4);box(ctx,p[3],x-4,y-11,10,1);}if(u>10){const v=u-24,xx=fx(v),yy=fy(v)-half;line(ctx,p[0],xx,yy-16,x,y-16,4);line(ctx,p[3],xx,yy-17,x,y-17,1);line(ctx,p[2],xx,yy-7,x,y-7,2);}}
      if(b.style==='cord'){for(let u=0;u<L-3;u+=2){const y=fy(u)-half-27+Math.sin(u/L*Math.PI)*19;box(ctx,p[0],fx(u),y,2,2);if(u%12===0)line(ctx,p[1],fx(u),y,fx(u),fy(u)-half+1,1);}}
      spanOrnament(ctx,b,L,x0,cy,p);return;
    }
    if(stage!==3)return;
    // Near rails always remain in front of the upper player; the back rail is
    // already behind the sprite. Rail lattice differs by region.
    for(let u=3;u<L;u+=24){const x=fx(u),y=fy(u)+half;box(ctx,p[0],x-3,y-27,7,30);box(ctx,p[2],x-2,y-25,4,26);box(ctx,p[3],x-2,y-25,1,24);box(ctx,p[4],x-4,y-28,9,2);box(ctx,p[1],x-4,y-3,9,3);if(u>10){const v=u-24,xx=fx(v),yy=fy(v)+half;line(ctx,p[0],xx,yy-20,x,y-20,5);line(ctx,p[3],xx,yy-21,x,y-21,1);line(ctx,p[1],xx,yy-5,x,y-5,3);if(stone){for(let k=1;k<=3;k++){const f=k/4,rx=xx+(x-xx)*f,ry=yy+(y-yy)*f;box(ctx,p[2],rx-1,ry-17,3,12);box(ctx,p[3],rx-1,ry-16,1,10);}}else if(b.style==='cord'){line(ctx,p[3],xx+3,yy-16,x-3,y-5,1);line(ctx,p[3],xx+3,yy-5,x-3,y-16,1);}else{line(ctx,p[1],xx+3,yy-7,x-3,y-16,3);line(ctx,p[3],xx+3,yy-8,x-3,y-17,1);}}}
    // Sculpted end standards and distinct small carved caps.
    for(const u of [8,L-8]){const x=fx(u),y=fy(u)-half;box(ctx,p[0],x-4,y-52,8,52);box(ctx,p[2],x-3,y-50,5,49);box(ctx,p[4],x-2,y-48,1,43);shape(ctx,p[1],[[x-8,y-54],[x,y-65],[x+8,y-54],[x+5,y-50],[x-5,y-50]]);line(ctx,p[4],x-7,y-54,x,y-64,1);box(ctx,p[0],x-6,y-47,12,15);box(ctx,p[6],x-4,y-45,8,10);box(ctx,p[4],x-3,y-44,3,7);box(ctx,p[1],x-1,y-46,2,12);box(ctx,p[0],x-8,y-34,16,2);}
  }
  function crossingViews(scene,view,w,t,reduced){const m=view.crossingSprites??=new Map(),r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay<375||w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170;for(const b of C.CROSSINGS){const d=spanDimensions(b),stage=root.LQValleyDevelopment.crossingState(w,b.id).stage;let a=m.get(b.id);const show=!scene.inside&&r.right>d.x-40&&r.x<d.x+d.w+40&&r.bottom>d.y-40&&r.y<d.y+d.h+40;if(!show){if(a)a.forEach(s=>s.setVisible(false));continue;}if(!a){a=['base','deck','rail'].map((k,i)=>{const tex=texture(scene,'cross-'+b.id+'-'+k+'-'+stage,d.w,d.h,c=>crossingPaint(c,b,stage,k));const s=scene.add.image(d.x,d.y,tex).setOrigin(0,0).setDepth([view.depthAt(b.y*T)-.12,17,19][i]);scene.areaObjs.push(s);return s;});m.set(b.id,a);}a.forEach((s,i)=>{const k=['base','deck','rail'][i],tex=texture(scene,'cross-'+b.id+'-'+k+'-'+stage,d.w,d.h,c=>crossingPaint(c,b,stage,k));if(s.texture.key!==tex)s.setTexture(tex);s.setVisible(true).setAlpha(1);});
    const p=scene.player,upper=scene._lqSurface===C.crossingSurface(b),z=C.crossingZ(b,p.x/T);if(!upper&&stage>=2&&p.x/T>b.left&&p.x/T<b.right&&Math.abs(p.y-(b.y*T-z))<39){a[1].setAlpha(.42);a[2].setAlpha(.36);}if(stage>=2&&!night){const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=24;i++){const x=b.left+(b.right-b.left)*i/24,v=C.crossingZ(b,x);pts.push({x:x*T+v*.24,y:b.y*T+v*.19-16});}for(let i=24;i>=0;i--){const x=b.left+(b.right-b.left)*i/24,v=C.crossingZ(b,x);pts.push({x:x*T+v*.24,y:b.y*T+v*.19+17});}view.shadows.fillStyle(0x203c35,w.weather.kind==='rain'?.08:.19).fillPoints(pts,true);}
    if(stage===3){const g=view.effects,pal=spanPal[b.style];for(const u of [8,d.L-8]){const x=d.x+d.x0+u,y=b.y*T-C.crossingZ(b,b.left+u/T)-d.cy+d.cy-22-40;if(night){for(let i=4;i>0;i--)view.light.fillStyle(b.style==='silver'?0x9fcbeb:0xffd58c,.028+(4-i)*.013).fillCircle(x,y,i*8);g.fillStyle(b.style==='silver'?0xe2f6ff:0xffedb7,.92).fillRect(x-1,y-2,3,4);}
    if(!reduced){let wave=Math.sin(t*3+u)*3,base=y+17;const c=parseInt(pal[5].slice(1),16);g.fillStyle(c,.95).fillPoints([{x:x+4,y:base-6},{x:x+17,y:base-5+wave},{x:x+13,y:base+5+wave},{x:x+4,y:base+4}],true);g.fillStyle(parseInt(pal[4].slice(1),16),.8).fillRect(x+6,base-2,5,1);}}
    }
  }}
  // Lake margins use distance to the exact collision polygon. No change to the
  // shoreline physics, and no fake traversable islands in the water.
  function crossingPoolDistance(x,y,p){let d=Infinity;for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){const a=p[i],b=p[(i+1)%p.length],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],q=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((x-a[0])*dx+(y-a[1])*dy)/(dx*dx+dy*dy)));d=Math.min(d,Math.hypot(x-a[0]-q*dx,y-a[1]-q*dy));}return d;}
  function refinedPool(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){if(ox+w<220*T)return;for(let pi=0;pi<C.EAST_POOLS.length;pi++){const p=C.EAST_POOLS[pi],b=C.bounds(p);if((b.x+b.w+1)*T<ox||(b.y+b.h+1)*T<oy||b.x*T>ox+w+32||b.y*T>oy+h+32)continue;const x0=Math.max(ox,Math.floor((b.x-.6)*T/2)*2),x1=Math.min(ox+w,(b.x+b.w+.6)*T),y0=Math.max(oy,Math.floor((b.y-.6)*T/2)*2),y1=Math.min(oy+h,(b.y+b.h+.6)*T);for(let y=y0;y<y1;y+=2)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x+=2){const tx=(x+1)/T,ty=(y+1)/T,inside=C.eastPool(tx,ty),dist=crossingPoolDistance(tx,ty,p),noise=wildNoise(x,y,33,811+pi),small=C.hash(x,y,911+pi),jitter=(noise-.5)*.21;
      if(inside){const d=Math.max(0,dist+jitter),deep=smooth(d/2.7),bank=smooth(d/.43),a=blend([113,157,130],[48,103,113],bank),z=blend(a,[28,63,84],deep*.82),c=z.map(v=>v+(noise-.5)*15+(small-.5)*3);box(ctx,rgb(c),x,y,2,2);if(d<.12&&small>.23)box(ctx,'#a6c4aa',x,y,2,1);else if(d<.65&&small>.973)box(ctx,'#669a8c',x,y,3,1);}
      else if(dist<.40+jitter){const soil=wildColor(x+1,y+1),q=smooth((.40+jitter-dist)/.40),c=blend(soil,[112,117,81],q*.75);box(ctx,rgb(c),x,y,2,2);if(small>.91&&q>.5)box(ctx,'#899f79',x,y,2,1);}}
      const rng=random(7790+pi);for(let i=0;i<190;i++){const x=(b.x-.35+rng()*(b.w+.7))*T,y=(b.y-.35+rng()*(b.h+.7))*T;if(x<ox-18||x>ox+w+18||y<oy-18||y>oy+h+18)continue;const d=crossingPoolDistance(x/T,y/T,p),wet=C.eastPool(x/T,y/T);if(wet&&d>.35&&d<1.05&&rng()<.42){const z=3+rng()*5;shape(ctx,'#416b59',[[x-z,y],[x-2,y-z*.66],[x+z-1,y-z*.5],[x+z,y+1],[x+2,y+z*.51]]);line(ctx,'#92ad7a',x-z+2,y-1,x+z-2,y-2,1);if(rng()<.35){box(ctx,'#d6c4ac',x-1,y-3,3,3);box(ctx,'#f5e5c3',x,y-3,1,1);}}
      else if(!wet&&d<.27&&!C.CROSSINGS.some(b=>Math.abs(y/T-b.y)<1&&x/T>b.left-1&&x/T<b.right+1)){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const dx=(rng()-.5)*7,hh=7+rng()*9;line(ctx,'#425f4d',x+dx,y,x+dx+(rng()-.5)*4,y-hh,1);line(ctx,'#8f9d66',x+dx+1,y-3,x+dx+1,y-hh+2,1);if(j%3===0)box(ctx,'#c2aa77',x+dx-1,y-hh,2,4);}}}
    }}
  // Replace flat deposit faces in eastern sites with a layered rock texture.
  function easternDepositDetail(ctx,node,state,rng){if(node.x<168||!['rock','iron','stone'].includes(node.kind)||state==='spent')return;
    const biome=C.eastBiome(node.x,node.y),material=C.NODE_MATERIAL[node.id]||node.kind,clay=material==='clay',iron=material==='iron'||node.kind==='iron',worked=state==='worked',y=worked?20:10;
    const colors=clay?['#614b43','#8f6050','#b97b60','#d69b78','#e1b896']:({amberwood:['#354a40','#59685a','#828677','#a6a38b','#c4bba0'],heath:['#464353','#675e70','#8a8290','#b1a1ac','#d0c2c9'],coppervale:['#543a34','#865145','#b57959','#d09b72','#e1b891'],glasswater:['#364d4b','#526d65','#789189','#9eb2a0','#bdd0b8'],goldgrass:['#5a5141','#81755a','#a69a77','#ccc09a','#ded2aa'],moonbloom:['#3f465a','#626d83','#8694a6','#acb8c8','#d0d9db']})[biome]||['#414940','#687267','#909889','#afb9a5','#d0d5bf'];
    const [dark,shade,mid,lit,edge]=colors;ctx.clearRect(0,0,66,44);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.22;shape(ctx,'#182b24',[[3,34],[14,29],[49,29],[63,34],[57,39],[19,41]]);ctx.restore();
    const top=[[8,29],[13,y+10],[23,y+4],[34,y],[47,y+4],[56,y+13],[59,29],[53,35],[35,38],[16,35]];
    shape(ctx,dark,top);shape(ctx,shade,[[10,29],[15,y+11],[25,y+6],[34,y+2],[48,y+6],[56,y+14],[57,28],[48,34],[33,36],[17,33]]);
    shape(ctx,mid,[[15,y+11],[25,y+6],[34,y+2],[48,y+6],[46,y+14],[34,y+17],[22,y+16]]);shape(ctx,lit,[[25,y+6],[34,y+2],[48,y+6],[39,y+10],[28,y+11]]);shape(ctx,shade,[[46,y+14],[48,y+6],[56,y+14],[57,28],[48,34],[42,26]]);shape(ctx,mid,[[13,y+14],[22,y+16],[34,y+17],[31,29],[18,32],[10,29]]);
    ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,top);ctx.clip();for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const yy=y+8+j*4;line(ctx,j%2?shade:lit,11,yy+3,26,yy,1);line(ctx,j%2?dark:mid,26,yy,51,yy+4,1);}for(let j=0;j<45;j++){const x=12+Math.floor(rng()*43),yy=y+3+Math.floor(rng()*(33-y));box(ctx,colors[1+Math.floor(rng()*4)],x,yy,1+Math.floor(rng()*2),1);}ctx.restore();
    if(iron||material==='copper'){const ore=material==='copper'?'#d49a6b':'#c1cbd0';for(let j=0;j<4;j++){let x=20+j*7,yy=y+13+(j%2)*4;line(ctx,dark,x-2,yy+5,x+4,yy,3);line(ctx,ore,x-2,yy+4,x+3,yy,1);box(ctx,edge,x+1,yy,1,1);}}
    else if(!clay){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const x=15+j*7,yy=29+(j%3);box(ctx,'#667b4f',x,yy,4,2);box(ctx,'#8b9b66',x,yy,2,1);}}line(ctx,edge,25,y+6,34,y+2,1);
  }
  // Additional authored silhouettes at the far rail. They are deliberately
  // outside the walking strip, so decorative architecture never blocks a ramp.
  function spanOrnament(ctx,b,L,x0,cy,p){const fy=u=>Math.round(cy-C.crossingZ(b,b.left+u/T))-22,mid=L/2,rng=random(geologySeed(b.id)+133),col=p[0],top=p[3],light=p[4];
    function column(u,h,w=8){const x=x0+u,y=fy(u);box(ctx,col,x-w/2,y-h,w,h);box(ctx,p[2],x-w/2+2,y-h+2,w-3,h-3);box(ctx,top,x-w/2+2,y-h+2,1,h-4);box(ctx,col,x-w/2-2,y-h,w+4,4);box(ctx,top,x-w/2-2,y-h,w+4,1);box(ctx,p[1],x-w/2-3,y-5,w+6,5);return [x,y-h];}
    if(b.style==='timber'){const a=column(mid-43,57),d=column(mid+43,57);for(let u=-54;u<54;u+=2){let x=x0+mid+u,y=a[1]-6-Math.round(15*(1-Math.abs(u)/56));box(ctx,col,x,y,2,10);box(ctx,top,x,y,2,2);box(ctx,p[(Math.floor((u+54)/7)&1)?1:2],x,y+3,2,5);}box(ctx,p[1],a[0]-6,a[1]-1,d[0]-a[0]+12,7);line(ctx,light,a[0]-6,a[1]-2,d[0]+6,d[1]-2,1);for(let i=0;i<60;i++){let x=x0+mid-54+rng()*108,y=a[1]-8+10*rng(),l=3+rng()*7;box(ctx,'#496046',x,y,l,3);box(ctx,'#8a9c60',x+1,y-1,l-1,2);if(rng()<.20)box(ctx,'#c8a65b',x+3,y-1,2,2);}}
    else if(b.style==='cord'){const a=column(mid-53,65,9),d=column(mid+53,65,9);for(let u=-53;u<53;u+=2){const y=a[1]+Math.round(26*(1-(u/53)**2));box(ctx,col,x0+mid+u,y,2,3);box(ctx,top,x0+mid+u,y,2,1);if((u+53)%12===0){line(ctx,p[1],x0+mid+u,y,x0+mid+u,fy(mid+u)+2,1);if((u+53)%24===0)shape(ctx,p[5],[[x0+mid+u,y+2],[x0+mid+u+7,y+3],[x0+mid+u+5,y+12],[x0+mid+u,y+8]]);}}for(const x of [a[0],d[0]]){shape(ctx,p[2],[[x-7,a[1]-1],[x,a[1]-13],[x+7,a[1]-1]]);box(ctx,light,x-1,a[1]-11,2,3);}}
    else if(b.style==='sandstone'||b.style==='silver'){const h=b.style==='silver'?66:56,r=b.style==='silver'?38:42,a=column(mid-r,h-12,13),d=column(mid+r,h-12,13),y0=fy(mid)-h+15;for(let u=-r;u<=r;u+=2){const yy=y0-Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-(u/r)**2))*27;box(ctx,col,x0+mid+u,yy,2,15);box(ctx,p[2],x0+mid+u,yy+3,2,10);box(ctx,top,x0+mid+u,yy+1,2,2);if((u+r)%8===0)box(ctx,p[1],x0+mid+u,yy+3,2,10);}const x=x0+mid,y=y0-27;shape(ctx,p[1],[[x-9,y+13],[x-7,y-3],[x,y-9],[x+7,y-3],[x+9,y+13]]);box(ctx,light,x-2,y-3,4,8);if(b.style==='silver'){shape(ctx,'#d4d4ac',[[x-4,y-15],[x,y-20],[x+3,y-16],[x+1,y-14],[x-1,y-17],[x-4,y-15]]);for(let u of [-r/2,r/2]){const xx=x+u,yy=y0-8;line(ctx,p[0],xx,yy-7,xx,yy+6,1);box(ctx,p[0],xx-4,yy+6,8,10);box(ctx,'#b5dbe1',xx-3,yy+7,6,7);box(ctx,'#e3f3e7',xx-2,yy+8,2,4);}}else for(let u of [-r,r]){const x=x0+mid+u;box(ctx,top,x-9,fy(mid+u)-50,18,4);box(ctx,p[1],x-10,fy(mid+u)-54,20,3);}}
    else if(b.style==='water'){for(const u of [mid-56,mid+56]){const a=column(u-11,36,5),d=column(u+11,36,5),x=x0+u,y=a[1];shape(ctx,p[0],[[x-20,y],[x,y-18],[x+20,y],[x+17,y+5],[x-17,y+5]]);shape(ctx,p[5],[[x-18,y-1],[x,y-16],[x+18,y-1],[x,y+2]]);line(ctx,light,x-15,y-2,x,y-14,1);box(ctx,p[1],x-3,y+6,6,9);box(ctx,p[6],x-2,y+7,4,6);}}
    else{for(const u of [mid-31,mid+31]){const a=column(u,40,8),x=a[0],y=a[1];shape(ctx,p[1],[[x-10,y+4],[x-8,y-6],[x,y-13],[x+8,y-6],[x+10,y+4]]);box(ctx,top,x-5,y-5,10,9);for(let k=-3;k<=3;k+=3){line(ctx,'#8a7549',x+k,y+3,x+k,y-4,1);box(ctx,'#dfc887',x+k-1,y-5,2,3);}}}
  }
  // Small hand-composed clusters rather than repeated geometric stamps. Each
  // native 1px motif has an outline, shadow and broken highlights. Positions
  // are still deterministic and do not alter resources or collision.
  const meadowMotifs={
    amberwood:[['....xx.....','...xAAx....','..xABBAx...','.xABBCAAx..','..xAABBx...','....xAx....','..a.xa.....','..aa.a.....'],['..x...x....','.xAx.xAx...','xABAxxBAx..','.xAAxxAx...','..xAxxA....','....aa.....','...a.aa....']],
    heath:[['....ccc....','...cBCc....','..cCBBCc...','...cAc.....','.ccBABcc...','cBCABCBCc..','.ccaaacc...','..aa.aa....'],['...c...c...','..cBC.cBc..','...c..cc...','...a..a....','.aaAaaAa...','aAAaAAa....','..a..a.....']],
    coppervale:[['.....x.....','...xxAx....','..xABBAx...','.xABBBCx...','xABCCBAAx..','xAABBAAAx..','.xxxxxxxx..','...aa......'],['...xx......','..xBBx.....','.xBCAAx....','xABBAAx....','xxxxxxxx...','.......a...']],
    glasswater:[['...a..a....','...B..B....','..aB..a.c..','..aB.aB.c..','.aABaBB.c..','aABAAABB..','..aaaAAa...','....a......'],['....c......','...cBCc....','..caBCac...','.aAcaaAaa..','aABBAABBAa.','.aaABBAaa..','...aaaa....']],
    goldgrass:[['...B..B....','..CB.CB....','...A..A..B.','..aA..AaCB.','.aAB.aBA.A.','aABaABAAaA.','...aaaaa...','....a......'],['...C....C..','..CBC..CBC.','...A....A..','.aAa.aAaaAa','aABAAAABAa.','..aaaaaaa..']],
    moonbloom:[['...cCc.....','..cCBCc....','...cBc..c..','....a..cCc.','..aAa..cBc.','.aABAa..a..','aAABaAaAa..','..aaaAa....'],['...a.......','..aAa..a...','.aABa.aBa..','aABBAaABBa.','..aAABAaa..','...aaa.....']]
  };
  const meadowPal={amberwood:['#3e5539','#557248','#819454','#b4bb76','#d4af6e','#f1cc86'],heath:['#483e52','#586553','#7b8e69','#a584ac','#c7a9c9','#ead4e3'],coppervale:['#694935','#775b40','#a5724f','#c99566','#e0bb88','#f2d3a5'],glasswater:['#34594b','#416c54','#68916b','#8fb27d','#b7bf86','#dfd7b8'],goldgrass:['#55553a','#707847','#a8a358','#c7b76c','#e6c57f','#f4d99b'],moonbloom:['#2e4d48','#456b60','#739989','#b4bdcc','#d6ccdc','#f0e2ed']};
  function authoredPlant(ctx,x,y,region,grove,rng){if(!meadowMotifs[region])return false;const p=meadowPal[region],m=meadowMotifs[region],n=grove>.57?2:grove<.25?1:Math.floor(rng()*2)+1;for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const rows=m[Math.floor(rng()*m.length)],scale=rng()>.88?2:1,xx=Math.round(x+(rng()-.5)*15-5*scale),yy=Math.round(y+(rng()-.5)*8-7*scale);const colors={x:p[0],a:p[1],A:p[2],B:p[3],c:p[4],C:p[5]};shadow(ctx,xx+5*scale,yy+rows.length*scale-1,7*scale,2.7*scale);for(let j=0;j<rows.length;j++)for(let i=0;i<rows[j].length;i++){const v=rows[j][i];if(colors[v])box(ctx,colors[v],xx+(k%2?rows[j].length-i-1:i)*scale,yy+j*scale,scale,scale);}}
    return true;
  }

  function skywayPaint(ctx,stage,layer){const b=C.SKYWAY,L=(b.right-b.left)*T,margin=20,cy=105;const x0=margin;
    const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));const ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w);const poly=(c,p)=>shape(ctx,c,p.map(([x,y])=>[Math.round(x),Math.round(y)]));
    const y=x=>cy-C.skywayHeight(b.left+(x-x0)/T);const rng=random(8506+stage*11);
    if(layer==='ground'){
      // Deep shadows, stepped stone bases, offset blocks and corroded anchor bolts.
      ctx.fillStyle='#25362a40';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x0+L/2+9,cy+12,L*.49,17,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      for(const px of b.pierXs){const x=x0+(px-b.left)*T,top=stage>=4?y(x)+18:cy-9;poly('#414b43',[[x-13,cy+11],[x-17,cy+3],[x-12,top],[x+11,top-3],[x+15,cy+2],[x+11,cy+11]]);poly('#8b9380',[[x-13,cy+5],[x-10,top+2],[x+2,top+2],[x+4,cy+5]]);poly('#616c62',[[x+2,top+2],[x+11,top-1],[x+15,cy+2],[x+4,cy+5]]);
        for(let yy=top+5;yy<cy+5;yy+=7){ln('#3c4a43',x-10,yy,x+12,yy+1);r('#a6ac91',x-9,yy+1,7,1);r('#727c6a',x+2,yy+2,4,2);ln('#465249',x+(Math.floor(yy)%2?3:-4),yy-5,x+(Math.floor(yy)%2?3:-4),yy);}
        r('#4d5b4e',x-16,cy+5,32,5);r('#a4a18a',x-13,cy+4,26,2);for(let k=0;k<8;k++)r(k%3?'#6b895c':'#a2b47d',x-14+rng()*27,cy+7+rng()*3,2,2);
